# SkMate web TV: Android design spec (1:1)

Implementation spec for the Preact web app (Samsung Tizen, LG webOS) in `web/`. The Android TV app is the source of truth. This file describes what Android draws and does, so each screen can be rebuilt to match it.

- **Android source:** `skmate-android-main`, detached at origin/main commit `3ad3636` ("боковые панели ровнее"). Every `path:line` points into that tree. Line numbers will drift as `main` moves, so check fresh `main` (`git show origin/main:<path>`) before you rely on a line number.
- **Path prefixes.** Each part spells out its own short forms at its top. The common ones are `A/` = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/` and `C/` = `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/`. Some parts write paths relative to `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/` with no prefix; they say so.
- **Screenshots:** the reference pack `skmate-ekrany/` (01-05 main screens, 10-11 settings list, 20-36 settings sections, 90-93 wizard), 1920x1080 from an Android TV emulator.
- **Strings:** section 3 was generated by a script straight from `engine/core/src/main/res/values{,-ru}/strings*.xml`, plus `app/src/main/res` where marked "(app res)". Nothing in it was typed by hand. Use the Russian and English text verbatim. Android placeholders `%1$s` and `%1$d` are kept as they are.
- **How this was made:** by reading the code and measuring the screenshots. **Nothing was run on a TV or on the Android app.** Behaviour derived from code only is marked as such where the authors were unsure.

## 0. Global decisions (read before any screen)

### 0.1 Scale: 1 mpx = 1 px, or 1 mpx = 1.111 px? (decision needed)

- `mpx` is defined as `1/1.8 dp` (`A/ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:23-29`). The comment says the design is matched 1:1 "at the default UI Zoom of 90%" (`StageTokens.kt:15-22`).
- But the real default is **UI Zoom 100%** (`C/theme/UiZoom.kt:11`, `DEFAULT = 100`), applied as density × zoom/100 (`A/MainActivity.kt:489-493`). On a 1080p TV (density 2.0) at 100%, **1 mpx = 2.0/1.8 = 1.111 px**.
- **The screenshots confirm this.** Five independent measurements agree:
  - search field: 48 mpx → about 53 px;
  - switch: 56×32 → about 62×35 px;
  - rail: 84 → 93 px;
  - guide row pitch: 62 → 69 px;
  - wizard card: 1040 → 1156 px.
- **Exception:** positions written as screen fractions match 1:1. These are `fx(n) = screenW*n/1920` in `A/features/live/LiveScreen.kt:665`, and `w*N` and percentage widths in settings (`A/features/settings/SettingsStage.kt:292,329`).
- **Recommendation:** render fixed sizes with one CSS variable `--mpx: 1.1111px`, or scale a 1728×972 mpx root by 10/9. Keep fraction-based positions unscaled. This matches the screenshots and what an Android TV shows at defaults.
- If the team keeps 1 mpx = 1 px, which is what the body of this spec writes and equals Android at 90% zoom, everything fixed-size will be about 10% smaller than the screenshots.
- Either way, keep the factor in one place. Android's own "Размер экрана" / UI zoom setting (wizard step 2, Display settings) changes it at runtime.
- **Older `dp` parts:** some components (popup buttons, seek bar, spinner, older player pieces) are sized in `dp`. 1 dp = 1.8 mpx, which is 1.8 px at 1 mpx = 1 px, or 2 px at 1 mpx = 1.111 px.

### 0.2 Only the "TV-box" branch ships

- `internal const val TvBoxNav = true` is hard-coded (`A/features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:2609`).
- Almost every shared component has two looks:
  - "Stage": glass, accent rings, glows, key hints;
  - "TV-box": flat grey bars, no key hints.
- **Build only the TV-box branch.** The other branch is dead code on the TV. That includes:
  - the guide's top control line;
  - key-hint rows;
  - the sport hub chips and cards;
  - the Floating/Docked navigation settings;
  - the wide two-column settings with a help panel.
- Each part marks such things "inactive" / "not shown".

### 0.3 Focus at a glance (full detail in 1.5)

- **Rows, buttons and items:** focus is a solid flat bar, `#E0E2E4` in dark and `#E7E8EA` in light, with 4 mpx corners. It has no ring, glow or shadow. Text on the bar switches to dark (`#121212`), and a pale accent becomes `#0086FC`. Only buttons scale (×1.04, 170 ms).
- **The wizard** uses a sliding grey bar, `#DADCDF`.
- **Player panels** use a dark grey bar `#3A3F45` with white text, not a light plate. This disagrees with the README's "white or light-grey plate" rule; the code is what is documented here.
- **Forced palettes:** the settings sheet and the player panels force the dark palette and SkMate blue `#0086FC` as accent and for switches. The INK accent shows mostly on the rail, tags and Home.

### 0.4 Code vs screenshot disagreements found (summary)

The details are in each part.

- **Scale:** see 0.1.
- **Wizard «Всё готово» (93):** the screenshot shows the guide row focused, but the code focuses Continue first.
- **Terms screen:** the fill is hard-coded dark `#232629`, but the text follows the theme, so in the light theme it would be dark on dark.
- **Channels (01):** there is no "playing" mark because the preview was not running on the emulator. «Вид телегида» looks brighter than the `Muted` colour the code specifies.
- **Settings:**
  - In the light theme the sheet turns white, although a code comment says it is always dark.
  - There is no search line, although a comment says "Search first".
  - The page count is passed in but never drawn.
- **Синхронизация ТВ (32):** the README says the connect window opens at once. In the code it opens only on OK on «Подключиться».
- **Эфир и буфер (28):** the screenshot shows live preview off. The code default is on, except on low-RAM devices.
- **Search (05):** «Поиск» is one of six rotating placeholder phrases, not a title.
- **Player:** a code comment calls the programme bar blue, but it is drawn white.

## 1. Design tokens

Source tree: `C:\Users\darsaec\Projects\skmate-android-main` (origin/main `3ad3636`). All paths below are relative to that repo root. Abbreviations used in citations:
`ST` = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/StageTokens.kt`, `SC` = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/stage/StageComponents.kt`, `SCtl` = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/stage/StageControls.kt`, `SD` = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/stage/StageDraw.kt`, `SP` = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/stage/StagePopup.kt`, `SSP` = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/stage/StageSidePanel.kt`, `PAL` = `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/theme/Palette.kt`, `SHELL` = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt`, `REPO` = `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/settings/SettingsRepository.kt`.

### 1.0 Read this first: the "TV-box" switch, units, and what the screenshots really show

1. **`TvBoxNav = true` is a hard-coded constant** (`SHELL:2609`). Almost every shared component has two looks, "Stage" (glass, accent rings, glows) and "TV-box" (flat grey bars). Because the flag is always on, **the TV-box branch is the shipped design**; the Stage branch is dead code on the TV. Wherever this spec says "TV-box", that is what to build. Examples: focus is a flat light-grey bar, not an accent ring (`SC:101-104`, `SC:194-213`); key-hint rows are not drawn at all (`SCtl:202`); menus and popups are solid cards, not glass (`SC:697`, `SP:100`).
2. **Units.** `1.mpx = 1/1.8 dp` (`ST:23-29`). The comment says the design is matched 1:1 "at the default UI Zoom of 90%" (`ST:15-22`), but the code default is **UI Zoom 100%** (`engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/theme/UiZoom.kt:11`, applied as `density × zoom/100` at `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/MainActivity.kt:489-493`). On a 1080p Android TV (320 dpi, density 2.0) at 100%, **1 mpx = 2.0/1.8 = 1.111 px**.
   - **Measured on the reference screenshots** (1920×1080): search field 48 mpx → ~52-53 px tall (`01-kanaly`, `10-nastroyki`); settings switch 56×32 mpx → ~62×35 px (`26-pleer-1`); switch knob 24 mpx → ~26 px. Ratio ≈ 1.10-1.11, i.e. the screenshots were taken at the 100% default.
   - **Consequence for web:** to match the screenshots pixel-for-pixel, render 1 mpx = **10/9 px (1.1111 px)** on the 1920×1080 stage (equivalently lay out a 1728×972 mpx canvas and scale it ×1.1111). If the team keeps 1 mpx = 1 px (the brief's assumption), everything will be ~10% smaller than the screenshots. **Decision needed; recommend 10/9.** Note: a few screens compute positions as a fraction of the real screen width, e.g. `fx(px) = screenW * px / 1920` in `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/features/live/LiveScreen.kt:665`, and settings `w * 64` etc. (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/features/settings/SettingsStage.kt:292,329`); those are true screen fractions and must NOT get the 10/9 factor.
   - Some older components use plain `dp` (popup buttons `SP:348-368`, seek bar `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/player/PlayerHudControls.kt:405-417`, spinner `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/components/StateViews.kt:28`). **1 dp = 1.8 mpx = 2 px at 100% zoom.**
   - Text sizes `N.mpxSp` follow the same rule plus the user's font-size % (`ST:28-29`; `MainActivity.kt:491-492`). Default font size 100%.
3. **Theme defaults:** theme mode `SYSTEM` (follows device; Android TV is dark) (`REPO:2804-2806`); accent preset **INK** (`REPO:2999-3001`); focus ring width setting 2 dp (`REPO:3024`, `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/Theme.kt:32-35`, only used by legacy non-Stage widgets); Glass ON at 50% opacity and background style STAGE on a fresh install (`REPO:3455-3467`); animations FULL (`REPO:886-887`). Main font Plus Jakarta Sans (`REPO:2824-2830`, `Theme.kt:96-97`).
4. **Many screens force their own palette:** the settings sheet always uses SkMate blue as accent (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/features/settings/SettingsStage.kt:507-510`), the player panels force the Dark palette + SkMate blue (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/features/shell/TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:150-154`). So the INK accent (cream/white) is mostly visible only on rails, tags and the Home screen.

### 1.1 Colours

#### 1.1.3 Accent presets (primary roles) — `PAL:95-124`

Each preset has 4 roles `primary / onPrimary / primaryContainer / onPrimaryContainer`. Default **INK** (`AccentColor.kt:12` in `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/theme/`, default at `REPO:3001`).

| Preset (string key) | Dark theme | Light theme |
|---|---|---|
| **INK** (`settings_accent_ink`) — default | `#F2F0ED` / `#121212` / `#343433` / `#FBFAF9` | `#121212` / `#FBFAF9` / `#F2F0ED` / `#121212` |
| TEAL (`settings_accent_teal`) | `#52DBC8` / `#003730` / `#004F46` / `#6FF8E4` | `#006B5E` / `#FFFFFF` / `#6FF8E4` / `#00201B` |
| BLUE (`settings_accent_blue`) | `#6FB0FF` / `#00315C` / `#134A7C` / `#D3E4FF` | `#1565C0` / `#FFFFFF` / `#D6E3FF` / `#001C3A` |
| VIOLET (`settings_accent_violet`) | `#CBBEFF` / `#312170` / `#483A88` / `#E7DEFF` | `#5B45C9` / `#FFFFFF` / `#E5DEFF` / `#190066` |
| GREEN (`settings_accent_green`) | `#6FDB94` / `#00391C` / `#1F5135` / `#8BF8AF` | `#1B6B3F` / `#FFFFFF` / `#A6F2C0` / `#00210F` |
| AMBER (`settings_accent_amber`) | `#FFB95C` / `#452B00` / `#624000` / `#FFDDB3` | `#8A5100` / `#FFFFFF` / `#FFDDB3` / `#2C1600` |

Custom hex accent (`PAL:156-177`): `primary` = the hex exactly; `onPrimary` = black if relative luminance > 0.5 else white; containers = same hue/saturation with HSL lightness pinned (dark: container L=0.26, onContainer L=0.90; light: 0.88 / 0.10).

Derived (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/OwnTVColors.kt:111-197`):
- `focusBorder` = custom "focus highlight" hex if set, else `primary` (`:131`). `focusGlow` = focus @ 0.40 dark / 0.28 light (`:159,:191`).
- `accentOnVideo` / `onAccentOnVideo` = the preset's **dark** roles in both themes (`:125-130`).
- `stageAccent` triple (`ST:233-239`): `accent = primary`, `onAccent = onPrimary`, `focus = focusBorder` (dark) or `primary` (light).
- HUD: `hudInk` = white (dark) / `text` (light) (`ST:187-189`); `hudShade(α)` = black@α (dark) / `rgb(244,247,245)` @ min(α×1.15, 0.92) (light) (`ST:194-195`); `hudAccent` = accentOnVideo (dark) / primary (light) (`ST:198-203`).
- Overrides: `LocalStageAccentOverride` (`ST:242`). Settings sheet, settings windows and player panels set **`StageAccent(accent = #0086FC, onAccent = #FFFFFF, focus = #FFFFFF)`** (`SettingsStage.kt:509`, `:1241`, `:1307`; `TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:152`). `LocalInSettingsSheet = true` inside the settings sheet and side panels (`SettingsStage.kt:510`, `SSP:114`) → every FX row there gets the grey focus bar.

### 1.2 Typography

- **Family:** Plus Jakarta Sans, one variable TTF `app/src/main/res/font/plus_jakarta_sans_variable.ttf`, weights 400/500/600/700/800 declared (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/FontCustomization.kt:46-53`; default at `:92-93`). User-selectable alternatives (Settings › font): Lora, Playfair Display, Dancing Script, Poppins (static 400/500/600/700), System sans, Monospace (`FontCustomization.kt:21-70`). Popups use the main font too (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/PopupTheme.kt:45-47`). `jetbrains_mono_semibold.ttf` also ships (font folder).
- The wordmark asks for weight **900** (`SkMateWordmark.kt:31`); Plus Jakarta Sans tops out at 800, so the TV renders it at 800.
- **Stage type rule:** `stageText(size, weight, letterSpacing)` = Plus Jakarta Sans at `size.mpxSp`, `FontWeight(weight)`, **line-height left to the font** ("`line-height: normal`") (`ST:247-257`). Web: `font: <weight> calc(<size> * var(--mpx)) "Plus Jakarta Sans"; line-height: normal;`. Explicit line heights where set: popup body 18 × 1.45 (`SP:195`), settings help text 17/27 (`SettingsStage.kt:588`).
- Tabular digits (`"tnum"`) on channel numbers (`LiveStage.kt:113,182`).
- Letter-spacing in `em`: tags 0.05em, group labels 0.12-0.13em, eyebrow 0.12em, LIVE badge 0.08em, wordmark 0.06em; titles with negative tracking: Live title −1 mpx (`LiveStage.kt:119`), popup title −0.5 mpx (`SP:188`).
- Group labels are passed already in CAPITALS; do not CSS-uppercase (Turkish i) (`SC:727-730`). Exception: `SettingPanelHeading` uppercases in code (`SettingsStage.kt:580-582`).

**TV-box rule:** every focus bar, every idle button/boxed tool fill, segmented capsule and search field uses **4 mpx** corners regardless of the element's own radius (`SC:254`, `SC:367`, `SC:468`, `SC:1343-1351`, `SC:1362`, `SCtl:98`). The element radii above apply only to things that are not the focus bar (cards, posters, popups, tags).

### 1.5 Focus treatment (the most important token)

`StageSurface` is the base of every focusable control (`SC:164-244`). Treatments (`SC:77-90`): FX (rows, cards), FILLED (menu items, sheet items, pills, tools, cells, default), PRIMARY (buttons), POSTER (posters), OUTLINE (rail icons), NONE.

**Shipped (TV-box) behaviour — build this:**

| State | Look | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Idle | nothing (rows) or `controlFill` with 4 mpx corners (buttons, boxed tools, search field, segmented capsule) | `SC:366-369`, `SC:468` |
| **Focused FX / FILLED / PRIMARY** | **solid bar `#E0E2E4` (dark) / `#E7E8EA` (light), corners 4 mpx, full width of the element, no border, no shadow, no glow** | `SC:194-213`, `SC:263-266` |
| Content on the focused bar | switched to the **Light palette** (text `#121212`, muted `#474645`, …) and `stageAccent` replaced by `(accent = SkMateBlue #0086FC if the real accent's luminance > 0.6 else the real accent, onAccent = #121212, focus = #121212)` | `SC:227-242`, `SC:1365` |
| Text/icons on the bar | use `onAccent` → `#121212` | e.g. `SC:424-435`, `SC:845` |
| Values on a focused menu row | `#0086FC` | `SC:794-800,1372` |
| Focused PRIMARY (buttons) | the grey bar **and** a scale-up to **1.04** about the centre | `SC:268-287` |
| Focused POSTER (posters, stills) | not the bar: Stage ring (see below) + scale **1.08** from (50%, 40%); episode stills 1.06 from (50%, 30%) | `SC:151-155`, `SC:268-287` |
| Focused OUTLINE (rail icons) | 2.5 mpx inner ring white (dark) / accent (light) | `SC:108`, `SC:130` |
| Boxed tool focused | the bar plus an extra overlay `text @ 9%` (4 mpx) | `SC:470-474` |
| Search field focused or editing | fill `#E0E2E4`, text/icon/caret `#121212`, placeholder `#121212` @ 60%; no ring | `SCtl:96-125,137-140,178-181` |
| In the player panels | focus is **`#3A3F45`** with white text (dark) / `#DDE0E4` with `#16181D` (light) | `TvBoxChannelPanel.kt:393-402` |

Scale animation: `animateFloatAsState(…, ownTvTween(170))` → **170 ms, FastOutSlowIn = `cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)`**, 0 ms when animations are off (`SC:277`; `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/Animations.kt:36-39`). The bar itself appears/disappears instantly (no fade).

Marquee: a cut-off title on a focused row scrolls (`basicMarquee`, infinite, initial and repeat delay **900 ms**); idle rows show "…"; focused overflow = clip (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/components/FocusMarquee.kt:11-25`). Compose default marquee velocity is 30 dp/s (= 54 mpx/s) — not overridden here.

**Stage treatment (dead code on TV, listed for completeness; only POSTER/OUTLINE still use it):** FX = accent 34%→14% horizontal sweep + 2 mpx inner focus ring + glow `0 0 44 accent@28%`; FILLED = accent fill + 2 mpx outer focus ring + `0 10 30 accent@35%`; PRIMARY = accent fill + 3 mpx outer ring + `0 14 40 accent@45%`; POSTER = 3 mpx outer focus ring + 4 mpx outer accent@35% ring outside it + `0 24 60 accent@35%` (`SC:129-156`). Light theme variants with a page-coloured 2.5/3 mpx gap ring (`SC:105-127`).

Mapping hint: `drawBoxShadow(color, blur, radius, dy, spread)` is literally CSS `box-shadow: 0 <dy> <blur> <spread> <color>` (`SD:49-83`); `drawOuterRing(color, w, r, inset)` = `box-shadow: 0 0 0 <inset+w>` ring; `drawInnerRing` = `inset 0 0 0 <w>` (`SD:85-109`). Shadows are skipped entirely when `DevicePerf.lite` (RAM < 3 GB) (`SD:66`; `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/DevicePerf.kt:12-24`).

### 1.7 Switch / toggle (`StageSwitch`, `SC:1293-1317`)

- Track 56×32 mpx, radius 16. Knob 24 mpx circle at x = 4 + 24·t, y = 4.
- Track colour = lerp(`ink@14%`, accent, t); knob = lerp(`knobOff` (#D0D3D6 dark / #FFFFFF light), #FFFFFF, t).
- In the settings sheet accent = `#0086FC` → ON = blue track, white knob; OFF = white 14% track, `#D0D3D6` knob (matches `26-pleer-1.png`).
- Animation: 200 ms FastOutSlowIn (`tween(MotionPrefs.dur(200))`); 0 on lite devices or animations off (`SC:1317`).
- Display-only: the row owns focus; OK on the row toggles. Stepper likewise: ◀ ▶ on the row change the value; − / + are only for mouse/touch (`SC:1258-1291`).

### 1.10 Scrims, page background, glass

- **Page:** root `page` colour (`MainActivity.kt:519`). In dark TV-box the shell base is `#232629` and the Stage lights background is **not** drawn (`SHELL:1070-1075,1296`). With Glass on (default) the shell base is transparent and the root `#232629` shows: same result. Light theme draws the Stage background: vertical wash `#F8FAF9` → `#F1F5F3` (55%) → `#EEF1F6` plus soft elliptical lights (`SD:121-149`); Plain style is `#F4F7F5` / `#070B0A`.
- Picture backgrounds (Settings › Glass & background): picture is blurred once on the CPU into 10 mip levels; glass panels draw the matching slice (fake `backdrop-filter`) (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/Glass.kt:29-98`, `SD:162-218`). Looks: SHARP dim 15 blur 0, SOFT dim 50 blur 80 (+accent light), DARK dim 75 blur 30 (`engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/theme/Background.kt:12-16`).
- **Glass panel (`stageGlass`)** (`SD:179-218`): dark fill `#1E2125` at the glass opacity (default 50%; "over content" chrome = 0.55 + 0.45×α); light fill white at 0.3 + 0.8×α; 1 mpx top highlight white 10% (light 90%); 1 mpx inner rim ink 7% (glass off 5%/8%); shadow `0 24 60` black 45% (light `rgb(20,40,32)` 16%, glass off `0 20 50` 14%). Glass off: solid `#1E2125`. In TV-box, menus and popups do **not** use glass (`SC:697`, `SP:100`); glass remains only on pills/buttons in non-TV-box branches → effectively rare on TV.
- Popup scrim: `scrim` token, full screen, tap outside closes (`SP:82-90`). Side panel scrim black 55% fading with the slide (`SSP:83-89`).
- Backdrop left wash (photos): light stops 0→100%, 30%→75%, 62%→0 (`SD:227-231`).

### 1.11 Popups, menus, side panels

**`StagePopup`** (`SP:55-124`): full-screen scrim; centred card (top-aligned when the on-screen keyboard is up, capped to the room above it); default width **880** (confirm/notice **760**, text input **820**); edge margin 40; solid card radius 30 (`#232629` / white, shadow `0 14 40` black 50%/16%); padding 12 sides / 30 top-bottom; heading: eyebrow 15/800 +0.12em dim → title 38/800 (top 6 after eyebrow) → body 18/400 lh 1.45 muted (top 10) → 22 gap; content default text 19/500; buttons row bottom-right, top 24, gap 14. Focus is trapped; Back closes (`SP:59`). Popup size and popup font size settings scale it (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/components/OwnTVPopup.kt:121-131`).
- `StageConfirm` (`SP:126-153`): Cancel + tinted confirm (56 high, 19 px); focus lands on confirm after 60 ms, or on Cancel when `focusCancel`.
- `StageNotice` (`SP:155-170`): one tinted OK button.
- Tinted button idle in TV-box = same `controlFill` slab as other buttons ("tinted is not a look it has") (`SC:358-370`).
- `StagePopupOption` rows: min 84 / 96, radius 18, FX focus (= grey bar), title 25/600, line 19/500, value "`<value> ›`" 18/700 accent (focused `#0086FC`) (`SP:202-268`).
- `StagePopupButton`: radius 14 dp, padding 22×12 dp (compact 13×6), icon 20 dp; primary idle accent 20% + accent text, secondary ink 8% + text (`SP:330-380`).
- `StagePopupLabel` 14/800 dim, padding top 6 bottom 10; `StagePopupDivider` 1 mpx ink 10% with 18 vertical margin (`SP:314-328`).

**`StageMenu`** (`SC:684-706`): solid card radius 28 (TV-box), padding 16/16/22/16, scrolls inside, max height screen − 64 dp. Header (`SC:708-725`), group label (`SC:727-745`), items 76 mpx with a 1 mpx hairline `text@8%` inset 14 each side (`SC:765-777`), icon 28 muted, text 23/600, value 17/700 dim, CHECK 22.

**`StageSidePanel`** (Android-TV system-style right panel, used for short single-choice lists such as language) (`SSP:51-177`): full height, **width 620**, fill `#1E2228` (white in light), padding top/bottom 48; title 34/800 at inset 30; subtitle 17/500 muted; rows `StageSidePanelOption` min 76, icon 30, title 22/700, subtitle 15/500, edge-to-edge grey focus bar (radius 0 requested; TV-box bar is 4 mpx). Enter = slide in from the right (`slideInHorizontally { it }`) + fade in; exit = slide out + fade out; **240 ms**, Compose default tween easing FastOutSlowIn; scrim black 55% fades over the same 240 ms. Back or tap outside closes with the same animation, then calls dismiss.

**Notices / toasts** (`TvNotices.kt:31-125`): top-centre stack 40 mpx from the top (`SHELL:2546-2548`), max 3, newest on top, identical message restarts instead of stacking; plate `#F53A3E44`, radius 14, padding 28×14, width 360-760, centred text. Enter = drops from behind the top edge (translateY from −(height+60)) 320 ms FastOutSlowIn; exit = rolls up 260 ms FastOutLinearIn (`cubic-bezier(0.4,0,1,1)`); default visible 5000 ms (toast 2200 ms, `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/components/InAppToast.kt:48`).

### 1.12 Key hints / keycaps

Not drawn on the TV: `StageKeyHints` returns immediately when TV-box (`SCtl:194-226`). (Stage look for reference: 17 px muted labels, 26 apart, caps min 34×30, radius 8, 14/800 on `keycap`.)

### 1.13 Icons

- **One enum `OwnTVIcon`, 97 names** (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/components/OwnTVIcon.kt:26-55`). Resolution order when drawing (`OwnTVIcon.kt:93-128`):
  1. not filled and present in **Unicons Line** set → filled path on a **24-unit grid** (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/components/UniconsIcons.kt:11-38`). Covered: DOWNLOADS, EPG, EXPAND, GEAR, GRID, TILES, INFO, LIST, MOVIES, PALETTE, PERSON, PHONE, PLAY_CIRCLE, REC, SEARCH, SEND, SPORTS, STAR, LIVE_TV, HOME.
  2. otherwise **Phosphor** (regular, or `fill` weight when `filled = true`) → filled path on a **256-unit grid** (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/components/PhosphorIcons.kt:12-223`, generated from `@phosphor-icons/core` 2.1.1, MIT, by `tools/icons/gen_phosphor.py`). Phosphor has an entry for **every** enum value, so steps 3-4 (core `StageGlyph` stroked paths in `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/ui/stage/StageGlyph.kt`, and the hand-drawn Canvas glyphs) are effectively unused.
- **Web reuse:** copy the path strings verbatim into inline SVG: `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentColor" d="…"/></svg>` for Unicons and `viewBox="0 0 256 256"` for Phosphor. Tint = `fill`. Size = the mpx size passed (square). Licences: Unicons (IconScout, free commercial), Phosphor MIT; see `docs/third-party/ui-icons.md` per the generator comment.
- Common sizes: menu item 28 (TV-box), sheet item 28, group item 23, pill/tool 20, search 20, segmented 17, side-panel option 30, popup icon 21, checks 22-24, warning 18, playing mark 30.

### 1.14 Motion

| Setting / token | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Default tween | 200 ms, FastOutSlowIn `cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1)` | `Animations.kt:36-39` |
| Focus lift (PRIMARY/POSTER) | 170 ms FastOutSlowIn | `SC:277` |
| Switch knob | 200 ms FastOutSlowIn | `SC:1298,1317` |
| Side panel slide + scrim | 240 ms | `SSP:83-101,138` |
| Settings sheet slide | 300 ms (`SheetSlideMs`); pages inside switch instantly (`SideInMs = 0`) | `SHELL:2616`; `SettingsStage.kt:573-574` |
| Side-menu push | 300 ms (`MENU_PUSH_MS`) | `SHELL:2638` |
| Full screen grow / shrink | 320 / 260 ms × speed (0 when not smooth) | `SHELL:2619-2620` |
| Picture dock into corner | 0 ms (instant on purpose) | `SHELL:2631` |
| First picture fade-in | 320 ms after a 250 ms delay | `SHELL:2641-2644` |
| Notice in / out | 320 ms FastOutSlowIn / 260 ms FastOutLinearIn | `TvNotices.kt:75-101` |
| Marquee delay | 900 ms | `FocusMarquee.kt:25` |
| Skeleton pulse | 900 ms, reverse | `LiveStage.kt:917-919` |
| Spinner | 1000 ms per turn, 12 steps | `StateViews.kt:64-68` |

**Animations off / weak device** (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/MotionPrefs.kt:13-126`):
- Settings › Animations On/Off (`AnimationLevel.FULL/OFF`; OFF scales every `ownTvTween` to 0 ms = instant snap, `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/theme/AnimationLevel.kt:9-17`).
- Speed FAST ×0.6 / NORMAL ×1 / SLOW ×1.5 (`MotionPrefs.kt:14,107-113`).
- "Adapt to the device" (`auto`, default on): animations stand still while the device is `throttled` — set at start on weak hardware (low-RAM, RAM < 3 GB, or ≤ 2 cores) or learned from dropped frames; `active = animationsOn && !(auto && throttled)` (`MotionPrefs.kt:23-55,100-105,125`). Web equivalent: treat old Tizen/webOS boxes as "lite" (no shadows, no glows, instant panels).
- `DevicePerf.lite` (RAM < 3 GB): no box-shadows, no background lights, no row hairlines, no skeleton pulse, switch snaps, menu overlays instead of pushing (`DevicePerf.kt:6-24`, `SD:66,156`, `LiveStage.kt:172,917`, `SC:1317`).


## 2. Screens

### 2a. Agreement screen and first-run wizard

Source tree: `skmate-android-main` at commit `3ad3636`. Paths below are short: `A/` = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/`, `C/` = `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/`. Strings are named by Android resource key (texts in section 3a).

#### 2a.0 Read this first: scale of the screenshots

- `mpx` is defined as `1/1.8 dp` (`A/ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:23-26`). The comment there says the design is matched at UI zoom **90 %** (`StageTokens.kt:18-21`).
- But the default UI zoom is **100 %** (`C/theme/UiZoom.kt:11`), and zoom multiplies density (`A/MainActivity.kt:490-493`). So on a real TV at defaults, **1 mpx = 1.111 screen px**.
- The reference screenshots 90-93 were taken at 100 % (screenshot 90 shows "Масштаб интерфейса 100%"). Measured: wizard column 1040 mpx = 1156 px (382→1538), button 260×76 mpx = 289×84 px, logo height 40 mpx = 44 px, row 84 mpx = 93 px. All match the 1.111 factor.
- Consequence for the web: if the stage is drawn with 1 mpx = 1 px, every screen will look about 10 % smaller than the screenshots (it then equals Android at 90 % zoom). To match the screenshots pixel for pixel at the default setting, use 1 mpx = 1.1111 px (e.g. `font-size`/`transform: scale(1.1111)` on the stage). This must be decided once for the whole app; it is not specific to this screen.
- Text size additionally scales with the font-size setting (60-140 %, default 100 %) via `fontScale` (`MainActivity.kt:492`, `C/theme/FontCustomization.kt:20-27`). `mpxSp` text follows it, `mpx` boxes do not.

#### 2a.1 When the agreement and the wizard appear

Order of decisions, every launch (`A/MainActivity.kt:504-615`):

1. **Terms of use** first, before anything else is composed. `termsAccepted = settings.termsAcceptedVersion >= TERMS_VERSION` (`A/features/shell/ShellViewModel.kt:186-188`), `TERMS_VERSION = 1` (`A/features/setup/TermsOfUseScreen.kt:40`). While the stored value is still loading (`null`) the screen is blank; `false` shows the terms; `true` continues (`MainActivity.kt:506-515`). Bumping `TERMS_VERSION` asks everyone again once.
   - Accept: `settings.acceptTerms(1)` (`ShellViewModel.kt:190-192`).
   - Decline: `finishAffinity()` = the app closes (`MainActivity.kt:511`).
2. Then a `Crossfade` of 320 ms (`ScreenFadeMs = 320`, `MainActivity.kt:153`, `:540`) between:
   - profile id or list not loaded yet → blank (`:543`);
   - adding a profile from the gate → `Onboarding(firstRun = false)` (starts at "Create profile") (`:545-550`);
   - **no active profile (`activeProfileId < 0`) → `Onboarding(firstRun = true)`** = the full first-run wizard (`:552-557`);
   - stored active id not in the list → `Onboarding(firstRun = false)` (`:561-566`);
   - profile gate "Кто смотрит?" only when the last profile has a PIN (TV-box mode, `TvBoxNav = true` `A/features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:2609`; `MainActivity.kt:412-416`) or a switch was requested;
   - otherwise the shell.
3. A wizard left half way: the created-but-unfinished profile id is kept in prefs `setup_pending/pending_profile_id` and deleted on the next start or when the wizard's ViewModel is cleared (`A/features/setup/SetupViewModel.kt:42-62`, `:276-277`). Finishing removes the mark (`:269-273`).

#### 2a.2 Agreement screen (Условия использования)

File `A/features/setup/TermsOfUseScreen.kt`. No screenshot in the pack.

Layout (all mpx):
- Full-screen fill `#232629` hard-coded (`:133-135`), in **both** themes. Text colours do follow the theme palette (`StageColors.Text`/`Muted`), so in the light theme the text would be dark on dark (code fact; the screen normally shows before any theme choice, default theme = System).
- Centred column, max width 1100, padding top 56 bottom 40, content centred horizontally (`:92-95`, `TEXT_WIDTH = 1100` `:138`).
- Title `terms_title`: 40 / weight 800, `StageColors.Text` (`:96`, `:140`, `:144`).
- Scrolling text block (fills remaining height, top padding 28) (`:97`). Sections in order (`:43-53`): `terms_intro` (no heading), then heading+body pairs `terms_h_player/terms_player`, `terms_h_content/terms_content`, `terms_h_data/terms_data`, `terms_h_warranty/terms_warranty`, `terms_h_liability/terms_liability`, `terms_h_license/terms_license`, `terms_h_changes/terms_changes`, `terms_h_contact/terms_contact`.
  - Heading: 26 / 800, Text colour, padding top 26 bottom 10 (`:100-103`, `:141`).
  - Body: 24 / 500, Muted (`:105`, `:142`, `:145`).
  - After the sections: `terms_confirm` 24/500 Muted, padding top 26 bottom 8 (`:107-112`).
- Buttons row under the text, padding top 24, gap 16, centred (the column is `CenterHorizontally`) (`:93-94`, `:122`). Order: `terms_decline`, then `terms_accept` (`:123-129`).
  - Each is a `StageButton` 56 high, text 19 (`:124`, `:127`, `BUTTON_TEXT = 19` `:143`). StageButton = text weight 700, horizontal padding 30, icon/text gap 12 (`A/ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:374-380`).
  - TV-box look: idle fill `ControlFill` = ink 6 % (dark: white 6 %), corners 4 (`FocusBarShape`) (`StageComponents.kt:359-367`, `:254`). `tinted = true` on Accept is ignored in TV-box mode (`:358-360`).
  - Focus: grey bar `#E0E2E4` (dark) / `#E7E8EA` (light), corners 4, text switches to the light palette ink `#121212` (`StageComponents.kt:194-198`, `:209-210`, `:228-241`, `:263`, `:266`), plus lift scale 1.04 animated 170 ms FastOutSlowIn (`:268-286`, PRIMARY).

Behaviour:
- Initial focus: **Accept**, requested after 150 ms (`:73-77`, `FOCUS_DELAY_MS = 150` `:137`).
- ◀ ▶: move between Decline and Accept (default focus search).
- ▲ ▼: do **not** move focus; they scroll the text by 160 mpx per press with an animated scroll (`animateScrollBy`, Compose default spring) (`:78-89`, `SCROLL_STEP = 160` `:139`). So Accept is reachable without scrolling to the end.
- OK on Accept: saves version 1, app continues. OK on Decline: app closes.
- Back: no handler on this screen; system default (leaves the app). Back held ~12 key repeats closes the app everywhere (`MainActivity.kt:66-74`, `:156`).
- Language: if the stored language is still "system default", it is set to `firstRunLanguage()` = the TV's language when the app has it and it is not English, otherwise **Russian** (`:65-72`; `A/features/settings/LanguageSettingsScreen.kt:290-301`).

#### 2a.3 Wizard frame (shared by every step)

Host: `Onboarding()` `A/features/setup/SetupWizard.kt:80-333`. Frame: `WizardFrame()` `A/features/setup/WizardFrame.kt:87-212`.

Backdrop and header:
- Backdrop: `StagePalette.page` = dark `#232629`, light `#FBFAF9` (`WizardFrame.kt:314-315`; `StageTokens.kt:96`, `:126`). Drawn once by the host (`SetupWizard.kt:111`).
- The header does **not** slide with the pages: the host draws it once and each frame reports its step (`SetupWizard.kt:110-112`, `:130`; `WizardFrame.kt:116-121`, `:247-248`).
- Logo: bitmap `skmate_logo.png` (`engine/core/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi/skmate_logo.png`), height 40, padding start 64 top 48 (`WizardFrame.kt:252-258`).
- Step indicator top right, padding end 64 top 52 (`:259`), `StepNumbers` (`:266-296`):
  - 5 steps: 1 `settings_language` (Язык), 2 `setup_step_display` (Экран), 3 `settings_group_profile` (Профиль), 4 `setup_step_playlist` (Плейлист), 5 `setup_step_ready` (Готово) (`:78`, `:319-325`).
  - Items in a row, gap 10 (`:270-273`). Between circles a line 22×2 (`:280`).
  - Circle 34×34, border 2, digit 16 / 800 (`:281-286`).
  - Reached (index ≤ current): fill and ring `SkMateBlue #0086FC` (`A/ui/brand/SkMateWordmark.kt:16`), digit white. Later: fill transparent, ring `#4A5059` dark / `#D9D3CB` light, digit `Muted` (`:276-279`, `:316-317`). The line before a circle takes that circle's ring colour.
  - The current step's name is shown right after its circle: 16 / 700, Text colour, one line (`:287-293`).
  - Animation on step change: colours 320 ms tween; name fades + expands horizontally in / fades + shrinks out 320 ms; the whole row `animateContentSize` 320 ms (`:268-298`). Default tween easing = FastOutSlowIn (cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)).
  - Screenshots 90-93 confirm: blue circles + blue lines up to the current, grey rings after, label beside the current.

Page transition between steps (`SetupWizard.kt:113-129`):
- Forward (`target.ordinal >= initial.ordinal` of the internal `Step` enum `:71`): new page slides in from +1/8 of the width and fades in over 300 ms; old page slides out to −1/8 and fades out over 150 ms. Backward: mirrored. `StepSlideMs = 300` (`:69`). All durations go through `MotionPrefs.dur()` (0 when animations are off).

Content column (`WizardFrame.kt:123-131`):
- Centred on screen both ways; width 1040 (`width` param, default `:94`) but never wider than screen − 128; max height screen − 210; scrolls vertically if taller; `focusGroup`.
- Optional `titleAbove` (welcome TV).
- Title: size 60 (welcome 64), weight 600, letter-spacing −0.03 em, Text colour, centred (`:101`, `:140`).
- Subtitle (if any): 20 / 500, line height 30, Muted, centred, top 14 (`:143-151`).
- Body: top 36 (welcome 0), rows stacked with gap 6 (`:103`, `:152`).
- Key hints row at bottom (`:203-210`) is **not drawn** in TV-box mode (`A/ui/stage/StageControls.kt:201-202`). Screenshots confirm: no hints.

Footer buttons (`WizardFrame.kt:153-201`, `WizardButton` `:218-245`):
- Order left→right: Back (if any), extra buttons (e.g. Reset), Next (primary) (`:154`).
- Row top padding 28, gap 14 (`:158`, `:181`).
- Width: 260 each when 2+ buttons; a single button is `singleWidth` = 520 (Add-playlist step: 1040) (`:105`, `:157`). Welcome: `matchTitleWidth` → button width = title width − (globe width + 14), min 260 (`:110`, `:166-168`).
- Height 76, corners 4 (`:232`, `:304`).
- Idle fill: ink 7 % (dark = white 7 %); a disabled **primary** button uses 3 % (`:223`). Label 24 / 800, Text colour, one line, ellipsis (`:241`). Disabled label alpha 0.4 (`:237`, `:312`).
- Icons: Back button gets a left arrow (`OwnTVIcon.BACK`, 26) before the text; the primary gets a right chevron (`CHEVRON`, 20) after it; icon-text gap 12 (`:238-243`).
- Focus: the buttons themselves draw nothing (`StageFocus.NONE`, `:228`). One shared highlight box (same width × 76, fill `#DADCDF`, corners 4) sits behind the row and **slides** to the focused button: 200 ms FastOutSlowIn when moving inside the row; when focus enters the row it snaps under the focused button; its alpha fades 0↔1 over 200 ms on enter/leave (`:169-190`, `HighlightMs = 200` `:301`, `WizardFocusFill` `:307`). Focused label colour `#1B1E22` (`:236`, `:310-311`). Same in light and dark.
- Back key: calls the Back button's action, or `onBack` when given (`:93`, `:115`). Steps with neither have no Back handler.

Choice cards `WizardCards` (`WizardFrame.kt:328-359`):
- Equal widths (weight 1), equal heights (tallest), min height 170, gap 16, content top-left (`:336-348`).
- Corners 16; idle fill ink 5 % (`:340-342`).
- Inside: padding 24; icon 38 (Muted idle, Text when focused); title 23 / 800, one line, top 18; line 16 / 500, line height 23, Muted, max 2 lines with ellipsis, top 6 (`:351-354`).
- Focus = `StageFocus.FX` → TV-box grey bar: fill `#E0E2E4` (dark) / `#E7E8EA` (light), **corners 4** (not 16), and the card content is drawn with the light palette (dark text) (`StageComponents.kt:190-243`). Screenshot 92 shows exactly this: the focused card is a light grey square-ish block with black text, the idle cards are dark rounded tiles.
- First card gets initial focus (60 ms after the step appears, `rememberFirstFocus` `SetupWizard.kt:336-341`).

Settings rows used inside the wizard (`StageSettingRow` `A/features/settings/SettingsStage.kt:891-990`, not in the side sheet so `side = false`):
- Height 84, full width, inner padding 0 22, gap 18 (`:932`, `:950-951`).
- Icon 36, Muted (`:954`).
- Title 21 / 700 Text (Dim when disabled), one line (marquee when focused) (`:957-964`); description 15.5 / 500 Muted, top 3, one line (`:976-982`). Focused description colour = `FxLine` of the light palette `#343433` (`:972`, `:1007`; `StageTokens.kt:135`).
- Value on the right (`SettingValueView` `:1010-1039`): Switch, Stepper, Segmented, Opens (chevron 20 Muted, optional accent text 18/700), Custom.
- Focus: FX → grey bar `#E0E2E4`, corners 4, content in light palette; the value is drawn with accent = SkMateBlue while focused (`focusedBlue` `:1634-1641`).
- ◀ ▶ on a row with `onStep` call it with −1/+1 and are consumed (`:934-944`).
- Stepper `.stp`: background ControlFill, corners 13, padding 4, gap 4: "−" box 34×34 (text 20/700 Muted), value 18/700 Text min width 64 centred, "+" box 34×34 (`A/ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:1262-1288`).
- Segmented (as row value, display only): background ControlFill corners 15 padding 4; options 40 high, padding 0 14, gap 2, text 17/700 (chosen Text, others Muted); the chosen pill (`segmentOn`: dark white 13 %, light white; corners 11) slides and stretches between options in 160 ms (`SettingsStage.kt:1041-1083`; `StageTokens.kt:110`, `:140`).
- Switch: 56×32 track corners 16; knob 24 at x = 4 + 24·t, y 4; track colour lerps from ink 14 % to accent, knob from `knobOff` (dark `#D0D3D6`, light white) to white; 200 ms FastOutSlowIn (`StageComponents.kt:1293-1317`). With the default accent INK, accent in dark = `#F2F0ED` (`C/theme/Palette.kt:121-124`, default accent `C/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:2999-3002`), so an "on" switch on an idle row is a near-white track (screenshot 90 "Пример строки") and on a focused row it is SkMateBlue.

Text field rows `StageFieldRow` (`SettingsStage.kt:1359-1523`):
- Height 90, padding 0 22, gap 18; icon 32 Muted; label 21/700 Text; line under it 19/500 top 3 (`:1416`, `:1452-1465`).
- Empty value: placeholder in Dim (`:1466-1471`). Value text: 19 weight 600 (SemiBold), Text colour; FxLine when focused/editing (`:1488-1491`). Password: dots (`:1502-1503`).
- Focused and not editing: "`common_edit`" (Изменить) 18/700 in SkMateBlue at the right (`:1513-1520`); a password row also shows "▶ `common_show`/`common_hide`" 18/700 Muted (`:1508-1510`).
- OK → editing: the row stays highlighted, the real text input takes focus, the system on-screen keyboard opens, the row is scrolled into view after 120 ms (`:1397-1407`, `:1409-1413`).
- ▶ on a password row (not editing) toggles show/hide (`:1419-1424`). Typing a printable key on a hardware keyboard starts editing with that character; Ctrl+V pastes (`:1428-1441`).
- Back (key up) or IME "Done" ends editing and returns focus to the row (`:1390-1396`, `:1481-1486`, `:1501`). Leaving focus also ends editing (`:1480`).

#### 2a.4 Step order (first run)

Internal steps enum (`SetupWizard.kt:71`): WELCOME, DISPLAY_SIZE, DISCLAIMER, SETUP_CHOICE, SYNC_DEVICE, CREATE_PROFILE, ADD_CONTENT, ADD_SOURCE_REMOTE, ADD_SOURCE, IMPORTING, EXISTING, IMPORT_BACKUP_CHOOSER, IMPORT_BACKUP_REMOTE, IMPORT_BACKUP. First run starts at WELCOME; "add profile" starts at CREATE_PROFILE (`:86`).

Main path: Welcome (indicator 1) → Display size (2) → Setup choice (3) → Create profile (3) → Add playlist (4) → a way to add → Importing (4) → All set (5) → app.

`DISCLAIMER` is still in code (`:140-146`) but nothing navigates to it any more: Display size goes straight to Setup choice (`:136-139`). Do not build it.

##### Step 1. Welcome (no screenshot in the pack)

`WelcomeStep` `SetupWizard.kt:360-374`.
- Indicator: step 1 (LANGUAGE).
- Above the title: looping Lottie animation `res/raw/welcome_tv.json` (120 frames at 24 fps, endless), box 220×198, bottom padding 8 (`:343-357`; file `app/src/main/res/raw/welcome_tv.json`, 40 KB).
- Title `setup_welcome_short` (Добро пожаловать!) size 64 (`:364`, `:369`). No subtitle, no body (bodyTop 0).
- Buttons row: one primary button `setup_get_started` (Начать) with chevron, width = title width − 76 − 14 (min 260); to its right a 76×76 language globe button (`:367`, `:371-372`).
- Globe `FirstRunLanguageGlobe` (`A/features/settings/LanguageSettingsScreen.kt:81-103`): corners 16, idle = 2 px border Text at 14 %, icon LANGUAGE 38 Text; focus FX (grey bar, corners 4). OK opens the language side panel.
- Language side panel (`LanguageSettingsScreen.kt:105-152`, `StageSidePanel` `A/ui/stage/StageSidePanel.kt`): title `settings_language`; width 620 (`StageSidePanel.kt:62`); slides in from the right + fades in, 240 ms, scrim fades with it (`:83`, `:100-101`, `PanelMs = 240` `:138`); panel fill `#1E2228`, scrim `#8C000000` (`:142-143`), vertical padding 48 (`:109`), side inset 30 (`:141`). (The panel itself is specified with the other side panels.) First row "`settings_language_system_default`" with line `settings_language_system_default_description`, then each language (endonym 22/700 Text, English name 15/500 Muted, radio on the right; row padding = side inset × 12 vertical, gap 12) (`LanguageSettingsScreen.kt:256-285`). Focus starts on the selected language after 80 ms. OK applies the language and closes.
- Initial focus: Get started (60 ms). ▶ goes to the globe.
- OK on Get started → Display size. Back: no handler in the wizard (system default).
- On first composition the language is set to `firstRunLanguage()` if still "system" (`LanguageSettingsScreen.kt:85-87`).

##### Step 2. Display size (screenshot 90-master-razmer)

`DisplaySizeStep` `SetupWizard.kt:381-481`, ViewModel `A/features/setup/DisplaySizeViewModel.kt`.
- Indicator: step 2. Title `setup_display_size_title`, subtitle `setup_display_sub`.
- Rows (StageSettingRow), in order:
  1. Icon ZOOM, `settings_ui_zoom` / `setup_line_zoom`, Stepper "`common_percent`" (e.g. 100%). ◀ −5, ▶ +5, OK +5. Range 50-150, step 5, default 100 (`:409-417`, `C/theme/UiZoom.kt:9-18`). Stepping below 85 % (`LOW_RAM_WARN`) the first time asks a confirmation (see below) (`:391-400`).
  2. Icon TEXT_SIZE, `settings_font_size` / `setup_line_font`, Stepper %. ◀ ▶ ±5, OK +5. Range 60-140, default 100 (`:418-425`, `C/theme/FontCustomization.kt:20-27`).
  3. Icon THEME, `settings_theme` / `setup_line_theme`, Segmented [`settings_theme_system`, `settings_theme_dark`, `settings_theme_light`]. ◀ ▶ move the choice (clamped, no wrap); OK cycles to the next with wrap (`:426-446`).
  4. Icon PLAY_CIRCLE, `setup_display_sample_title` / `setup_display_sample_desc`, Switch, starts ON; OK flips it. It is only a sample row: the value is not saved anywhere (`:447-455`).
- Buttons: Back (`common_back`, arrow) → Welcome; Reset (`settings_reset`) → zoom 100, theme System, font 100 (`DisplaySizeViewModel.kt:59-64`); Continue (`setup_continue`, chevron, primary) → Setup choice (`:401-408`, `:136-139`).
- Saved immediately on every press (no Apply): `uiZoomPercent`, `fontCustomization.sizePercent`, `themeMode` (`DisplaySizeViewModel.kt:38-51`). The whole wizard re-lays out live because zoom/font scale the density (`MainActivity.kt:490-493`).
- Initial focus: the zoom row (60 ms).
- D-pad: ▲ ▼ between rows and to the buttons row; ◀ ▶ on a row change the value (consumed, focus does not leave the row); in the buttons row ◀ ▶ move between Back / Reset / Continue.
- Back key → Welcome.
- Low-zoom confirmation (`:458-480`): `StageConfirm` (`A/ui/stage/StagePopup.kt:131-153`), width 760, no eyebrow, title `settings_low_zoom_warning_title`, body `settings_low_zoom_warning` (both %d = 85), buttons Cancel (`common_cancel`) and `settings_low_zoom_accept`, 56 high text 19; **focus starts on Cancel**. Accept applies the pending zoom and stops asking for this visit; Cancel/Back keep the zoom. Focus returns to the zoom row. Popup title 38/800 letter-spacing −0.5, body 18/400 line height 1.45 Muted top 10, 22 gap before buttons (`StagePopup.kt:172-200`).
- Screenshot 90 vs code: matches (3 buttons 260 wide, theme row focused shows white chosen pill "Как в системе" on grey, sample switch on = near-white track).

Avatar: icon PERSON, `setup_avatar` / `setup_avatar_line`, value = avatar strip: 6 avatars starting from the chosen one (wrapping), each 44 inside a 50 box, gap 8; the chosen one ringed 3 px SkMateBlue (circle); then "+N" (count not shown) 17/700 Muted (`:535-544`, `AvatarStrip` `:575-589`). ◀ ▶ previous/next avatar (wraps), OK next.

##### Step 4b. Type it here (form, no screenshot)

`AddSourceScreen` `A/features/setup/AddSourceScreen.kt:85-850`, called from the wizard with `showDefaultToggle = false` and without `onStartStalker`, so **Stalker is hidden** (`SetupWizard.kt:210-238`, `AddSourceScreen.kt:114`, `:596`). This page is not a `WizardFrame`: the wizard header stays as it was (step 4).
- Page `StageFormPage` (`SettingsStage.kt:1227-1291`), not windowed: centred column width 1000 (max screen − 128), full height with padding top 116 bottom 60, content vertically centred (`:1250-1266`, `FormWidth` `:1350`).
- Path line: parent `setup_add_source` 30/700 −0.02 em Dim, "›" 30/700 Dim, title `settings_add_type_here` 30/800 −0.02 em Text, gap 14 (`:1267-1273`; `AddSourceScreen.kt:500-501`).
- Rows: top 28, gap 6, scroll; footer below rows with top 28 (`:1274-1287`).
- Rows when adding (only what the source needs; the "optional" block is edit-only, `full = editing` `:140`, `:690-786`):
  1. Type (icon LIST, `settings_source_type` / `settings_line_source_type`), Segmented [`setup_m3u`, `setup_xtream`]; default M3U (or what the last failed try used); ◀ ▶ change, OK cycles (`:594-619`).
  2. M3U: field `setup_playlist_url_local_file`, placeholder `setup_playlist_example`, URL keyboard, check mark at the right (`:641-646`); then local `.m3u/.m3u8` files found on the TV as rows (PLAYLIST icon, file name / folder name, Switch = selected), or a row FOLDER `setup_files_hidden_grant_access` / `settings_line_local_file` without storage permission, or FOLDER `setup_local_files_none` / `setup_local_files_none_hint` (`:647-656`, `:945-994`).
  3. Xtream: field `setup_server_url` (placeholder `setup_server_example`, URL keyboard, check mark; pasting a full `get.php?username=…&password=…` link splits it into server, user and password), field `setup_username`, password field `setup_password` (`:622-640`).
- Validation (`:407-480`): an address without `://` (M3U: unless it starts with `/`) shows `setup_error_address_scheme`. When the fields look complete, after a 900 ms pause the source is tested automatically, up to 3 attempts 1.5 s apart while "unreachable" (`:423-457`, `:930-934`). Result in the URL row: Ok = CHECK icon 28 `#1FA34A`; failed = CLOSE icon 26 `StageColors.Danger`; running = `setup_testing` 16/600 Dim (`:459-466`, `:937`). Failure texts: `setup_error_login`, `setup_error_expired`, `setup_error_not_playlist`, `setup_error_denied`, `setup_error_unreachable`.
- Error lines under the rows: INFO icon 24 Muted + text 18/600 Text, gap 10; lines 20 apart (`:558-565`).
- Submit button `setup_start_import` (footer): 440×76, corners 4; idle fill Text colour when it can be sent, Text 8 % when not; label 22/800 with DOWNLOADS icon 26, gap 12, label colour = page colour (or Dim when disabled); focused fill SkMateBlue, label white; disabled = not focusable (`:567-590`). Enabled when no errors and (check OK, or M3U check failed — still allowed, or local file) and required fields filled (`:467-474`).
- ▶ from any row jumps straight to the submit button (`focusProperties { right = … }`, `:516`).
- First focus: the rows (Type row) (`:189-191`, `:556`).
- Back → `AddSourceDraft` cleared, back to Add playlist (`:555`; `SetupWizard.kt:236`). A failed import brings the form back filled in (draft kept, password included only for that try) (`:484-489`, `:135-137`).
- Start: name blank → `setup_default_iptv` (Xtream) / `setup_name_default_playlist` (M3U); new sources auto-refresh daily (`MANUAL, 1`) (`SetupWizard.kt:211-222`; `AddSourceScreen.kt:168-172`).

##### Step 4d. Importing (no screenshot)

`ImportProgressStep` `SetupWizard.kt:743-819`, state from `SourceImporter`.
- Running: indicator 4; title `setup_importing_title` (with the name) or `setup_importing_catalog`; subtitle `setup_watching_during_import`. Body (padding 12 vertical, centred, gap 10): spinner, then primary progress text 24/700 Text (or `setup_preparing_catalog`), then detail 18/500 Muted (`:768-781`).
  - Spinner `OwnTVSpinner(sizeDp = 36)` in SkMateBlue: **36 dp** (= 64.8 mpx), 12 rounded spokes, the lit one stepping every 1000/12 ms, the others fading to a floor of 18 % (`A/ui/components/StateViews.kt:26-68`).

One row: icon EPG, `setup_add_guide`, line = `content_guide_loading` while the guide downloads / `setup_guide_attached` / `setup_add_guide_line`, chevron (`:802-808`, `:826-837`). One primary button `setup_continue` (520 wide, focused). Back = Continue.
  - OK on the guide row: finishes the wizard and opens Settings › EPG sources › Add (`:245`).
  - Screenshot 93 vs code: the row is shown focused (grey bar) although the code puts first focus on Continue (`rememberFirstFocus` on the button, `:799`). The screenshot was probably taken after pressing ▲. Initial focus = Continue.
- Failed: indicator 4; title `setup_import_failed`, subtitle = reason; buttons Back (cancel to origin) and primary `setup_try_again_caps` (focused) (`:811-817`).
