##### Backup restore branch (no screenshots)

- Chooser (`SetupWizard.kt:255-272`): indicator 3 (from setup choice) or 4 (from add playlist); title `setup_restore_a_backup`, subtitle `setup_restore_choice_description`; cards PHONE `setup_from_phone` / `setup_upload_from_wifi_device`, FOLDER `setup_local_file` / `setup_pick_backup_local`; Back → origin.
- From phone: `RemoteBackupRestoreScreen` (`A/features/settings/RemoteBackupRestoreScreen.kt`), title `settings_restore_remote_title`, body `settings_restore_remote_description`, QR/URL/PIN; Back → chooser.
- Local file: `StorageBrowser` titled `setup_pick_backup_file` for `.own`/`.json` (`SetupWizard.kt:724-731`).
- After a file is chosen: a section picker popup (`SectionPickerDialog`, `A/features/settings/BackupScreen.kt:599`) titled `settings_backup_what_restore`, all sections ticked, confirm `settings_backup_restore_action`, Cancel → origin (`SetupWizard.kt:300-318`).
- Then: Running → title `setup_restoring`; password needed → title `setup_enter_backup_password` / `setup_wrong_backup_password`, subtitle one of `setup_backup_passwords_encrypted_prompt`, `setup_backup_encrypted_prompt`, `setup_password_mismatch`, `setup_password_mismatch_sealed`, a password field `setup_backup_password`, buttons Back, `setup_skip_no_passwords` (not for a sealed file), primary `setup_restore` (enabled when typed); failed → `setup_restore_failed` + reason + Back (`:673-733`). A successful restore finishes the wizard with no profile chosen (the gate asks which) (`SetupViewModel.kt:241-244`).

##### Device sync branch (no screenshot)

`SetupLocalSyncScreen` `A/features/settings/LocalSyncScreen.kt:246-…`: a `WizardFrame` at indicator 3, title `setup_sync_device`, subtitle `local_sync_find_hint`, Back button `common_cancel`, primary `settings_backup_continue` (enabled when a manual address is typed). Discovery starts at once; rows: while none found REFRESH `local_sync_searching` / `setup_device_appear`; found devices (line = address or `local_sync_already_paired`); field `local_sync_manual_address_label` (placeholder `setup_device_address_hint`). Then PIN, sections, confirm, result `local_sync_done` + `local_sync_received_items` + `common_done`. On success the wizard ends like a restore.

#### 2a.5 What the wizard saves

| Step | Saved | Where (code) |
|---|---|---|
| Terms | `termsAcceptedVersion = 1` | `ShellViewModel.kt:190-192` |
| Welcome / terms | UI language = first-run language (if still system) | `LanguageSettingsScreen.kt:85-87`, `TermsOfUseScreen.kt:68-72` |
| Display size | `uiZoomPercent`, `fontCustomization.sizePercent`, `themeMode` (immediately) | `DisplaySizeViewModel.kt:38-64` |
| Profile | new profile: name, avatarId, isKids, PIN (hashed by core), custom picture; pending mark | `SetupViewModel.kt:123-130` |
| Playlist | a source (type, URL/server/user/password, name, auto-refresh daily; author playlist 6 h) | `SetupWizard.kt:190-232` |
| Finish | pending mark removed, new profile made active | `SetupViewModel.kt:269-273` |

#### 2a.6 Focus and key summary

| Screen | Initial focus | ◀ ▶ | ▲ ▼ | OK | Back |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Terms | Accept (150 ms) | Decline ↔ Accept | scroll text 160 | accept / close app | system (leaves app) |
| Welcome | Get started | Get started ↔ globe | — | next / language panel | none in wizard |
| Display size | zoom row | change value on rows; move in buttons | rows ↔ buttons | +step / cycle / flip; buttons | → Welcome |
| Setup choice | card 1 | cards | cards ↔ Back button | open | → Display size |
| Profile | Name field | avatar row changes avatar; buttons | rows ↔ buttons | edit / next avatar / chooser / flip | → Setup choice |
| Add playlist | card 1 | cards | cards ↔ Skip | open / skip | **nothing** |
| Type it here | Type row | Type row changes type; ▶ elsewhere = submit | rows | edit / pick file | → Add playlist |
| Importing | Run in background | buttons | — | | Cancel |
| All set | Continue | — | guide row ↔ Continue | | = Continue |


### 2b. Home / channel list

Reference screenshot: `01-kanaly.png`. Paths below are relative to `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/` (Android tree `skmate-android-main`, commit 3ad3636) unless they start with `engine/`.

**What draws this screen.** The screen on `01-kanaly.png` is **not** `features/home/HomeScreen.kt`. It is Live TV: `features/live/LiveScreen.kt` (`LiveScreen`, state, focus, layout, menus) + `features/live/LiveStage.kt` (header, row, preview pane, categories, skeletons), mounted by the shell for `MainSection.LIVE_TV` (`features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:1361-1383`). The clock is the shell's `StageStatusCluster` (`features/shell/components/StageStatus.kt`, placed at `OwnTVShell.kt:1636-1666`). From `features/home/` only the text helpers `timeLeftText`/`durationText` are used (`features/home/HomeRowText.kt:51-61`).

**The "TV-box look" switch.** Almost every value below depends on `TvBoxNav`, which is a compile-time constant `true` (`features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:2609`). Only the `TvBoxNav == true` branch is specified here; the other branch is dead code on the current build. Default layout is `LiveLayout.STAGE` (not `SEPARATE`) (`engine/core/.../settings/SettingsRepository.kt:1394`), so the categories are a slide-in sheet, not a permanent column.

> **Scale warning (code vs screenshots).** The design is "matched at UI Zoom 90%" (`ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:18-21`), but the default zoom is **100%** (`engine/core/.../theme/UiZoom.kt:11`). Measured on `01-kanaly.png`: all fixed sizes are **x1.111** the mpx value (toolbar top 118 mpx -> y=131 px; toolbar height 48 -> 53; row pitch 92+8=100 -> ~111; preview top 120 -> 133), while horizontal layout is a fraction of the 1920 width (`fx()`, `LiveScreen.kt:665`) and matches 1:1 (list 28..946, preview 980..1892). So the screenshots are ~11% larger vertically/in type than "1 mpx = 1 px". Decide globally (P1) whether the web uses 1 mpx = 1 px (code, 90% zoom) or 1 mpx = 1.111 px (screenshots, default zoom). The numbers below are mpx as in code.

---

#### 2b.1 Page and layout grid

| Item | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Page background (dark) | `#232629` (`TvBoxMenuFill`, same as side menu) | `OwnTVShell.kt:1067-1072`, `features/shell/components/StageRail.kt:540` |
| Page background (light) | palette `page` `#FBFAF9` | `OwnTVShell.kt:1071`, `ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:126` |
| `fx(px)` | `screenW * px / 1920` (horizontal geometry is proportional) | `LiveScreen.kt:664-665` |
| Side margin | `fx(28)` | `LiveScreen.kt:667` |
| Row (usable width) | `1920 - 2*28 = 1864` | `LiveScreen.kt:668` |
| Preview pane width `stageW` | `fx(912)` | `LiveScreen.kt:695` |
| Gap list -> preview `gapStage` | `fx(34)` | `LiveScreen.kt:677` |
| List width `listW` | `row - stageW - gapStage = 1864-912-34 = 918` | `LiveScreen.kt:697` |
| List x | `margin = 28` | `LiveScreen.kt:699` |
| Preview x | `screenW - margin - stageW = 980` | `LiveScreen.kt:1039` |
| Categories sheet width `sheetW` | `fx(450)` | `LiveScreen.kt:694` |
| Preview hidden (Settings > panel widths, preview 0%) | list takes `row` width | `LiveScreen.kt:669,700` |
| User panel widths (Stage) | sheet = `screenW*sheet%`; list = `(row-gapStage)*list%`; preview = rest | `LiveScreen.kt:685-691` |

Vertical anchors (mpx): title top 28; toolbar top 118; preview top 120; list starts at toolbar bottom + 20 (`Spacer 20`, `LiveScreen.kt:880`) = 186.

#### 2b.2 Header: title

- Text `live_screen_title` («Ваши каналы»), `stageText(46, 800, letterSpacing -1)`, colour `StageColors.Text` (dark `#F1F2F3`, light `#121212`), 1 line, ellipsis. `LiveStage.kt:119`.
- Position: x = `fx(48)`, top = 28 mpx; width = `listX - fx(48) + listWidth` (ends at the list's right edge). `LiveScreen.kt:755-761`.
- TV-box look shows **only the title** — the "‹ category · N channels" crumb is not drawn (`LiveStage.kt:120-121`). Code vs screenshot: matches (no crumb).

#### 2b.3 Header: clock, date, weather (top-right cluster)

- Cluster: `Row` aligned TopEnd, `padding(end = 64, top = 34)`, spacing 22. `StageStatus.kt:81-86`. In TV-box look the pills (Continue, playlist) are **not drawn**, only the clock (`StageStatus.kt:87-88`).
- Hidden while Settings/More sheet is open (`OwnTVShell.kt:1634-1635`).
- Layout (TV-box): `Row(spacing 12, centerVertically)`: [weather icon 42x42] [Column(date, weather line), left-aligned] [Spacer 6] [time]. `StageStatus.kt:190-205`.
  - Date: `DateFormat.getBestDateTimePattern(locale, "EEEdMMM")` -> ru «ср, 25 нояб.», `stageText(19, 600)`, colour `clockColors.date` default `Muted` (`#A9ADB2`). `StageStatus.kt:139,146-148,196-197`.
  - Weather line: `common_weather_degrees_city` («9° Tashkent»), same 19/600, colour default `Muted`; °F if setting. `StageStatus.kt:198-201`.
  - Time: system time format (24h in ru: «13:54»), `stageText(40, 700, letterSpacing -0.5)`, `fontFeatureSettings "tnum"`, lineHeight 40, colour default `Text`. `StageStatus.kt:138,145,204`.
  - Each colour is user-configurable (blank = default, "accent" = accent, or hex). `StageStatus.kt:124-130`.
  - Dark: text shadow black 60%, offset y 1 mpx, blur 3 mpx; plus a soft oval glow behind the block (radial black 34% -> 16% at 0.55 -> 0, radius = 1.2 x height, squashed horizontally to 0.8 x width). Light + TV-box: no shadow, no glow. `StageStatus.kt:154-188`.
  - Weather icon: canvas-drawn sun/moon/cloud/rain/snow/fog/thunder, sky colour follows hour of day (`features/shell/components/WeatherConditionIcon.kt:19-40`). Moon `#DDF8FF` dark / `#8A9BC4` light, cloud `#DDEFE9`, rain `#76A7FF`, snow `#F0FCFF`, thunder `#FFD166` (`WeatherConditionIcon.kt:33-36`). Web: SVG set with the same colours is enough.
- Update: re-read time every 15 s (`StageStatus.kt:144`).
- Animation: every changed character "rolls": new char slides in from below (+fade in), old slides out upward (+fade out), 420 ms, default tween easing (FastOutSlowIn), per character. `StageStatus.kt:243-266`. Applies to time, date and weather lines.
- Not focusable in TV-box look (pills absent; the clock is plain text).

#### 2b.4 Toolbar (search, «Поставщик», «Вид телегида», «…»)

Container: inside the list column (`padding(start = listX, top = 118)`, width `listWidth`) (`LiveScreen.kt:763-767`). Row: `spacedBy(8)`, `padding(start 4, end 4)`, `fillMaxWidth`, `CenterVertically`, own focus group (`LiveScreen.kt:825-834`). Focus entering the toolbar clears the pending preview switch (`LiveScreen.kt:832`).

All four controls are 48 mpx high, corner radius 4 mpx (`FocusBarShape`, `ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:254`), idle fill `ControlFill` = ink 6% (dark: white 6% on #232629; light: #121212 6%) (`ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:79`).

**a) Search field** — `StageSearchField` (`ui/stage/StageControls.kt:67-192`), `weight(1f)` (takes all remaining width) (`LiveScreen.kt:842-846`).
- Height 48, radius 4 (TV-box overrides `radius`) (`StageControls.kt:73,98`), horizontal padding 18, inner gap 12 (`StageControls.kt:133-134`).
- Icon `SEARCH` 20x20; idle colour `Dim` (`#6E7378`), focused `#121212` (`StageControls.kt:137,284`).
- Placeholder: `content_search_in` with the current category name («Поиск: Все каналы») (`LiveScreen.kt:839`), `stageText(18, 400)`, idle `Dim`, focused `#121212` at 60% (`StageControls.kt:140`).
- Typed text `stageText(18, 500)`, caret `#121212` when lit (`StageControls.kt:178-181`).
- Focus ("lit" = focused or editing) with `LocalWhiteFocus = true` (provided by LiveScreen, `LiveScreen.kt:657`): fill **pure white** `#FFFFFF` + shadow elevation 3 mpx colour `#33000000`, no ring (`StageControls.kt:100-125`, `StageComponents.kt:260`). Same in dark and light.
- OK = start editing (shows the TV keyboard, scrolls field into view after 120 ms) (`StageControls.kt:102-113,129`). Back while editing: stop editing, keep text, focus stays on the field (`StageControls.kt:153-160`). ▲/▼ while editing: stop editing, hide keyboard and move focus up/down (`StageControls.kt:164-174`). IME "Search": stop editing (`StageControls.kt:183-187`).
- The filter is live (every character) through `vm.setSearchQuery` (`LiveScreen.kt:837`).
- ◀ (not editing): opens the categories sheet (`LiveScreen.kt:852-860`). While editing ◀/▶ move the text cursor.
- The preview keeps playing while the search is used (TV-box) (`LiveScreen.kt:849-850`).
- If the screen opened while the list was loading and focus sat on the search, the first rows arriving move focus to the list (`LiveScreen.kt:553-588`).

**b) «Поставщик» (sort)** — `LiveSortTool` -> `StageTool(text=null, icon=SORT, value=<sort name>, boxed=true)` (`features/live/LiveSortMenu.kt:39-44`, `LiveScreen.kt:862-867`).
- Label: `content_epg_sort_provider` («Поставщик») when mode is PLAYLIST, otherwise the sort's short name (`LiveSortMenu.kt:23-29,42`).
- StageTool geometry: height 48, padding h 16, gap 9, icon 20, text `stageText(18, 700)` (`StageComponents.kt:467,476-484`). Idle colours: icon `Muted` `#A9ADB2`, value `Text` `#F1F2F3` (`StageComponents.kt:480-483`).
- OK -> sort menu (below).

**c) «Вид телегида»** — `StageTool(text = content_live_guide_view, icon = GRID, boxed = true)` (`LiveScreen.kt:868-873`). Idle: icon and text `Muted` (`StageComponents.kt:480-482`). OK -> `liveVm.setLiveView(GUIDE)`: the in-place guide replaces the list (`OwnTVShell.kt:1375`, `OwnTVShell.kt:1361-1364`) — see 2e (guide). Code vs screenshot: in the screenshot «Вид телегида» looks nearly as bright as «Поставщик»; code says the text part is `Muted`, the value part `Text`.

**d) «…» (more)** — `LiveMoreTool` -> `StageTool(text=null, icon=MORE, boxed=true)`; width = 16+20+16 = 52 (`LiveSortMenu.kt:65-69`). OK -> more menu.

**Tool focus look** (TV-box, FILLED style): grey bar `#E0E2E4` (dark) / `#E7E8EA` (light), radius 4, no scale, no shadow (`StageComponents.kt:194-198,209-210,263-266`), plus a darker overlay `#121212` at 9% on top because the tool is boxed (`StageComponents.kt:470-474`). Content switches to dark ink `#121212` (`StageComponents.kt:228-241,480`). Change is instant (no animation; `stageLift` scale is 1.0 for FILLED, `StageComponents.kt:268-286`).

**D-pad in the toolbar.** ◀/▶ move between the four controls; ◀ on the search opens categories (above); ◀/▶ never leave the pane (`LiveScreen.kt:813-822`). ▼ goes to the list; entering the list pane lands on the current channel row, never the first row by geometry (`LiveScreen.kt:806-811`). ▲ from the toolbar is cancelled (focus stays) (`LiveScreen.kt:818`).

##### Menus opened from the toolbar

All three use `GuideMenuHost` (`features/epg/GuideStage.kt:310-329`): a popup window, scrim `StageColors.Scrim` (dark rgb(4,5,6) 55%, light rgb(18,18,18) 30%; `StageTokens.kt:106,136`), card **centred** on screen (TV-box ignores the x/top args), max height = screen − 96 mpx, width capped at screen − 64. Back closes. Focus trapped inside.
- Card = `StageMenu` (`StageComponents.kt:690-702`): solid card radius 28 (`StageRadii.Menu`), fill `#232629` dark / `#FFFFFF` light, drop shadow black 50% (light 16%) blur 40 mpx, y +14 (`StageComponents.kt:1328-1335`); padding start 16, end 16, top 22, bottom 16; scrolls inside when long.
- Header `StageMenuHeader`: padding 12/12/bottom 12; title `stageText(23, 800)` `Text`; subtitle (2 mpx below) `stageText(15, 500)` `Muted` (`StageComponents.kt:708-725`).
- Item `StageMenuItem` (TV-box): height 76, hairline at the bottom `Text` 8% inset 14 each side; padding h 14, gap 14; icon 28 `Muted`; text `stageText(23, 600)` `MenuItemText` (`#DDE0E3` dark), marquee when focused; value `stageText(17, 700)` `Dim`, focused `#0086FC` (SkMateBlue); check icon 22 in accent when `checked` (`StageComponents.kt:751-804,1368-1372`). Focus = grey bar as above.
- Closing returns focus to the tool that opened it (`LiveScreen.kt:1298,1317,1338`).

1. **Sort menu** (`LiveSortMenu.kt:46-63`), width 560. Header `live_sort_title`, subtitle `content_sort_applies_hint`. Items in order (`engine/core/.../live/LiveSort.kt:9-11`): PLAYLIST `content_sort_provider_order` · ALPHA `settings_sort_alpha` · ALPHA_DESC `live_sort_alpha_desc` · NUMBER `live_sort_number` · RECENT `live_sort_recent`; each with icon `SORT`; the current one has a check and no value and gets initial focus; the others show their hint (`*_hint`) as value. OK = apply + close.
2. **More menu** (`LiveSortMenu.kt:71-99`), width 760. Header `live_more_title`. Items: `live_fix_guide` (icon `SPARKLE`, value `live_fix_guide_hint` or `live_fix_guide_running`; initial focus) -> toast `live_fix_guide_running`, then `live_fix_guide_done` (plural) / `live_fix_guide_none` for 5 s (`LiveScreen.kt:1302-1318`); `live_cleanup` (icon `LAYERS`, value `live_cleanup_hint`) -> if duplicates: confirm popup `live_cleanup_title`/`live_cleanup_body`/`live_cleanup_confirm`, focus on Cancel; if none: toast `live_cleanup_none` (`LiveScreen.kt:1320-1333`); then four `live_pick_now` items («Сейчас: %1$s» with `live_pick_sport`, `_movies`, `_news`, `_kids`), icon `LIVE_DOT`.
3. **Pick menu** ("On now: Sport") (`LiveSortMenu.kt:108-131`), width 760: header `live_pick_now` + `live_pick_hint`; while loading one item `live_pick_loading` (icon `CLOCK`); empty `live_pick_empty` (icon `INFO`); else one item per channel (text = channel name, value = programme title, icon `LIVE_DOT`); OK = watch full screen.

#### 2b.5 Channel list

Container: `LazyColumn`, `verticalArrangement spacedBy(8)`, `contentPadding(bottom 16)`, laid out 24 mpx wider on each side and shifted back so focus glows aren't clipped (net: rows span exactly the list width) (`LiveScreen.kt:903-926`). Item key = channel id, paged (`LiveScreen.kt:927-931`).

**Row anatomy** — `LiveStageRow` (`LiveStage.kt:145-218`) on `StageRow` (`StageComponents.kt:655-682`):

| Part | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Row height | **92** (TV-box) | `LiveStage.kt:169` |
| Row pitch | 92 + 8 gap = 100 | `LiveScreen.kt:913` |
| Padding h / gap between parts | 20 / 18 | `StageComponents.kt:660-661` |
| Hairline under each row | 1 mpx, `#14000000` (black 8%), inset 20 left/right, at the row bottom; omitted on weak TVs (`DevicePerf.lite`) | `LiveStage.kt:171-176,911-912` |
| Number | shown when "direct tune" setting is on (default **on**); width 50, **left-aligned** (TV-box), `stageText(20, 600)`, `tnum`, idle `Dim` `#6E7378`, focused `#343433` | `LiveStage.kt:178-187`, `features/live/LiveViewModel.kt:438`, `engine/core/.../SettingsRepository.kt:1685` |
| Logo plate | 76 x 54, radius 12 (`StageRadii.Plate`); logo `contain`-fit; fill only when no logo: `ControlFill` (ink 6%); no-logo fallback icon `LIVE_TV` at 0.78 x 54 = 42 mpx, `Dim` | `LiveStage.kt:188,254-272`, `ui/components/ChannelLogoTile.kt` |
| Logo plate for hard-to-read logos | logo ink measured; if contrast < 3:1 vs surface, plate `#EDF1F5` (on dark) / `#161C24` (on light); while loading a grey `Text` 6% placeholder (radius 10%) | `ChannelLogoTile.kt` (MIN_CONTRAST 3, LightPlate/DarkPlate, loading placeholder) |
| Name | `stageText(23, 700)`, 1 line; idle `Text`, focused `FxText` (`#121212` on the bar); ellipsis idle, **marquee** when focused (starts after 900 ms, repeats every 900 ms) | `LiveStage.kt:190,976-984`, `ui/components/FocusMarquee.kt:11-25` |
| Programme line | 2 mpx under the name; `stageText(18, 400)`; `"<title> · <осталось N мин>"`; title idle `Muted`, focused `#343433`; the " · time left" part same colour at 60% alpha; marquee when focused; shown only if a current programme title is known | `LiveStage.kt:191-203` |
| Time-left text | `home_time_left` with `player_duration_minutes` (<60) or `home_duration_hours_minutes`; minutes rounded **up** | `features/home/HomeRowText.kt:51-61`, `LiveStage.kt:283` |
| Row progress | 8 mpx under the text; height 4, full width of the text column; track ink 16%; fill = ink (`Text` colour: white-ish in dark idle, `#121212` on the focus bar), round caps (radius = h/2); weak TVs draw it only on the focused row (space kept) | `LiveStage.kt:957-974`, `ui/stage/StageControls.kt:254-280` |
| Trailing group | `Row(spacedBy 8)`: playing mark, provider tags, favourite heart | `LiveStage.kt:206-214` |
| Playing mark | the channel in the preview while preview is on: icon `PAUSE` filled, 30x30, `#0086FC`, start padding 6 | `LiveStage.kt:208`, `ui/components/PlayingMark.kt:11-12` |
| Provider tags | parsed from the name (e.g. "UK", "HD"): `stageText(13, 800, +0.05em)`, `TagText`, bg ink 8% (focused: black 18%), radius 6, padding 7x3 | `LiveStage.kt:209`, `StageComponents.kt:597-621` |
| Favourite | icon `FAVORITE` filled 22x22 in accent | `LiveStage.kt:212` |
| Archive badge «3 дня» | only if channel has catch-up; text `settings_epg_guide_days_value` plural of `catchupDays`, or `content_catchup` («Архив») if days unknown; box at row **top-right corner**, bg `#0086FC`, corners: top-right 4, bottom-left 10, others 0; padding 12 x 4; `stageText(14, 600)` white | `LiveStage.kt:164,216,220-281` |
| Catch-up ↺ icon / playlist marks | not drawn in TV-box look | `LiveStage.kt:210-213` |

Screenshot check: row 1 «Sky Sports News HD 50 UK» with blue «3 дня» at the top-right corner, progress bar, no pause mark — the preview was not actually playing on the emulator (pane shows the 288 mpx logo on black, so `effectivePreview` was off), which is why no playing mark is drawn. Row 2 shows no programme line and no bar (no guide). Matches code.

**Row focus look.** `StageRow` uses `StageFocus.FX`; in TV-box look this becomes the "sheet focus": a flat grey bar `#E0E2E4` (dark theme) / `#E7E8EA` (light), corner radius **4**, full row size, no scale, no ring, no shadow (`StageComponents.kt:190-212,253-266`). Inside the bar the content is re-themed to the light palette: name `#121212`, second line `#343433`, number `#343433`, progress fill `#121212` (`StageComponents.kt:227-241`, `LiveStage.kt:183,198-200`). Focus changes are instant (no transition). The hairline is still drawn under the focused row.

**Scrolling.** Edge scrolling: the list scrolls only as far as needed to keep the focused row plus **one row of room** visible (`edgeScrollSpec`, `LiveStage.kt:536-551`, applied `LiveScreen.kt:910`). ▲/▼ are handled by row index, not geometry: `stepTo(i)` scrolls by one row pitch when the target would be within one row of the edge, then focuses it; held keys chain from the pending target (`LiveScreen.kt:312-335,979-982`). ▲ on row 0 / ▼ on last row: not consumed by the row; the pane's `onExit` cancels vertical exit, but ▲ from row 0 reaches the toolbar via normal focus search (pane-internal) (`LiveScreen.kt:813-822`).

**Paging (CH+/CH−, ⏪/⏩).** Enabled by default (`SettingsRepository.kt:1998`). Short press: ±`skip` rows, default 10 (`SettingsRepository.kt:286`); long press (≥600 ms): first/last row (disabled on the «All channels» list) (`ui/components/ChNavPaging.kt:17-94`, `LiveScreen.kt:769-805`, defaults `engine/core/.../settings/RemoteShortcuts.kt:47-58`). Fires on key **up**. Jump = instant `scrollToItem` + focus + the preview switches to that channel immediately.

**Number keys.** No digit handling on this list; direct tune by number lives in the player (P5) (`player/DirectTune.kt`, `LiveViewModel.kt:1560`). The setting only controls whether numbers are shown.

**Row keys** (`LiveScreen.kt:957-999`):
- **OK**: watch full screen (`vm.watchFullscreen(channel, list)` then the shell opens the player; P5) (`LiveScreen.kt:995-998`).
- **Hold OK** (key auto-repeat, `repeatCount == 1`) or remote **Menu** key, or long-click: channel context menu (`LiveScreen.kt:959-970,999`).
- **◀**: open the categories sheet (2b.8).
- **▶**: focus the "next" programme under the preview (`scheduleFocus`) (`LiveScreen.kt:977-978`).
- **▲/▼**: step rows (above).

**Preview follows focus.** Focus resting **1000 ms** on a row (`PreviewDwellMs`) makes it the preview channel; passing over rows does not (`LiveScreen.kt:177-189,986-994,1776`). Focus on the toolbar cancels a pending switch (`LiveScreen.kt:768,832`). Returning from full screen: after 350 ms the preview re-takes the channel (`LiveScreen.kt:191-197,1783`), and focus returns to that channel's row (`LiveScreen.kt:463-479`).

**Remember position.** Setting "remember last item per category": on -> each category keeps its own scroll/channel; off -> list scrolls to top on category change (`LiveScreen.kt:234-244`).

**List appearance animation.** When a category's rows arrive: fade 0 -> 1 and rise from +14 dp (= 25.2 mpx) to 0, 240 ms, FastOutSlowIn (`LiveScreen.kt:884-889,915,1778-1780`).

**Now-playing refresh.** Programme lines refresh on each minute boundary (`LiveScreen.kt:161-169`).

#### 2b.6 Empty, loading and error states (list)

- Loading (no rows yet, first 2.5 s after a category change, no search): `ChannelListSkeleton` — 8 rows, each 84 high, padding h 20, gap 18: bar 40x16 r8, plate 76x54 r12, two bars 220x18 r9 and 300x12 r6 (gap 8); colour `Text` pulsing alpha 5% <-> 10%, 900 ms, reverse repeat (static 7.5% on weak TVs) (`LiveScreen.kt:882-893`, `LiveStage.kt:914-935`).
- Empty after that: `NoChannelsNote` centred in the list area: icon `LIVE_TV` 72, gap 14, text `stageText(22, 600)` `Muted`, centred (`LiveStage.kt:937-946`). Text: search active -> `content_no_channels_found` (with query); no live sources -> `live_no_playlist`; else `content_no_channels_here` (`LiveScreen.kt:890-895`).
- Background sync pill (shell, bottom-centre, while any sync runs, never over full-screen video): bg `#3A3E44`, radius 4, padding 24x12, max width 1100, 28 from bottom; each line spinner 14 dp + `stageText(16, 600)` `ItemText`; max 4 lines then `sync_status_more` (`features/shell/components/SyncStatusPill.kt:155-180,409,419`, `OwnTVShell.kt:1717-1721`).
- Key hints row (`StageKeyHints`) is **not drawn** in TV-box look (`ui/stage/StageControls.kt:200-202`).

#### 2b.7 Preview pane (right) — `LiveStagePane` (`LiveStage.kt:302-509`)

Position x 980, top 120, width 912 (`LiveScreen.kt:1025-1041`).

**Video card**
- 16:9 (912 x 513), radius **28**, background black, drop shadow black 55% (light: x0.15) blur 80, y +30 (`LiveStage.kt:354-371`).
- Inner ring: white 8%, 1 mpx, inside the radius (`LiveStage.kt:402`).
- Behind the video: channel logo 288x288 centred (fallback icon `LIVE_TV` 84 `Dim`) — this is what the screenshot shows (`LiveStage.kt:383-389`).
- Bottom shade: vertical gradient transparent at 58% -> black 72% at the bottom (only when a channel is set) (`LiveStage.kt:399-401`).
- **«ЭФИР» facts** (TV-box): top-left at (20, 18); row spacing 8; up to 4 facts: `player_live` + (when playing) stream facts except aspect ratio ("16:9") and bitrate ("… Mbps"); each `uppercase`, `stageText(14, 700)`, white 92%, bg black 18%, **1 mpx white 70% outline**, radius 5, padding 8 x 3 (`LiveStage.kt:404-423`). Matches the screenshot ("ЭФИР" outlined chip). No red dot in TV-box look.
- Channel line bottom-left at (26, bottom 22), spacing 14: logo plate 64 x 46 (radius 12); column: number `stageText(15, 600)` `#CFD8D4`, name (parsed, without tags) `stageText(22, 700)` white (`LiveStage.kt:432-442`).
- Loading: skeleton card `#1A1C1F` with a diagonal light sweep (white 7%, band 0.8 x width) moving -1 -> 2 widths, 1300 ms linear, infinite (static on weak TVs) + white 85% spinner 36 dp centred (`LiveStage.kt:394-398,850-876`). The video surface fades in 0 -> 1 over 260 ms once the stream has played 250 ms (700 ms if no LOADING was seen) (`LiveStage.kt:328-353,951-955`).
- Single-stream provider blocks preview: centred note `content_preview_single_stream`, `stageText(15, 700)` white on black 70%, radius 8, padding 12 x 6 (`LiveStage.kt:444-450`).
- No channel at all (empty profile): card filled `#1E2125` (light `#EDEEF0`), centred column gap 12: icon `PLAY_CIRCLE` 44 `Dim` + `content_preview_pick_channel` `stageText(19, 600)` `Muted` (`LiveStage.kt:374-382`).
- Preview audio is muted by default (`SettingsRepository.kt:2481`); preview is on by default except on low-spec devices (`SettingsRepository.kt:2447-2451`).

**Programme info** (column `padding(start 4, end 4, top 34)` under the card) (`LiveStage.kt:456-479`):
- Title: `stageText(42, 800, letterSpacing -0.5)`, `Text`, max **2 lines**, ellipsis.
- Details line: `content_live_time_range_plain` («12:15 – 14:45») + categories + year + rating + length, joined with " · "; `stageText(19, 400)` `Muted`, 1 line; padding top 8, bottom 16 (`LiveStage.kt:290-300,463-467`).
- Progress row: `StageProgress` (height **6**, track ink 12%, fill horizontal gradient accent -> focus colour, round caps) weight 1 + gap 16 + time left `stageText(17, 400)` `tnum` `Muted` (`LiveStage.kt:468-471`, `StageControls.kt:258-280`). With the default INK accent the fill is `#F2F0ED` in dark (`engine/core/.../theme/Palette.kt:121-124`).
- Synopsis (if any): `stageText(19, 400)`, line height 19 x 1.55, colour `#CCD6D2` dark / `Body` light, max 4 lines, padding top 18 (`LiveStage.kt:472-478`).
- No programme yet: for 1.5 s a text skeleton (bars 70%x40 r10, 25%x16 r8, 100%x6 r3, 95%x14 r7, 80%x14 r7, gap 14, `Text` alpha pulsing 5% <-> 11% 900 ms) (`LiveStage.kt:453-460,878-896`); then `NoGuideCard`: `content_no_guide` `stageText(22, 700)` `Muted` + three bars (45%x16, 100%x14, 75%x14) at `Text` 6% (`LiveStage.kt:898-909`).

**Next list** (`LiveStage.kt:480-534`)
- Shown when there is a "next": column `padding(top 18)`, shifted `x -12`, full width, **max height 240**, scrolls vertically, spacing 2, own focus group.
- Items: next (accent time, `Text` title), then later (muted), then up to 6 more upcoming (muted) (`LiveStage.kt:480-505`).
- Item: `StageSurface` radius 12, full width; text `"HH:mm␣␣Title"` with the time **bold** + `tnum`; `stageText(20, 400)`; padding 12 x 6; 1 line ellipsis. Colours: next time = accent, next title = `Text`; later time + title = `Muted`; focused = grey bar (`#E0E2E4`, radius 4) with `#121212` text (`LiveStage.kt:511-534`). Matches screenshot (first row bright, rest grey).
- D-pad: entered by ▶ from a row; ▲/▼ move; ◀ on the **first** item returns to the channel row (`LiveStage.kt:496-502`, `LiveScreen.kt:1036`). OK -> the guide's programme menu (`GuideProgrammeMenu` with watch / play from start / reminder / record / match EPG / offset), title + subtitle "time range · number name" (`LiveScreen.kt:1082-1111`); its dismissal returns focus to the next list.

#### 2b.8 ◀ from the list: categories sheet (hand-off to the side menu)

(Details of the categories list itself and of the side menu belong to 2c; summarised here because the list reacts to it.)
- ◀ on a row or on the search field (not typing) -> `categoriesOpen = true` (`LiveScreen.kt:971-976,852-860`).
- Sheet: x 0, width `fx(450)`, full height; background `#232629` dark / `#EFF2F5` light (`TvBoxPanelLight`), 1 mpx hairline `#14000000` on its right edge, padding h 16 v 30 (`LiveStage.kt:693-702`, `StageRail.kt:558`). Title `content_category_browser_title` `stageText(26, 800)` (`LiveStage.kt:707-715`). Rows `StageSheetItem` 56 high (2-line names grow), icon 28, text `stageText(20, 600)`, count `stageText(16, 600)` `Dim`, hairline `#12000000` inset 16; selected = accent text/icon (no dot in TV-box) (`StageComponents.kt:806-880`). Search field only when > 20 entries (`LiveStage.kt:744-760,948-949`). Focus lands on the **open** category (`LiveStage.kt:726-738`).
- Animation: sheet slides in from the left (`-width -> 0`) 280 ms FastOutSlowIn; out 240 ms; the whole list+preview layer is **pushed right** by `sheetW` with the same 280 ms FastOutSlowIn (`LiveScreen.kt:733-753,1045-1056,1785-1786`).
- Dwell auto-open: resting on another category for 7 s starts a 3-2-1 countdown ring (1 s per step) and then opens it without OK (`LiveStage.kt:682-692`, `ui/components/DwellCountdown.kt:56-61`).
- OK on a category: select it, close the sheet, focus the previewed channel's row (`LiveScreen.kt:636-639,559-582`). ▶ from the sheet: close and focus the list (`LiveScreen.kt:1066`). Back in the sheet (focus in it): close, focus current row (`LiveScreen.kt:546`); with a search typed, Back first clears it (`LiveStage.kt:666-675`). Focus returning to the list closes the sheet (`LiveScreen.kt:746`). ◀ again from the sheet opens the side menu beside it (`TvBoxPanels.besideOpen`, `LiveScreen.kt:590-597`) — P4.
- CH± in the sheet moves the highlight only (`LiveScreen.kt:610-635`).

#### 2b.9 Back on this screen

- Focus in the list/toolbar/preview: shell Back handler; on the channels screen (TV-box) a first Back shows a notice `shell_press_back_again` for 2 s; a second Back within **2 s** exits the app (no dialog) (`OwnTVShell.kt:1049-1061,2613`).
- From any other section Back returns to Live TV (`OwnTVShell.kt:1053`).
- Menus/dialogs: Back closes them and focus goes back to the control/row that opened them (`LiveScreen.kt:336-462`).

#### 2b.10 Long-press channel menu — `ChannelContextMenu` (`LiveScreen.kt:1386-1511`)

- Popup over scrim, `StageMenu` width **640**, centred, vertical padding 40, focus trapped, Back closes, first item focused (`LiveScreen.kt:1425-1427,1474-1487`).
- Header: logo plate 70 x 50 + title `"<number> · <name>"` + subtitle `"<category> · <playlist>"` (`LiveScreen.kt:1488-1494,1195`).
- Group labels (uppercased): `content_menu_group_watch` / `_channel` / `_guide_data` / `_organise`, drawn when the group changes; style `stageText(12.5, 800, +0.13em)` `Dim`, padding 14/14/top 12/bottom 5 (`LiveScreen.kt:1468-1473,1495-1497`, `StageComponents.kt:731-745`).
- Items (default order; user-reorderable via Settings > long-press menus, `arranged(ContentMenu.LIVE, …)`): WATCH: favourite (`content_add_favourite`/`content_remove_favourite`, `FAVORITE`), catch-up (`content_catchup`, `REWIND`, value «N дней», only if catch-up), record (`recording_record`/`recording_stop`, `REC` filled), schedule (`recording_schedule`, `CLOCK`), add to multiview (`multiview_add_to`, `MULTIVIEW`, only if multiview on; toast `multiview_added`), play external (`content_play_external_short`, `EXTERNAL`). CHANNEL: rename (`content_rename`, `PENCIL`), logo (`content_channel_logo`, `IMAGE`), hide (`content_hide_channel`, `EYE_OFF`), remove from history (`content_remove_history`, `HISTORY`, only in History). GUIDE DATA: match EPG (`content_match_epg`, `EPG`, value `content_epg_match_linked`/`settings_auto`), EPG offset (`content_epg_time_offset`, `CLOCK`, value offset label or `content_epg_offset_global_short`). ORGANISE (only in folder/custom/favourites): move (`content_move`, `MOVE`), move to category (`content_move_to_category`, `FOLDER`), remove from category (`content_remove_from_category`, `CLOSE`, only in custom) (`LiveScreen.kt:1434-1467`).
- Sub-dialogs: rename/logo `TextInputDialog`; `CatchupDialog` (StagePopup, list of past programmes, `content_catchup_title`…); `EpgMatchDialog` (StageMenu 640, search field, list height 400, buttons 56 high 19 px); `EpgOffsetDialog` (StagePopup 760, ◀/▶ step 30 min from −12 h to +14 h, value `stageText(36, 800)` accent); `ScheduleRecordingDialog`; `MoveOrderOverlay` (`LiveScreen.kt:1112-1364,1513-1773`). These are shared dialogs; geometry of `StagePopup` is in P1 tokens.

#### 2b.11 Animation summary

| What | Type | Duration / easing | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Row / tool / search focus | instant swap of fill | 0 | `StageComponents.kt:203-214` |
| Name / programme overflow | marquee | start 900 ms, repeat delay 900 ms | `FocusMarquee.kt:11-25` |
| Category rows arriving | fade + rise 14 dp | 240 ms FastOutSlowIn | `LiveScreen.kt:884-889` |
| Categories sheet in / out | slide from left | 280 ms FastOutSlowIn / 240 ms | `LiveScreen.kt:1045-1056` |
| List + preview push | translateX by sheet width | 280 ms FastOutSlowIn | `LiveScreen.kt:735-753` |
| Clock digits | per-char slide up + fade | 420 ms | `StageStatus.kt:243-266` |
| Preview video appear | alpha 0 -> 1 | 260 ms (after 250/700 ms settle) | `LiveStage.kt:342,353` |
| Preview skeleton sweep | linear sweep | 1300 ms infinite | `LiveStage.kt:854-858` |
| Text / list skeleton pulse | alpha | 900 ms reverse infinite | `LiveStage.kt:881-887,917-921` |

All durations go through `MotionPrefs.dur()` / `slideMs()`: 0 when animations are Off, scaled by the user's speed factor otherwise (`ui/theme/MotionPrefs.kt:107-116`). Weak devices drop the infinite animations and hairlines (`DevicePerf.lite`).


### 2c. Side menu rail and categories column

Android tree: `C:\Users\darsaec\Projects\skmate-android-main` (origin/main, commit 3ad3636). All paths below are relative to `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/` unless they start with `engine/`. Screenshots: `02-menu.png` (menu + categories open), `01-kanaly.png` (both closed).

#### 2c.0 Read this first: which code path is live, and the screenshot scale

1. **Only the "TV-box" path matters.** `internal const val TvBoxNav = true` (`features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:2609`). Every `if (TvBoxNav) … else …` branch in the rail and the categories takes the TV-box side. The Navigation settings (Floating / Docked, Size Normal/Wide/Extra wide, Length, hide delay, edge glow) are **overridden** while it is on (`OwnTVShell.kt:2603-2607`, `OwnTVShell.kt:780`, `features/shell/components/StageRail.kt:161`, `:198`). The web app should implement only the TV-box rail described below; ignore `RailState.CAPSULE`, `PINNED`, the glass capsule, `RailBrand`/`Wordmark`, labels, counts and details (they are never drawn when `TvBoxNav`).
2. **Screenshot scale.** The screenshots were taken at UI Zoom 100% (the real default: `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/theme/UiZoom.kt:11` `DEFAULT = 100`) on a 2.0-density emulator, while `mpx` is defined as 1/1.8 dp (`ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:23`; the comment there saying "default UI Zoom of 90%" is out of date). So **in the screenshots 1 mpx = 1.111 px**. Measured proof on `02-menu.png`: rail strip 84 mpx -> hairline at x = 93 px; rail item pitch 58+6 = 64 mpx -> 71 px (icons at y = 387, 458, 529, 600, 671); avatar 46 mpx -> 51 px; logo height 30 mpx -> 33 px. **Exception:** geometry written with `fx(n) = screenW * n / 1920` (LiveScreen) is a fraction of the screen and matches screenshot pixels 1:1 (categories sheet `fx(450)` -> 450 px: x 93..543). Decide project-wide whether web `1 mpx` = 1 px (spec value) or 1.111 px (to match screenshots pixel for pixel); mixing breaks the alignment shown in `02-menu.png`.

#### 2c.1 States and overall geometry

| State | What is on screen | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Closed (default) | Nothing at the left edge. No capsule, no glow (`RailEdgeGlow` is skipped when TvBoxNav). Rail stays composed off-screen (translated by -(width+edge)) and **stays focusable**, so ◀ can reach it. | `StageRail.kt:80-82`, `:198`, `:208-214`; `OwnTVShell.kt:780` |
| Menu open (no categories) | 84 mpx full-height strip at x = 0, page pushed right by 84 mpx. No scrim. | `StageRail.kt:114`, `:162`, `:194-197`, `:200-206`; `OwnTVShell.kt:788-801`, `:1339` |
| Categories open (no menu) | 450 (fx) wide full-height sheet at x = 0; the channel list side (header, toolbar, list, preview) pushed right by the sheet width. | `features/live/LiveScreen.kt:694`, `:735-753`, `:1045-1078` |
| Categories + menu open (`02-menu.png`) | Rail strip 0..84 mpx, then categories sheet 450 (fx), then the pushed channel screen. Page content starts at 48 fx + 450 fx + 84 mpx (screenshot: "Ваши каналы" moves from x=48 to x=591). | as above, both pushes add up |

- Rail open state is `RailState.OPEN` only while the rail **holds focus that was sent there on purpose**: within `RAIL_KEY_WINDOW_MS = 400` ms of a ◀/▶ key or of `focusRail()`, or once any key is pressed while focus is on the rail (`OwnTVShell.kt:749-758`, `:1103-1108`, `:2601`). Otherwise `HIDDEN` (`OwnTVShell.kt:780`). A touch/tap beside the open menu closes it (`OwnTVShell.kt:774-777`, `:1668-1692`) — not needed on TV remotes but harmless.
- Whole-app background in the dark TV-box look is the menu grey `#232629` (`TvBoxMenuFill`), not the Stage page gradient (`OwnTVShell.kt:1067-1075`, `:1296`; `StageRail.kt:540`). Light theme: `StagePalette.Light.page` `#FBFAF9`.

For the web: copy the SVG `d` strings verbatim; Unicons paths use `viewBox="0 0 24 24"`, Phosphor `viewBox="0 0 256 256"`, both `fill=currentColor`.

#### 2c.5 Rail behaviour: keys, focus, Back, animation

Opening:
- ◀ in the content when the D-pad search finds nothing further left -> `focusRail()` (`OwnTVShell.kt:1340-1350`, `:166-170`). From the channel list, ◀ first opens the **categories** (2c.7); ◀ again from the categories finds nothing to the left -> rail opens beside them.
- Focus entering the rail from outside always lands on the **selected section's item** (`focusSection`), not on the geometrically nearest one: Settings/More -> MORE item; Search or a visible section -> itself; otherwise the first item (`StageRail.kt:171-176`, `:215-229`, `:326`). Re-pull at most every 300 ms (`StageRail.kt:561`).
- From the full-screen player, the player's ◀ chain shows a copy of the same rail over the picture; OK on another section exits the player first (`OwnTVShell.kt:1800-1812`) — details in 2d.

Inside the rail:
- ▲/▼ move between items (Compose focus search; nothing custom). The logo is not focusable; the avatar is reachable by ▼ after the last item.
- ▶ always leaves for the content: to the screen's own entry (`enterContent`), which on the channels screen means the **categories if they are open, else the current channel row (never the search field)** (`StageRail.kt:230-256`; `LiveScreen.kt:598-606`).
- OK on an item (`selectFromNav`, `OwnTVShell.kt:1235-1252`):
  - a different section: switch to it, menu closes, focus is put into the new screen (retries every 50 ms, up to 60 tries) (`OwnTVShell.kt:1249`, `:1013-1031`, `:2634-2635`);
  - the section already open (e.g. Home while on channels): if the categories are open beside the menu they close and focus goes to the current channel row; otherwise the menu just closes to the content (`OwnTVShell.kt:1249-1250`; `LiveScreen.kt:593-597`);
  - MORE (gear): the categories beside the menu close first, then the Settings sheet slides in from the right edge over the channels screen (300 ms, FastOutSlowIn, with alpha 0->1; channel preview keeps playing) (`OwnTVShell.kt:1246-1248`, `:1385-1459`, `:2616`, `:969-974`);
  - SEARCH: plain section switch (query cleared) (`OwnTVShell.kt:1236-1243`, `:1251`).
- Rail closes as soon as focus leaves it (state goes back to HIDDEN) (`OwnTVShell.kt:780`).

Back (`OwnTVShell.kt:1032-1065`, in priority order):
1. Full-screen player -> leave player (`:1034`).
2. Avatar picker / playlist picker / exit dialog open -> close it (`:1035-1037`).
3. Settings section -> back to More (`:1044`).
4. Came from a Search result -> back to Search (`:1046`).
5. **Focus in the rail -> close the menu, focus back to the screen's entry** (channels: the open categories, else the current row) (`:1048`, `:1000-1012`).
6. On the Guide (EPG) -> open the rail again (`:1052`).
7. On any other section than channels -> go to channels (`:1053`).
8. **On the channels screen: no dialog.** First Back shows toast `shell_press_back_again` for 2000 ms; a second Back within 2000 ms (`ExitBackWindowMs`) closes the app (`:1054-1061`, `:2613`).
- Long-press Back (600 ms) only toggles focus in and out of the mini player while one is docked (`OwnTVShell.kt:1077-1130`, `:818-822`).

Animation (all on the same 300 ms clock so rail and page move as one strip):

| What | Type | Duration / easing | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Rail slides in | `translationX` from -(84 mpx) to 0 (snap to -1 first, then animate) | `tween(MotionPrefs.ms(300), FastOutSlowInEasing)` | `StageRail.kt:181-190`, `:207-210` |
| Rail slides out | `translationX` 0 -> -(84 mpx), **alpha = 1 + progress** (fades while sliding) | same 300 ms FastOutSlowIn | `StageRail.kt:186-190`, `:211-213` |
| Page pushed right | content box `translationX` 0 -> 84 mpx and back | `tween(MotionPrefs.ms(MENU_PUSH_MS = 300), FastOutSlowInEasing)` | `OwnTVShell.kt:787-801`, `:1339`, `:2638` |
| Animations off / weak device | `snap()` (instant), push duration 0 | `MotionPrefs.smoothMenu` false | `StageRail.kt:185`; `OwnTVShell.kt:797`; `ui/theme/MotionPrefs.kt:30-37`, `:100-105` |
| Speed setting | multiplies durations: Fast 0.6, Normal 1.0, Slow 1.5 | `MotionPrefs.ms` | `ui/theme/MotionPrefs.kt:14`, `:110` |

FastOutSlowInEasing = CSS `cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)`.

Not in screenshot (11 entries). |

| Icon per entry | Favorites `FAVORITE` (heart, Phosphor), Recently watched `HISTORY` (Phosphor), Catch-up `REWIND` (Phosphor), All channels `LIVE_TV` (Unicons TV); every provider group also gets `LIVE_TV` in TV-box | `LiveStage.kt:578-584`, `:784`; `PhosphorIcons.kt:22`, `:36`, `:38`; `UniconsIcons.kt:30` |
| Name | 20/600; idle `StageColors.ItemText` (dark `#D7DADD`, light `#343433`); selected = accent colour (default INK dark `#F2F0ED`); focused = on-accent; up to 2 lines, ellipsis | `StageComponents.kt:852-860`; `StageTokens.kt:91`, `:122` |
| Tags (provider tags parsed from the group name, e.g. "HD") | on their own line under the name (2-line mode), gap 6 mpx vertical / 8 horizontal; `StageTag` 13/800 +0.05 em, padding 7×3, radius 6, fill `Ink` 8% | `StageComponents.kt:861-866`, `:596-621`; `LiveStage.kt:788-790` |
| Count | right side, 16/600, idle `StageColors.Dim`, focused on-accent; grouped by locale (`NumberFormat.getIntegerInstance()`, ru shows "1 689" with a no-break space) | `StageComponents.kt:877`; `LiveStage.kt:785`; `ui/components/CountFormat.kt:6` |
| Selected marker | TV-box: **no dot, no bar** — only accent-coloured text and icon | `StageComponents.kt:846-850` |
| Focused | grey bar `#E0E2E4` (dark) / `#E7E8EA` (light), radius 4 mpx, full row; text/icon `#121212` (light palette); no ring, shadow or scale | `StageComponents.kt:194-213`, `:228-241`; `LiveStage.kt:693` |

Entries and order (`features/live/LiveViewModel.kt:2446-2458`, `:785-795`; labels `features/live/LiveRailText.kt:11-18`, `LiveStage.kt:570-572`):

1. Избранное — `content_category_favorites`
2. Недавно просмотренные — `content_category_recently_watched` (the History key; the sheet overrides the `content_category_history` «История» label)
3. Архив — `content_catchup` — **only when the playlist has at least one catch-up channel** (`LiveViewModel.kt:764-766`, `:2453-2458`)
4. Все каналы — `content_category_all_channels`
5. heading «ГРУППЫ», then the provider groups (folders) and custom categories in the user's order (A–Z when sort = alphabetical; manually moved ones stay first; same-named groups of several playlists merged) (`LiveViewModel.kt:768-795`). Counts shown for every row, including 0 (screenshot: Избранное 0, Недавно просмотренные 0).

Screenshot check (`02-menu.png`, scale 1.111): title «Категории» left at x ≈ 127 = 93 + (16+14)×1.111; row icons at x ≈ 135; counts right-aligned at x ≈ 503 = 543 − (16+4+16)×1.111; row pitch 62 px = 56 mpx. «Все каналы» is the selected row (brighter text/icon), no focus bar visible in the sheet because focus is in the rail. Matches code.

#### 2c.7 Categories: keys, focus, Back, dwell, animation

Open:
- ◀ on a channel row -> `categoriesOpen = true` (not on a pinned/locked list) (`LiveScreen.kt:971-976`).
- ◀ on the channel search field when not typing -> same (`LiveScreen.kt:851-860`).
- After one frame focus goes into the sheet and lands on the **currently selected category** (scrolling to it if needed), never on a row merely browsed before (`LiveScreen.kt:547-548`; `LiveStage.kt:726-738`).

Inside:
- ▲/▼ move; vertical exits are trapped at both ends (`LiveStage.kt:739`).
- CH+/CH− page through categories, moving the highlight only (Settings › channel paging skips apply) (`LiveScreen.kt:610-635`; `LiveStage.kt:646-653`).
- OK = select the category: the list switches to it, the sheet closes, focus goes to the channel that is in the preview (else the first row) (`LiveScreen.kt:636-639`, `:558-582`).
- ▶ = close the sheet without changing the category, focus to the preview's channel row (`LiveStage.kt:678-680`; `LiveScreen.kt:1066`).
- ◀ = open the side menu beside the sheet (sheet stays open) (`LiveScreen.kt:620-625` comment; shell `OwnTVShell.kt:1340-1350`).
- Focus landing anywhere in the channel side while the sheet is open closes it (`LiveScreen.kt:746`).
- **Dwell auto-open**: focus resting on a category other than the selected one for 7000 ms starts a 3-2-1 countdown (1000 ms per step); the count text is replaced by a `CountdownRing` (30 mpx, 2.5 mpx stroke, track `#0086FC` 20%, arc `#0086FC` with round cap filling clockwise from 12 o'clock over 3000 ms, number 15/800 `#0086FC`); at 0 the category opens as if OK was pressed. Paused while OK is held or a context menu/move window is open; any focus move restarts it (`LiveStage.kt:682-692`, `:775-791`; `ui/components/DwellCountdown.kt:28-50`, `:56-59`; `ui/components/CountdownRing.kt:29-49`; `ui/brand/SkMateWordmark.kt:16`).
- Long press OK (or held OK) on a **provider group or custom category** opens the category menu (2c.8). Favorites / Recently watched / Archive / All channels have no menu (`LiveScreen.kt:640-647`).

Back:
- With a category search typed: clears it and returns focus to the search field (`LiveStage.kt:666-675`).
- Focus in the sheet: closes the sheet, focus to the current channel row (`LiveScreen.kt:544-546`).
- Focus in the menu beside the sheet: first Back closes the **menu** only, focus returns to the sheet; second Back closes the sheet (`LiveScreen.kt:545-546`, `:598-604`; `OwnTVShell.kt:1048`).

Animation:

| What | Type | Duration / easing | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Sheet in | slide from x = −(own width) to 0 | `tween(slideMs(SHEET_SLIDE_MS = 280), FastOutSlowInEasing)` | `LiveScreen.kt:1045-1052`, `:1785` |
| Sheet out | slide to −(own width) | `tween(slideMs(SHEET_HIDE_MS = 240))` (Compose tween default easing = FastOutSlowIn) | `LiveScreen.kt:1053-1055`, `:1786` |
| Channel side pushed | `translationX` 0 -> sheetW and back (no dimming in TV-box) | `tween(slideMs(280), FastOutSlowInEasing)` both ways | `LiveScreen.kt:733-753` |
| Animations off | 0 ms | `MotionPrefs.slideMs` -> `dur` | `ui/theme/MotionPrefs.kt:113-116` |

The rail opening beside the sheet then pushes the whole content (sheet included) a further 84 mpx (2c.5).

#### 2c.8 Category long-press menu, restore confirmation, move window

`CategoryContextMenu` (`features/shell/components/CategoryContextMenu.kt:36-100`), opened from `LiveScreen.kt:1366-1383`:

| Property | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Backdrop | full-screen `StageColors.Scrim` (dark rgb(4,5,6) 55%; light rgb(18,18,18) 30%) | `CategoryContextMenu.kt:61-63`; `StageTokens.kt:106`, `:136` |
| Card | centred, width 520 mpx, max height screen − 64 dp; solid card radius 28 mpx, fill `#232629` dark / `#FFFFFF` light, shadow black 50% (light 16%) blur 40 mpx dy 14; padding start 16, end 16, top 22, bottom 16 | `CategoryContextMenu.kt:65`; `ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:690-706`, `:1328-1335`; `StageTokens.kt:218` |
| Header | category name, 23/800 `Text`, 1 line; padding 12/12, bottom 12 | `ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:708-725` |
| Items (TV-box) | 76 mpx high, text 23/600 `MenuItemText` (dark `#DDE0E3`), icon 28 mpx `Muted`, padding 14, gap 14, hairline under each row 1 mpx `Text` 8% inset 14; focus = grey bar as above | `ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:747-805`, `:1368-1369` |
| Items, in order | **Переместить** (`content_move`, icon `MOVE`) · **Скрыть** (`common_hide`, `EYE_OFF`) · **Вернуть по умолчанию** (`content_category_restore_custom` for a custom category, `content_category_restore_playlist` for a playlist group; icon `REFRESH`) · **Отмена** (`common_cancel`, `CLOSE`) | `CategoryContextMenu.kt:67-86`; `LiveScreen.kt:1370-1381` |
| Focus | first item gets focus on open; focus trapped inside; Back = close | `CategoryContextMenu.kt:53-58`, `:65` |

Restore asks first (`RestoreConfirmPopup`, `CategoryContextMenu.kt:102-173`): same card; text `content_category_restore_question` («Что произойдёт:») or `content_category_restore_nothing`, 18/600 `MenuItemText`, padding 14/14/8/12; then one line per change (6 mpx muted dot + 17/500 `Muted`, padding start 22, bottom 10) built from plurals `content_category_restore_hidden`, `content_category_restore_moved` and `content_category_restore_order` (`engine/.../core/customize` `categoryRestoreSummary`); 18 mpx gap; buttons: restore label (only if there is something to undo) and «Отмена». Focus goes to the first button.

«Переместить» opens `MoveOrderOverlay` (`ui/components/MoveOrderOverlay.kt:40-122`): title `content_move`, 760 mpx wide, rows spaced 6 mpx, ▲/▼ carry the picked row, OK picks up / puts down, CH± move a page, only «Сохранить» (`common_save`, tinted) or «Отмена» (`common_cancel`) close it; buttons 56 mpx high, 19 px text. Hint text `sports_move_instructions`. (Only the shell of this overlay was read; read the file for the row look.)

#### 2c.9 Things in the brief that do not exist in the TV-box build

- **Labels, counts, profile name, "· сменить" line, brand wordmark in the rail**: code exists for the non-TV-box rail (`StageRail.kt:370-389`, `:451-478`, `:503-516`) but is never drawn while `TvBoxNav = true`. Screenshot agrees (icons only).
- **Home, Movies, Series, Downloads** destinations: hidden; selecting them is redirected to Live TV (`StageRail.kt:543`; `OwnTVShell.kt:965-968`).
- **"Ещё" (More) page**: in TV-box the gear opens the More/Settings sheet over the channels screen (described in 2f); `features/more/MoreScreen.kt` is that sheet's content.
- **Exit-app confirmation dialog** (`showExit`) is only for the non-TV-box path (`OwnTVShell.kt:1062`); TV-box uses "press Back again" (2c.5).
- **Settings › Navigation (Floating/Docked, size, length, hide delay)** and **Menu items (Dynamic/Static)**: only the Menu-items visibility rule still affects the TV-box rail (which of Sport / Guide show) (`features/settings/NavigationSettingsPopup.kt:35-41`; `engine/.../core/nav/NavVisibility.kt:38-47`).
- `features/settings/ContentMenuSettingsScreen.kt:63-72` is "Long-press menus" (order of actions in content menus), **not** the side-menu item order. The side-menu order is fixed in code.


### 2d. Player with panels

No screenshot of the player exists in the archive, so every fact here comes from code (Android `main`, commit `3ad3636`). Paths are shortened: **`A/`** = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/`, **`C/`** = `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/`, **`P/`** = `engine/player-core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/player/`.

**Units.** Most TV-box player parts are in `mpx` (1 mpx = 1 px on the 1920×1080 stage). Several older player parts are still written in **`dp`**, not `mpx`. On the reference TV 1 dp = 1.8 mpx (`A/ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:23-26`: `DP_PER_MPX = 1/1.8`). So **px = dp × 1.8**. Both values are given below as `NN dp (= MM px)`. Text sizes from `stageText(size, weight)` are always mpx (`StageTokens.kt:251-257`).

**Theme.** The player always draws in the **dark** Stage palette, whatever the app theme is (`A/features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:1813-1815`: `LocalStagePalette provides StagePalette.Dark`). So in the player `hudInk` = `#FFFFFF`, `hudShade(a)` = black at alpha `a`, and `hudAccent` = the accent tuned for dark surfaces (`A/ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:187-203`). The brand blue used across the TV-box player is `SkMateBlue = #0086FC` (`A/ui/brand/SkMateWordmark.kt:16`).

**Which HUD is used.** `TvBoxNav` is a hard constant `true` (`A/features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:2609`). The live player therefore always uses the **TV-box steps** (info strip → timeline → tile row). This applies when the item is a tuned Live TV channel and not a catch-up programme (`A/player/PlayerHud.kt:311`: `tvLive = TvBoxNav && isLive && tvBoxInfo != null`; `tvBoxInfo` is non-null only when `!catchupActive`, `OwnTVShell.kt:2113`). Catch-up programmes, movies and episodes use the **classic HUD** (top bar, centre transport, bottom dock), described in §2d.9.

---

#### 2d.1 Opening and closing full screen (Live TV)

| What | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Grow out of the channels screen's preview into full screen | Layout lerp from `TvBoxPanels.previewRect` to the full screen. **320 ms × speed**, FastOutSlowIn. 0 ms when animations are off. | `OwnTVShell.kt:1824-1835`, `:2619` |
| Shrink back on Back (only on the Live TV screen, when a preview rect exists) | **260 ms × speed**, FastOutSlowIn, then the player closes | `OwnTVShell.kt:638-641`, `:1836-1840`, `:2620` |
| From Search / Sports | No shrink; closes at once | `OwnTVShell.kt:637-641` |
| First picture of a channel | Black cover. Fade starts **250 ms** after the engine reports PLAYING, and fades over **320 ms** (default tween = FastOutSlowIn) | `OwnTVShell.kt:1900-1925`, `:2641-2644` |
| HUD during grow | alpha = `((grow − 0.7) / 0.3)` clamped 0..1. alpha = 0 while shrinking | `OwnTVShell.kt:2168` |
| Speed factor | `MotionPrefs.Speed`: FAST 0.6, NORMAL 1.0, SLOW 1.5. `dur()` returns 0 when animations are off or the device is throttled | `A/ui/theme/MotionPrefs.kt:14`, `:110-116` |
| HUD on open | `controlsVisible` starts `true`, so the info strip shows on every channel opened and auto-hides | `PlayerHud.kt:309` |

---

#### 2d.2 TV-box info strip (step 1: OK or a zap shows it)

**Container** (`A/player/PlayerHud.kt:857-917`):
- Enter: fade + slide-in from below by 1/6 of its height. Exit: the reverse. **220 ms** (`TvHudFadeMs`), default easing FastOutSlowIn, scaled by `MotionPrefs.dur` (`PlayerHud.kt:859-862`, `:1255`).
- Bottom shade: a full-width box anchored bottom, height **420 dp (= 756 px)**, or **560 dp (= 1008 px)** while the tile row is open. Vertical gradient: 0.0 transparent → 0.5 `#B3000000` at 0.35 alpha → 1.0 `#B3000000` (`PlayerHud.kt:869-870`; `TvBoxShade = #B3000000`, `A/player/TvBoxLiveHud.kt:106`).
- Content column anchored bottom-start: padding start/end **44 dp (= 79.2 px)**, bottom **36 dp (= 64.8 px)**, gap **22 dp (= 39.6 px)** between the info and the tile row (`PlayerHud.kt:871-874`).
- With the info up and no tiles, focus sits on an invisible 1-mpx focus holder (`TvBoxInfoFocus`, `TvBoxLiveHud.kt:110-112`; used at `PlayerHud.kt:914`).

**Info anatomy** (`A/features/shell/components/TvBoxPlayerInfo.kt`, all mpx):

| Element | Spec | Line |
|---|---|---|
| Outer column | gap 18 between the info row and the timeline | :53 |
| Info row | gap 24, vertically centred | :54 |
| Logo | 132×132 box, inner padding 6, no plate (transparent fill). Fallback: `OwnTVIcon.LIVE_TV` 56 px, white at 0.6 | :55-60 |
| Text column | gap 8 | :61 |
| Programme title | 34/700, white, 1 line, ellipsis. With no guide: `content_no_guide` in the same style | :62-69 |
| Line 2 (gap 16) | time range `content_live_time_range_plain` 20/500, Soft = white 0.8. Then a mini bar 64×4 (track white 0.3, fill white, round ends). Then minutes left `player_duration_minutes` 20/500 Soft. Then channel number + name (gap 8) 20/700 white. Then up to **4** stream chips | :70-84, :111-121, :204 |
| Stream chip | text UPPERCASE 14/700, white 0.85, 1-mpx border white 0.45, radius 5, padding h7 v2 (e.g. "FHD", "25 FPS", "STEREO") | :123-130 |
| Line 3 (next) | time range + title, 20/500, Faint = white 0.62, gap 16 | :85-90, :203 |
| Timeline | full width, 24 high | :96-103, :136-153 |

**Timeline** (`TvBoxPlayerInfo.kt:136-153`):
- Track: height 4 (8 while on the timeline step), track white 0.25 (0.35 on the step), fill `SkMateBlue` up to the watched moment.
- Dot: radius 7 (12 on the step). Colour white, or `SkMateBlue` when behind live and not on the step. On the step there is also a halo: `SkMateBlue` 0.45, radius dot + 5.
- Progress is measured at the *watched* moment (clock − timeshift offset), so in the archive the dot is behind (`TvBoxPlayerInfo.kt:50`, `:93-95`). In the archive the info shows the archive programme, not the live one (`OwnTVShell.kt:2117-2124`).
- **Seek counter** while ◀ ▶ move through the archive: a white pill, radius 10, padding h14 v6, text 22/700 black. Format `player_seek_counter` = "−1:20 · 13:42:10" (signed offset with U+2212 minus, then the landing time with seconds). Placed above the dot with a 10 px gap, centred on the dot, clamped inside the bar, takes no layout height (`TvBoxPlayerInfo.kt:155-200`). It disappears **1.5 s** after the last press (`PlayerHud.kt:320-327`, `:1305`).
- The info data refreshes on a **1 s** clock (`OwnTVShell.kt:2116`).

**Auto-hide** (`PlayerHud.kt:502-511`):
- **4.5 s** after the last key on the info step. **8 s** (`TV_BOX_BUSY_HIDE_MS`) on the timeline or tile steps (`:1311`).
- Every key that reaches the HUD while it is shown restarts the timer (`wakeTick++`, `:794`).
- While paused it never hides: it waits until playing or buffering again (`:508`).
- It never hides while a panel/overlay is on top (`inert`) or a dialog/error is up (`forceShow`).

**Pause mark** (live, TV-box): a circle 96 dp (= 172.8 px), fill `#66000000`, centred, with `OwnTVIcon.PAUSE` 44 dp (= 79.2 px) white. Shown when the user paused a stream that had started, with no error and no panel (`PlayerHud.kt:850-856`).

---

#### 2d.3 TV-box tile row (step 3: ▼ from the info, or ▼ again from the timeline)

`A/player/TvBoxLiveHud.kt:37-103`, list built at `A/player/PlayerHud.kt:883-912`.

- Row: full width, `Arrangement.SpaceBetween`, each tile `weight(1f)`, so the tiles spread evenly across the strip.
- Tile: a column (gap 10): a glyph disc 64×64, then the caption.
  - Disc: transparent when idle, **white circle** when focused.
  - Icon: 32×32. Tint `#16181D` when focused, `SkMateBlue` when active, white 0.86 otherwise. Filled glyph when active.
  - Caption: 18 px, weight 500 (700 when focused). Colour white 0.78 (white when focused). 1 line, centred, padding h4.
- **OK acts on key-up**, and only if the key-down also happened on that tile (`:55-74`).
- The row's ends are walls: ◀ on the first tile and ▶ on the last are consumed (`:76-80`).
- When the row opens, focus goes to the **first tile** (`PlayerHud.kt:355-359`).

**Tile order** (each shown only when its callback exists; `PlayerHud.kt:884-910`):

| # | Label key | Icon | Condition | Action |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `settings_seg_guide` | EPG | live tuned channel | hide HUD, open the guide over the picture (§2d.6) |
| 2 | `content_channel_overlay_title` | LIST | `isTunedLive && liveCanZap` | hide HUD, open the channel panel |
| 3 | `content_category_history` | HISTORY | tuned live | hide HUD, open the history panel |
| 4 | `player_previous_channel` | SKIP_PREVIOUS | a previous channel exists | hide HUD, go to the previous channel |
| 5 | `player_tool_favorite` / `content_favorited` when on | STAR / FAVORITE (active, blue) | there is a favourite context | toggle favourite |
| 6 | `recording_record` / `recording_stop` | REC (active while recording) | "record what I'm watching" setting on, or an archive is on screen | start/stop recording |
| 7 | `player_tool_audio` | AUDIO | more than 1 audio track | Audio dialog |
| 8 | `player_tool_subtitles` | SUBTITLE | at least 1 subtitle track | Subtitles dialog |
| 9 | `player_tool_aspect` | ASPECT | always | Zoom dialog |
| 10 | `player_tool_mini` | PIP | dock available | mini player |
| 11 | `multiview_button` | LIST_GRID | Multiview setting on | Multiview |
| 12 | `player_tool_audio_only` | HEADPHONES | always on live | audio-only mode |
| 13 | `player_sleep_timer` | BEDTIME | always | Sleep-timer dialog |

Shell conditions: `OwnTVShell.kt:2037-2126`.

---

#### 2d.4 Channel panel (◀ from the picture) with the categories column

Wiring: `OwnTVShell.kt:2232-2272` → `A/features/shell/TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:37-115` → `A/features/shell/components/TvBoxChannelPanel.kt`.

**The picture moves into a corner** (`TvBoxChannelPanel.kt:76-87`, `TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:181-221`):
- Dock rect: Left **680**, Top **36**, radius **14**.
- Width = `1920 − 680 − inset − 36`. That is **1204** with the categories column closed and **764** with it open (inset 440). It narrows further by the side menu's push (84) when the menu is open (`StageRail.kt:114`).
- Height = width × 9/16 (677.25 / 429.75).
- The video surface itself is laid out at the in-between size (lerp from full screen), not scaled. **220 ms × speed**, FastOutSlowIn (`DockAnimMs`, `TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:221`). `inset` animates 0 ↔ 440 the same way (`:191-196`).
- Panel fill over everything except the picture: dark `#F2232629`, light `#F2EEF1F4` (`TvBoxChannelPanel.kt:396-402`). Its alpha follows dock progress. A transparent rounded rect is cut out (`BlendMode.Clear`) where the picture is, with radius 14 × progress (`:180-200`).
- While a channel is opening, a spinner sits in the picture's window: `OwnTVSpinner` 44 dp (= 79.2 px), white 0.9 (`:201-208`).

**Panel colours** (`tvBoxPanelColors`, `TvBoxChannelPanel.kt:392-402`). The player is always dark, so the dark set applies:

| token | dark | light |
|---|---|---|
| fill | `#F2232629` | `#F2EEF1F4` |
| text | `#FFFFFF` | `#16181D` |
| soft | white 0.8 | `#CC16181D` |
| faint | white 0.55 | `#8C16181D` |
| focus (row bar) | **`#3A3F45`** | `#DDE0E4` |
| focusInk | `#FFFFFF` | `#16181D` |
| line | `#1FFFFFFF` | `#1A000000` |

> **Code vs README:** the README asks for a white or light-grey focus plate. In the player's channel panel, the dark-theme focus bar is a *dark* grey `#3A3F45` with white text (`TvBoxChannelPanel.kt:400-401`, comment: "the focus bar a step lighter on it"). Follow the code. The categories column next to it does use the white focus card (see below).

**Channel list column** (`TvBoxChannelPanel.kt:209-247`, `:307-361`, `:391`):
- Width **640**, full height, starts at x = inset. Padding top 36.
- Title: 24/700, `text`, 1 line, padding h28. The text is the category name, or `content_channel_overlay_title` ("Каналы") as a fallback (`OwnTVShell.kt:2241`). For history it is `content_category_recently_watched`.
- List: top padding 14, content padding start 12 / end 12 / bottom 24, row gap 4. Scrolls only at the edges (`edgeScrollSpec`).
- **Row** 92 high, padding h16, gap 16, vertically centred. Focus: background `focus`, shape `FocusBarShape` = radius 4 (`A/ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:254`). No scale, no border. No indication ripple.
  - Logo tile 72×52, transparent fill. Fallback: `LIVE_TV` icon at 80% of the short side, `faint`.
  - Text column (gap 5):
    - Line 1 (gap 10): number 20/700 in `soft` (focused: focusInk 0.62), then name 20/700 in `text`. The name is `SkMateBlue` when that channel is playing. Marquee when focused (`focusMarquee`).
    - Line 2: current programme 16/500 `soft`, marquee when focused. Under it a flat progress bar, 4 high, full width (`StageProgress(flat=true)`, `A/ui/stage/StageControls.kt:259-280`): track = ink 0.16, fill = ink (white), because `TvBoxNav` (`:273`). *The code comment in `TvBoxChannelPanel.kt:351` calls this line "blue", but the code draws it in white ink. Follow the code.* With no guide: `content_no_guide_short` 16/500.
  - Trailing (one of):
    - countdown ring 32 (3-2-1, below);
    - `PlayingMark` = `OwnTVIcon.PAUSE` 30×30, filled, `SkMateBlue` (`A/ui/components/PlayingMark.kt:11-13`);
    - on history only, the time watched 15/500 `soft`.
- Focus is kept **by channel id**, not by row index. On open (and on return from the categories) focus goes to the playing channel. The list scrolls only if that row is off screen, putting it 3 rows from the top (`:128-141`).

**Block under the picture** (`TvBoxChannelPanel.kt:248-304`):
- x = 680 + inset, y = 36 + dockH + 24, width = dock width.
- It follows focus after a **120 ms** settle (`HEADER_SETTLE_MS`). The schedule follows after **250 ms** (`ScheduleSettleMs`). The progress clock ticks every **30 s** (`:403-405`).
- Header row (gap 16): logo 68×48, number 24/700 `soft`, name 24/700 `text`.
- Schedule: previous + now + next + upcoming, sorted by start, max **14** rows. Top padding 14. Row height 50, gap 20:
  - time range `content_live_time_range_plain` 21/600. `SkMateBlue` when on air, `faint` when past, `soft` otherwise.
  - title 21/500 (700 when on air). `SkMateBlue` when on air, `faint` when past, `text` otherwise.
  - on air only: a bar 240×6 (base = text, track 0.22) and `home_time_left`("осталось N мин") 19/600 `soft`.
  - With no guide: `content_no_guide_short` 21/500 `faint`, top padding 22.

**Live preview by resting on a row (dwell countdown)** (`TvBoxChannelPanel.kt:150-159`, `A/ui/components/DwellCountdown.kt:89-122`, `A/ui/components/CountdownRing.kt:41-61`):
- Armed only when the "live preview" setting is on (`TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:62`, `:77-80`; default = not a low-spec device, `C/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:2447-2451`) and the focused row is not the playing channel.
- Sequence: **1.5 s** quiet (`ChannelQuietMs`), then 3, 2, 1, each **1 s**. Then the focused channel plays in the docked picture.
- Ring: diameter 32, stroke 2.5 px, `SkMateBlue` 0.2 track, `SkMateBlue` arc that fills over **3 s** from 12 o'clock with round caps. The digit is 15/800 `SkMateBlue`. It follows the wall clock, not an animation.
- With live preview **off**, the channel changes only on OK (`TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:35`).

**Keys in the channel panel** (`TvBoxChannelPanel.kt:142`, `:213-219`; `TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:70-76`):

| Key | Action |
|---|---|
| ▲ ▼ | move focus row by row |
| OK | play that channel, close the panel (`ensurePlaying`) |
| ◀ | open the categories column (history panel: close) |
| ▶ | consumed, does nothing |
| Back | close. If other channels were previewed, the channel that was on when the panel opened plays again |
| Keys while the categories have focus | the panel takes no keys or focus (`active = false`) |

**Entrance.** Two motions stack on the channel panel:
1. `PanelEnter(fromX = −48)`: fade 0→1 plus a slide from −48 **dp** (= −86.4 px). **260 ms**, FastOutSlowIn. The first frame is skipped before the motion starts (`TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:125-145`; call `OwnTVShell.kt:2236`).
2. `slideInFromLeft`: translateX from −25% of the panel width to 0, alpha 0→1. **240 ms**, FastOutSlowIn (`TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:227-243`; applied at `:85`).

The side menu's push (84 px, **300 ms**, FastOutSlowIn) also moves the whole panel group right (`OwnTVShell.kt:787-801`, `:2235`).

**Categories column** (opened by ◀ in the channel panel; `TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:88-114`, `A/features/shell/components/CategoryBrowserOverlay.kt:62-177`, rows from `A/features/live/LiveStage.kt:608-800`):
- Width **440** (`CategoriesColumnWidth`, `OwnTVShell.kt:2626`). Flush with the left edge, full height.
- Background `#232629` (light `#EFF2F5`). A 1-px hairline `#14000000` on its right edge. Padding h16 v30 (`LiveStage.kt:699-701`).
- Same entrance (`slideInFromLeft`, 240 ms). At the same time the channel panel and the picture move right by 440 (220 ms).
- Heading `content_category_browser_title` ("Категории") 26/800, padding start/end 14, bottom 16 (`LiveStage.kt:707-716`).
- Search field (`content_search_categories` without the trailing "…"). Shown only when there are **more than 20** entries or a query is set. Height 54, radius 16, padding h16 (`LiveStage.kt:745-760`, `:949`).
- Rows: `StageSheetItem`, 56 high (min 56 when 2 lines), radius 16, `LIVE_TV` icon 28, text 20/600, count 16/600. The selected row has accent text plus a dot. Focus is the **white card** (`LocalWhiteFocus`) (`LiveStage.kt:693`, `:779-794`; `StageComponents.kt:812-880`). Details are in the categories section (2c).
- On entry, focus lands on the category being played. If that row is within the first 10, the list stays scrolled to the top (`CategoryBrowserOverlay.kt:96-100`, `:153`).
- Dwell: resting **7 s**, then 3-2-1 (1 s each), then the category opens by itself. This is paused while OK is held or while a menu is open (`LiveStage.kt:683-692`; `DwellCountdown.kt:118`).
- Keys:

  | Key | Action |
  |---|---|
  | OK | load that category's channels into the panel and close the column (`LiveViewModel.kt:1293-1294`) |
  | ▶ or Back | back to the channel panel; focus returns to the playing channel |
  | ◀ | open the app side menu over the picture; the menu push moves everything right (`TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:100`, `OwnTVShell.kt:2257-2269`, `:1804-1811`) |
  | Long OK | `CategoryContextMenu` (hide / restore) |
  | CH± / RW / FF | page by the configured skip counts (`chNavPaging`, `CategoryBrowserOverlay.kt:139-147`; factory bindings `C/settings/RemoteShortcuts.kt:47-58`: short = page ±N, long = first/last; default N = 10, `SettingsRepository.kt:286`) |

#### 2d.5 History panel (▶ from the picture)

`OwnTVShell.kt:2307-2323`. It is the same `TvBoxChannelPanel`, with these differences:
- title `content_category_recently_watched`;
- `watchedAt` times trailing on rows that are not playing;
- no live-preview countdown;
- inset 0;
- ◀ closes it.

Entrance: `PanelEnter(fromX = +48)` (from the right, 260 ms). The picture docks into the same corner (`OwnTVShell.kt:1877`).

Nothing is drawn if the history list is empty (`OwnTVShell.kt:2308`).

#### 2d.6 Guide over the picture (tile "Телегид")

`TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:147-173`, called at `OwnTVShell.kt:2229-2231`:
- `PanelEnter(fromY = 40)` (40 dp = 72 px up-slide, 260 ms).
- Full-screen scrim `#C7000000`.
- Dark palette, with the accent forced to `SkMateBlue` / white / white.
- Content: `EpgScreen(overlay = true, liveMode = true, previewEnabled = false, focusChannelId = playing)`. Its layout is in the guide section (2e).
- OK on a channel → watch and close. OK on a past programme → catch-up and close. Back → close.

#### 2d.7 Number-key zapping (direct tune)

`PlayerHud.kt:390-490`, `:549-588`, `:813-838`; card `A/player/PlayerHudChrome.kt:218-275`.
- Active only on a tuned live channel at the live edge, with the "direct tune" setting on (default **true**, `C/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:1685`; gating `OwnTVShell.kt:2080`). Disabled while a panel or dialog is open.
- Digits are consumed globally (key-down and key-up). Up to **5** digits; the 5th submits at once.
- Each digit restarts a **2 s** window; when it ends, the number is submitted. OK submits at once. Back clears the entry.
- Card position: top-start, padding start 28 dp (= 50.4 px), top 28 dp (= 50.4 px), or 92 dp (= 165.6 px) while the HUD is up. Stack gap 12 dp.
- **Entry card** (`ChannelNumberCard`):
  - min width 148 dp (= 266 px), max 340 dp (= 612 px). Radius 14 dp (= 25 px). Fill black 0.55.
  - Padding start 16 / end 20 / top 12 / bottom 12 dp.
  - Label `player_channel_label` ("КАНАЛ") 20 px bold, accent, letter-spacing 2 sp.
  - Digits 43 px bold white, letter-spacing 3 sp.
  - Blinking caret 3×22 dp in the accent, alpha 1↔0, 600 ms linear, reversing. Steady when animations are off.
  - Bottom bar 3 dp: track white 0.08, accent fill that drains from full to 0 over 2 s (linear).
- **Result**:
  - Found: the channel card `ChannelOsdCard` (logo 44 dp radius 10 on `#004F46`, letters fallback in `#6FF8E4`; name 25/500; `#N` 20/500 white 0.5; then the zap guide lines). Max width 420 dp (= 756 px) with a guide, 340 dp without. Radius 14 dp, fill black 0.55, padding 14 dp (`PlayerHudChrome.kt:176-202`). It stays until the new stream plays (max **8 s** wait), then **1.5 s** more.
  - Not found / several / failed: the same card with an error line 22/700 `#FF8A80`, for **1.5 s**. Texts: `player_channel_not_found`, `player_multiple_channels`, `player_tune_failed`.
- **Zap guide lines** (under the found card) (`A/features/shell/components/LiveEpgCard.kt:174-226`):
  - slot labels UPPERCASE 20/500 bold, letter-spacing 1.2 sp;
  - "В эфире сейчас" in the accent: title 25/400 white, then `content_live_time_remaining` / `content_live_time_range` 20/500 white 0.5, then a bar 2 dp (track white 0.18, accent fill);
  - "В эфире далее" white 0.45: title 22/700 white 0.72, time 20/500 white 0.4.

#### 2d.8 Remote keys in the TV-box live player

Default key map (`C/settings/LiveKeys.kt:43-48`): OK = INFO, ▲ = NEXT_CHANNEL, ▼ = PREVIOUS_CHANNEL, ◀ = CHANNEL_LIST, ▶ = HISTORY. The user can change these in Settings › Remote buttons (`A/features/settings/ChNavSettingsScreen.kt:90-111`). A custom map applies only where the code marks the key "free" (`PlayerHud.kt:633-690`). The table shows the **defaults**. Line numbers are `A/player/PlayerHud.kt` unless stated.

| Key | HUD hidden | Info step | Timeline step (`tvSeek`) | Tile row (`tvActions`) | Panel / guide open (HUD inert) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **OK** | show the info (:708-709) | pause / resume, repeat count 0 only (:731-736) | pause / resume (same branch) | activate the tile on key-up (`TvBoxLiveHud.kt:67-74`) | the panel's OK (§2d.4) |
| **▲** | next channel, then the info shows (:762, :412-417) | hide the HUD (:723) | back to the info (:718-722) | to the timeline if the channel can rewind, else to the info (:724-729) | move up |
| **▼** | previous channel, then the info shows (:763) | to the timeline if the channel can rewind and has guide data, else to the tile row (:710-717) | to the tile row (:712-717) | nothing (timer restart) | move down |
| **◀** | in the archive: step back (:739-746). Otherwise, with setting "Left/right rewind live" on and guide data: scrub back (:773-786; default **off**, `SettingsRepository.kt:1694`). Otherwise: channel panel (:788-789) | in the archive: scrub. Otherwise: hide + channel panel (:756-757) | scrub back (:749-754) | previous tile; wall on the first | panel: open categories; categories: side menu |
| **▶** | in the archive: step toward live. Otherwise (same setting rule): history panel (:790-791) | in the archive: scrub. Otherwise: hide + history (:758-759) | scrub toward live | next tile; wall on the last | channel panel: nothing; categories: back to channels |
| **Back** | leave the player (shell `BackHandler`, `OwnTVShell.kt:1032-1034`; shrink 260 ms on Live TV) | hide the HUD (:350) | back to the info (:354) | back to the **info** (:352), not to the timeline | close that panel / column / guide |
| **Long Back** (~12 repeats ≈ 1 s) | closes the app (`A/MainActivity.kt:66-75`, `:150`) | same | same | same | same |
| **CH+ / CH−** | next / previous channel, key-up swallowed, info shows (:592-597) | same | same | same | categories: page ±10 (long = first/last). Channel panel: not wired |
| **Media Next / Prev** | zap ±1 (:760-761) | same | same | same | — |
| **0-9** | direct tune (§2d.7) | same | same | same | ignored (entry cancelled while inert, :475) |
| **Play/Pause, RW, FF, Info, Menu, colour keys** (no user binding) | the hidden-HUD catch box shows the info and swallows the key (:799-804) | not consumed by the HUD (timer restart, :794). Goes on to the system MediaSession: Play/Pause toggles; RW/FF seek only on non-live (`P/PlaybackSession.kt:266-283`) | same | same | — |
| **User-bound remote shortcut** | runs on key-up; long press ≥ **600 ms** (:598-632, `:117`). Paging actions are ignored in the player (:603-607). Factory binding: LAST_CHANNEL key → previous channel (`RemoteShortcuts.kt:58`) | same | same | same | — |

Archive scrub steps (`PlayerHud.kt:1288-1308`):
- per press **10 s**;
- held (auto-repeat count): <8 → 10 s, <20 → 30 s, <40 → 60 s, then 300 s;
- the first step from the live edge is at least `CatchupJumps.MIN_OFFSET_SEC`.

Zap stream open (`A/features/live/ZapTuneDelay.kt:8-36`): an isolated press opens at once. If the previous press was within 1.5 s, the stream opens **500 ms** after the presses stop.

Held keys are paced by the activity (`MainActivity.kt:85-107`): a repeat of ▲▼◀▶ / CH± / PgUp / PgDn is dropped until the previous step's frame has drawn.
