#### 2d.9 Classic HUD (catch-up programmes, movies, episodes)

This applies when `tvLive` is false (`PlayerHud.kt:919-1006`). Sizes are in **dp** (px = ×1.8). Same Back and auto-hide rules: first Back hides; 4.5 s auto-hide.

- **Scrims**:
  - top: height 210 dp (= 378 px), gradient black 0.72 → 0.68 @0.5 → transparent;
  - bottom: height 260 dp (= 468 px), transparent → 0.68 @0.45 → 0.78 (`:924-937`).
- **Top bar** (`PlayerHudChrome.kt:65-162`). Padding 20 dp. Two equal halves with the clock centred between them.
  - Left half: `CircleButton(BACK, 40 dp)`, a 14 dp gap, then on live a logo 46 dp (radius 10, `#004F46` plate).
  - Live layout: chips row first (22/700 white 0.5, separated by 3-dp dots white 0.3), then `#N` 32/600 white 0.45 + title 32/600 white.
  - VOD layout: subtitle 22/700 white 0.45, then title, then chips (year, duration `N мин`, stream chips).
  - Right half: `LiveEpgCard` (live only, below).
- **Centre clock** `PlayerClock` (`A/player/PlayerClock.kt:50-119`). Radius 10 dp, fill black 0.38, 1-dp border white 0.10, padding h14 v8, column gap 14.
  - Each column: label UPPERCASE 20/500 bold, letter-spacing 1.2 sp; time 43 px semi-bold; date UPPERCASE 20/500 ("EEEdMMM").
  - "Текущее время" in white (label/date at 0.45).
  - While an archive plays, a second column "Время передачи" comes first, in the accent (date at 0.7), with a 1×38 dp divider.
  - Ticks every 10 s.
- **Live guide card** `LiveEpgCard` (`LiveEpgCard.kt:47-167`). Row gap 16 dp, hairline 1×34 dp white 0.18 between the halves.
  - Now: max width 300 dp; label "В эфире сейчас" in the accent; title 25/500 semi-bold white; `content_live_time_remaining` 20/500 white 0.5; synopsis 2 lines 20/500 white 0.62; bar 2 dp.
  - Next: max width 240 dp; label white 0.45; title 25/400 white 0.72; time white 0.4.
  - Archive variant: radius 8 dp, accent 0.09 fill, 1-dp accent 0.45 border, padding h10 v6. Labels "Воспроизводится" / "Затем". When an archive plays, the live card is shown at alpha 0.55 under the archive card (`OwnTVShell.kt:2143-2158`).
- **Centre transport** (`PlayerHudChrome.kt:279-316`, `A/player/PlayerHudControls.kt:97-138`).
  - Capsule: radius 44 dp, fill black 0.55, 1-dp border white 0.10, padding h10 v8, gap 10 dp.
  - Buttons: prev (if any) 44 dp, rewind 44, **play/pause 64 (primary: white 0.95 fill, dark icon `#0E1513`)**, forward 44, next 44.
  - Icon = 42% of the button size.
  - Focus `StageFocus.FILLED` with `hudAccent` fill and a white ring.
  - In timeshift, a line above it 25/600 accent: `player_behind_live` / `player_at_live_edge`.
- **Bottom dock** (`PlayerHudChrome.kt:320-516`, `PlayerHudControls.kt:140-553`). Padding h28 v20 dp.
  - Band A:
    - VOD seek bar: 24 dp high, track 4 dp (6 when focused), white 0.22 (0.4 focused), buffer white 0.28, accent fill. Playhead 26 dp halo accent 0.22 + 14 dp dot. Bubble `−mm:ss` 22/700 on black 0.9, radius 8, 32 dp above.
    - Or the live timeline (◀▶ = ±60 s, `LIVE_SCRUB_STEP_SEC`): programme ticks 1 dp (2 dp for the current one) × 10/12 dp; red live dot `#FF4D4D` 14 dp; bubble "20:54 · Programme".
    - Then `LiveStateBadge`: radius 7 dp, red `#DC3232` 0.85 with a pulsing dot (900 ms reverse) "ЭФИР", or amber `#E8A33D` "−mm:ss" when behind.
  - Band B: two clusters at the screen edges, gap 4 dp. Order from core `C/player/PlayerControls.kt:59-137`:
    - Media cluster: Go live pill (108 dp, red 0.85, "К эфиру"), Volume, Speed, Subtitles, Audio, Favourite, Catch-up ("Вернуться к…"), Previous channel, Other channels.
    - Tools cluster: Aspect, Quality, Mini, Audio only, Multiview, Record, Sleep timer, Info, Report (only while Info is on).
  - Tool tile `ToolTile`: width 104 dp, min height 74 dp, radius 14 dp, icon 26 dp in a 28-dp box, 6 dp gap, caption 19 px (500 / 700 focused). Focus `StageFocus.FX` (accent rim, wash and glow). Active tint = accent; favourite = `favorite` colour; record = `#EF4444`. Count badge 16 dp accent circle at the top-end.
- **Next-episode card** (VOD): bottom-end, padding end 28 dp, bottom 120 dp. It appears 30 s before the auto-advance at duration − 8 s (`PlayerHud.kt:298-307`, `:1008-1019`).
- **Engine/notice toast**: bottom-centre, 104 dp from the bottom, 25/600, radius 20 dp, black 0.55, padding h16 v8. Shown for 1.8 s; the smoothness hint stays 6 s (`PlayerHud.kt:366-381`, `:1021-1039`).

#### 2d.10 Dialogs from the player

All of them use `StagePopup` (`A/ui/stage/StagePopup.kt:64-130`):
- solid card radius 30 in the TV-box look;
- padding h12 v30, inner h18;
- title 38/800;
- body 18/400 with line height 1.45;
- buttons right-aligned, gap 14, height 56, text 19.

The component itself is specified in the tokens section.

Player lists use `DialogScaffold`: width **640**, a lazy list with gap 4 (`A/player/PlayerHudDialogs.kt:416-424`). Rows are `StagePopupOption` with a radio check (24 px, `CHECK` icon) on the right (`:429-440`). Focus starts on the selected row.

| Dialog | Title key | Rows | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Audio | `player_audio_track` | tracks. Then, where the engine supports it, a stepper `player_av_sync` (±25 ms, range ±5000) and a check `player_av_sync_remember` | `PlayerHudDialogs.kt:58-182`, `PlayerHud.kt:1174-1194` |
| Subtitles | `player_subtitles` | `common_off`, tracks (image tracks labelled `player_image_track`). Then label "ДОБАВИТЬ СУБТИТРЫ" + "Поиск в OpenSubtitles" / local file (VOD only). Then label "НАСТРОЙКА" + `player_subtitle_timing` (±100 ms, range ±60 s; OK resets) | same file :111-153 |
| Aspect | `settings_player_zoom` | Fit, Fill/Crop, Stretch, Original 1:1, Force 16:9, Force 4:3 (`P/OwnTVPlayer.kt:121-124`) | `PlayerHudDialogs.kt:312-325` |
| Speed (VOD) | `settings_playback_speed` | 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0 (normal), 1.25, 1.5, 2.0 | `:48`, `:203-220` |
| Quality | `player_tool_quality` | Auto + heights (`%dp`) | `:328-346` |
| Volume | `player_volume` | − / value 44/800 accent / + (step 5, 0..150), buttons Mute/Unmute + Done; width 640 | `:348-371` |
| Sleep timer | `player_sleep_timer`. While running: `player_sleep_timer_remaining` | Off (only while running), 15/30/45/60/90 min (`P/SleepTimer.kt:147`), End of programme (live, when the guide knows the end), End of movie/episode, check "Также выключать экран" | `:228-310` |
| Go back to… (catch-up channels) | `content_catchup_jump` | body `content_catchup_jump_prompt`; time rows with a CATCHUP icon; last row "Выбрать точное время…" → `CatchupManualTimeDialog` (width 760; day / hour / minute wheels; Cancel / Play) | `A/features/live/CatchupJumpDialog.kt:35-100`, `CatchupManualTimeDialog.kt:43-140` |
| Continue where you left off | `player_timeshift_resume_title` / `_message` | `StageConfirm`: cancel "К эфиру", confirm "Продолжить" | `A/ui/components/TimeshiftResumeDialog.kt:13-22`, `OwnTVShell.kt:2221-2227` |

Note: in the TV-box live steps there is no tile for "Go back to…". The dialog is reachable only from the classic dock, which a tuned live channel never shows. Treat it as low priority. Speed and Volume have no TV-box tile either.

#### 2d.11 Buffering, errors and badges

- **Buffering** (`PlayerHud.kt:1126-1161`):
  - TV-box live: a quiet white spinner `OwnTVSpinner` 48 dp (= 86.4 px), white 0.9, centred. Not drawn under an open panel; the panel draws its own.
  - Classic HUD: 56 dp in the accent.
  - Spinner: 12 rounded spokes, one step every 1000/12 ms, with fading trails (`A/ui/components/StateViews.kt:26-68`).
  - After **10 s** of a stall: `player_reconnecting` 25/400 white 0.85.
  - Provider back-off: `player_provider_retry_after` (code, reason or `player_provider_busy`, seconds).
- **Error screen** (`PlayerHud.kt:1041-1121`):
  - full screen `#0B0F14`, centred column 70% wide;
  - illustration 190 dp (= 342 px): SIGNAL / NO_INTERNET / DEVICE (`errorArtOf`, `:1258-1265`);
  - 26 dp gap, then title 40/700 white (`player_error_title_signal` / `_internet` / `_device`);
  - 10 dp gap, then the failure text 25/400 white 0.7; the reason 25/400 white 0.92;
  - 18 dp gap, then **Retry** (`common_retry`).
  - `ErrorButton`: radius 4 (`FocusBarShape`), white 0.14 idle / **white** focused, text 23/700 white / `#0B0F14` focused, padding h30 v15 dp (`A/ui/stage/ErrorIllustration.kt:125-138`).
  - Retry takes focus 150 ms after the screen opens.
  - When a recording holds the only stream: the line `player_recording_conflict`, then two buttons: "Продолжить просмотр (остановит запись)" + "Закрыть".
  - The HUD is forced visible while an error shows. Back leaves the player.
- **Error under an open panel**: only a quiet mark, 96 dp illustration at alpha 0.6, on `#0B0F14` (`:1047-1052`, `:1268-1269`).
- **REC badge**: top-end, padding top 18 / end 22 dp, radius 6 dp, an 8-dp pulsing red dot `#EF4444`, text "REC". Always drawn while recording, HUD up or down (`A/player/RecordingBadge.kt:50-107`; `PlayerHud.kt:840-847`).
- **Audio-only badge** (channel without video): title `player_audio_only_title` 40/600 + body 25/400 white 0.7 (`A/player/AudioOnlyBadge.kt:41-70`).
- **Stream info overlay** (Info tool): top-end, padding top 112 dp, end 20 dp (`PlayerHud.kt:808-811`).
- **Corner clock** (Settings › player clock, default **off**, `SettingsRepository.kt:2641`) (`A/player/PlayerCornerClock.kt:23-98`; `ClockStyle.kt:17-24`):
  - top-right, end 40, top 28 (0 with a plate);
  - default style: 26/700 white on a black plate at 75% opacity, which hangs from the top edge with bottom corners radius 8 and padding h16 v6;
  - options: sizes 20/26/36; inks white / `#FFD54F` / `#8CC8FF`; backgrounds none (text shadow black 0.45, blur 8) / black / white (ink `#15181C`) / `SkMateBlue`; transparency 0/25/50/75%;
  - ticks on the minute;
  - not drawn while docked beside the panel or while growing (`OwnTVShell.kt:1951-1954`).
- **Goal toast** (Sports): top-end, padding top 45, end 60, 8 s (`OwnTVShell.kt:2004-2006`, `:2171-2173`, `:2556`). It belongs to the Sports section.

#### 2d.12 Not used on the TV-box path (do not build first)

- `ChannelListOverlay` (`A/features/shell/components/ChannelListOverlay.kt`) is drawn only when `!TvBoxNav` (`OwnTVShell.kt:2288-2305`, `:2324-2340`). With `TvBoxNav = true` it is dead code.
- The zap "now watching" card (`channelFlash`, `PlayerHud.kt:362-364`) is shown only when not `tvLive`. On TV-box live, a zap shows the bottom info strip instead (`:416`).
- `MiniPlayer` (`A/player/MiniPlayer.kt`) is the docked corner player (24 dp padding, width fraction from settings, 16:9, radius 14 dp; `OwnTVShell.kt:1866-1872`). It is reachable via the "Мини" tile. Lower priority.


### 2e. Guide, Sport, Search

Source tree: `C:\Users\darsaec\Projects\skmate-android-main` (origin/main, commit `3ad3636`). Unless a path starts with `engine/`, paths are relative to `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/`. Sizes are mpx (1 mpx = 1 px on the 1920×1080 web stage). `fx(n)` in the code means "n/1920 of the screen width", so on a 1920 stage it is also n px.

> **Important: the reference screenshots are scaled.** Values written in mpx come out about 11 % bigger in `03-sport.png`, `04-teleguide.png` and `05-poisk.png` than in the code. Values written as `fx(n)` (screen fractions) match exactly. Measured examples: guide rows are 56 mpx high with a 6 mpx gap, so they repeat every 62 mpx, but in the screenshot they repeat every 69 px. The guide video is 640 mpx wide but 710 px in the screenshot. The search field is 48 mpx high but 52 px in the screenshot. Sport cards repeat every 106 mpx but 118 px in the screenshot. Meanwhile the guide channel column (fx 48 → 344), the sport side margin (180 px) and the search-field x (537 px) match exactly. So the emulator was at UI zoom 100 % (density 2.0, 1 mpx = 1.111 px), not the 90 % default that `ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:15-23` assumes. **Build with the mpx numbers as px.** When you compare with the screenshots, expect fixed sizes to look 11 % smaller in the web build, while positions based on screen fractions stay the same.

> **TV-box mode is always on.** `internal const val TvBoxNav = true` (`features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:2609`). Every `if (TvBoxNav)` branch below is the live one. Anything only in the `else` branch (the guide's control line, key hints, record menu items, sport hub chips/cards) is **not shown** in SkMate. Such things are marked "inactive".

Shared facts for all three screens:
- Page background (dark): `TvBoxMenuFill` `#232629` (`features/shell/components/StageRail.kt:540`, used as `shellBase` at `features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:1069-1074`). This is the same value as `StagePalette.Dark.page` (`ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:96`).
- The clock/date/weather cluster at the top right of every screenshot is the shell's `StageStatusCluster` (`features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:1637+`), not part of these screens. See section 2b/2c.
- Key hints (`StageKeyHints`) are **never drawn**: `if (TvBoxNav) return` (`ui/stage/StageControls.kt:200-202`). The guide's `GuideKeyHints`, the sport hub hints and the search hints in the code therefore show nothing.
- The default accent is INK. In dark it is primary `#F2F0ED` and on-primary `#121212` (`engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/theme/Palette.kt:121-124`). `stageAccent.accent` = `#F2F0ED`, and `stageAccent.focus` is the same colour. The brand blue `SkMateBlue` = `#0086FC` (`ui/brand/SkMateWordmark.kt:16`).
- Focus on any `StageSurface` with FX/FILLED/PRIMARY style (rows, tools, tabs, buttons, menu items) is a flat light-grey bar: `#E0E2E4` in dark (`SheetFocusFill`) and `#E7E8EA` in light (`LightFocusFill`). Corner radius 4 (`FocusBarShape`). No ring, no shadow, no scale (`ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:191-199, 207-212, 254, 263-266`). FX and FILLED do not lift. PRIMARY scales to 1.04 over 170 ms with FastOutSlowIn (`StageComponents.kt:269-287`; `ownTvTween` uses FastOutSlowInEasing, `ui/theme/Animations.kt:38`). In TV-box mode StageButton uses PRIMARY, so a focused button grows to 1.04. Content on the bar is re-themed to the **light** palette (text `#121212`). The accent becomes `SkMateBlue` when the accent is pale (luminance > 0.6, which is true for INK dark) (`StageComponents.kt:216-232, 1365`).
- Easing names: FastOutSlowIn = `cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)`, Linear = `linear`. "Animations off" sets every duration to 0 (`ui/theme/MotionPrefs.kt:110-116`). Speed factors: FAST ×0.6, NORMAL ×1, SLOW ×1.5 (`MotionPrefs.kt:14`).
- Popup menus (`GuideMenuHost`, used by the guide **and** the sport board). Back closes them (`features/epg/GuideStage.kt:311`). In TV-box mode they are **centred** on screen, at most `screenHeight − 96` tall and scroll beyond that. The scrim is `StageColors.Scrim` (dark `rgba(4,5,6,.55)`, light `rgba(18,18,18,.30)`, `ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:106,136`). Width = the given width, capped at screen width − 64. Focus is trapped inside (`GuideStage.kt:309-329`). The card is `StageMenu`: `solidCard(28)`, fill `#232629` in dark or `#FFFFFF` in light, shadow black 50 % (light 16 %) with blur 40 and dy 14, padding 16 left/right, 22 top, 16 bottom (`ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:691-703, 1328-1337`). The content scrolls vertically.
  - Header `StageMenuHeader`: padding 12 left/right, 12 bottom. Title 23/800 `Text`, a 2 px gap, subtitle 15/500 `Muted`, each one line with ellipsis (`StageComponents.kt:709-726`).
  - Group label `StageGroupLabel`: 12.5/800, letter-spacing 0.13 em, `Dim`, passed in UPPERCASE, padding 14 left/right, 12 top, 5 bottom (`StageComponents.kt:731-747`).
  - Item `StageMenuItem` in TV-box mode: **76** high, padding 14 left/right, gap 14. Icon **28** `Muted`. Text **23**/600 `MenuItemText` (`#DDE0E3` dark), with a marquee when focused. Value **17**/700 `Dim`, or `SkMateBlue` when focused. ✓ check icon 22 in accent. Disabled items are at 45 % opacity. A 1 px hairline in `Text` at 8 % runs along the bottom, inset 14 on each side (`StageComponents.kt:751-806, 1365-1372`).

---

#### 2e.1 Guide (Телегид), `features/epg/EpgScreen.kt` + `GuideStage.kt` + `GuideCore.kt`

The current Stage guide is `EpgScreen` (`features/epg/EpgScreen.kt:167-822`). `GuideStage.kt` draws its parts, and `GuideCore.kt` draws the programme cells and the old detail dialog. **GenreColor (`ui/components/GenreColor.kt`) is not used by the guide.** No code outside that file references `ChannelGenre`, so cells have no genre colours. `GuideLoading` is used only by the setup wizard. `DayStepperDialog` is used only in EPG settings and source forms.

**Where it opens** (`features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:1554-1570, 1614-1629`; `features/shell/TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:147-171`):
1. Side menu → «Телегид» (`MainSection.EPG`). `liveMode = false`.
2. Channels screen with «Вид телегида» = guide (`MainSection.LIVE_TV` + `LiveView.GUIDE`). `liveMode = true`: the category follows the channel list, and a «Вид списка» button appears.
3. Over the full-screen player (`TvBoxGuideOverlay`). `overlay = true`, `liveMode = true`, no video. Dark palette is forced, and the accent is overridden to `SkMateBlue` / white / white (`TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:150-153`). The scrim behind is `#C7000000` (black 78 %) (`TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:173-174`). Focus opens on the playing channel's row (`focusChannelId`, `EpgScreen.kt:338-346`).
- Enter animation for cases 1 and 2: `PanelEnter(fromY = 24, startAlpha = 0.7)`. Opacity goes 0.7 → 1 while the screen moves from +24 dp (not mpx: it is multiplied by density) to 0, over 260 ms with FastOutSlowIn (`TvBoxPlayerPanels.kt:125-145`). On the web: translateY 43 px (24 dp × 1.8) → 0, opacity .7 → 1, 260 ms.

##### Layout (1920 × 1080)
| Part | Geometry | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Top band | Padding left 64, right 64, top **44**; height **360** | `EpgScreen.kt:477` |
| Video | 640 × 360 (16:9), radius **28**, black fill. Drop shadow `stageShadow(0.55)` (black 55 %), blur 80, dy 30. A bottom gradient goes from transparent at 58 % to black 72 % at 100 %. 1 px inner ring of white 8 %. | `GuideStage.kt:105-158`, `EpgScreen.kt:479-486` |
| Video: no-picture state | Channel logo 200 × 200 centred, on a transparent tile. Fallback: icon `LIVE_TV` 48 in `Dim`. Spinner 24 while LOADING. | `GuideStage.kt:137-143` |
| Video: caption | Bottom-left, padding 22 left/right, 18 bottom. «`N · Channel`» 19/700 white + « · now-title» in `Muted` SemiBold, one line with ellipsis. **No ЭФИР badge in TV-box mode** (`if (!TvBoxNav)`). | `GuideStage.kt:146-156` |
| Programme block | Right of the video, `weight 1`. Padding start **46** (0 in overlay), top **76**, max width 1080. | `EpgScreen.kt:488-498` |
| • eyebrow | Icon `CLOCK` 18 in accent, gap 10, text **18/700**, letter-spacing +0.02 em, accent colour, one line. Text = «В эфире сейчас · 12:15 – 14:45 · 150 мин · Channel» or «Начнётся через N · …». | `GuideStage.kt:160-172, 194-199` |
| • title | **60/800**, letter-spacing −1.5 mpx, `Text`, one line with ellipsis, padding top 10, bottom 6. Without a programme it shows the channel name. | `GuideStage.kt:200-205`, `EpgScreen.kt:493` |
| • details | 19/400 `Muted`, one line: categories · episode · year · rating · length. With no programme: «На это время телепередач нет.». | `GuideStage.kt:174-181, 206`, `EpgScreen.kt:494` |
| • synopsis | 19/400, line-height 28.5 (1.5), colour `#CCD6D2` in dark / `Body` in light. **Exactly 2 lines** (min 2, max 2), max width 1000, padding top 12, bottom 20. | `GuideStage.kt:207-212` |
| «Вид списка» button | Only with `liveMode` and not overlay. Bottom-right of the top band. `StageTool` boxed, icon `LIST`. | `EpgScreen.kt:501-507` |
| Control line (Today ▾, Now, Category ▾, Order ▾, Search, Auto-match) | **Inactive**: the whole row is wrapped in `if (!TvBoxNav)`. | `EpgScreen.kt:515-572` |
| Grid area | Fills the rest. Padding top **30** (TV-box), so its top edge is at y = 44 + 360 + 30 = **434**. | `EpgScreen.kt:575` |
| Day label | At the grid top, x = 48 + 16, width = labelW − 24. Format `EEEdMMM` («ср, 25 нояб.»), **19/700 `SkMateBlue`**. It shows the day at 3/8 of the visible timeline. | `EpgScreen.kt:513-514, 596-601` |
| Channel column width | `labelW = 296` (= 1920 × 296/1920, `ChannelColShare`). With custom widths: `(W − 112) × channels%`. | `GuideCore.kt:51-53`, `EpgScreen.kt:461` |
| Timeline | x = `gx` = 48 + labelW = **344**. Width = W − 48 − labelW − 64 = **1512**. | `EpgScreen.kt:462, 593` |
| Scale | **7.2 mpx per minute**, so 1512 px = 3.5 h; slot = 30 min = 216 px | `GuideCore.kt:57-59` |
| Ruler | x = 344, width 1512, height **40**, at the grid top (y 434). Labels at y +4, the now-chip at y 0. | `EpgScreen.kt:602-605`, `GuideStage.kt:261-262` |
| Rows list | Padding left 48, right 64, top **48** → first row at y = **482**. Row height **56**, gap **6**, bottom padding 24. | `EpgScreen.kt:607-617`, `GuideCore.kt:54-56` |
| Now-line | x = gx − 1.5 + (now − windowStart) × ppm − scroll. Starts 38 below the grid top (y 472) and runs to the bottom. **3 wide**, `SkMateBlue`, glow = box-shadow in `SkMateBlue`, blur 14. Hidden (alpha 0) when outside the timeline. | `EpgScreen.kt:666-681`, `GuideStage.kt:297-302` |

With the default 7-day window, about 9 rows are visible (1080 − 482 = 598 / 62 ≈ 9.6). The screenshot shows 8 rows because it is at 111 % scale.

##### Ruler (`GuideRuler`, `GuideStage.kt:216-266`)
- Time labels every 30 min, aligned to :00/:30. **Labels within 12 min of now are skipped.** Style **17/700**, `font-feature-settings: "tnum"`, colour `Muted` (`#A9ADB2`). Each label's left edge sits at its time (not centred).
- Now-chip: the current time «13:55», **15/800** tnum, white on `SkMateBlue`, radius 8, padding 9 left/right, 3 top/bottom. Its left edge is shifted **34 left** of the now x (`chipShift`), and it sits 4 higher than the labels. Line 230 picks the chip fill: `SkMateBlue` in TV-box mode (otherwise the accent).
- The ruler, the now-line and every row share one horizontal scroll value (`scrollPx`, `EpgScreen.kt:257-264`). The clock refreshes every **30 s** (`EpgScreen.kt:209-214`).

##### Time window and scrolling (`EpgScreen.kt:257-336`, `features/epg/EpgViewModel.kt:888-900`)
- `windowStart` = now rounded down to :00/:30, minus max(2 h, the deepest catch-up archive, capped at 7 days). `windowEnd` = now rounded down + `guideDaysToKeep` days (default **7**, range 1–14: `engine/core/.../settings/SettingsRepository.kt:164-166`).
- On open: `showTime(now)` puts now at **3/8** of the timeline width (`EpgScreen.kt:265-272`). There is no animated scroll; it jumps.
- Cursor follow: when the cursor comes within **30 min (216 px)** of either edge, scroll so it stays 30 min inside. The jump is instant (`EpgScreen.kt:327-336`).
- Vertical: ▲▼ use `stepTo`, which scrolls by one row pitch to keep **one row of room** ahead of the focused row (`EpgScreen.kt:290-313`; `edgeScrollSpec` at `features/live/LiveStage.kt:538-551`). Held keys keep counting from the target row.

##### Channel label (`GuideChannelLabel`, `GuideStage.kt:268-295`)
Row, padding start 8, items 10 apart, vertically centred:
1. Playlist dot (7 px): **hidden in TV-box mode**.
2. Number: **17/700 tnum**. Width = max(38, digits × 11) (`EpgScreen.kt:594-595`). Colour `Dim` (`#6E7378`), or `SkMateBlue` on the focused row. Never wraps.
3. Logo plate **58 × 40**, radius **10**, fill `ControlFill` (ink 6 %). Without a logo: `LIVE_TV` icon at 78 % of the plate height in `Dim` (`features/live/LiveStage.kt:254-272`).
4. Name: **17/700**, `Text` (`#F1F2F3`), or `SkMateBlue` on the focused row. Fills the rest, one line with ellipsis. Provider tags are removed (`ProviderTags.parse`). If the category name is a prefix of the channel name, it is cut off (`EpgScreen.kt:406-416`).
5. Catch-up mark: if the channel has catch-up, icon `REWIND` **20** in `Dim` (or `SkMateBlue` on the focused row), end padding 8.
The label column is **not focusable**. "Selected row" = the grid has focus and this is `focusedRow`.

##### Programme cells (`ProgrammeStripCanvas`, `GuideCore.kt:62-215`; TV-box branch)
Each row draws all its programmes on one canvas. The whole row is a single focus target.
- Geometry: `x = (start − windowStart) min × 7.2 − scroll + 3`, `w = duration × 7.2 − 6`, which leaves a 6 px gap between cells. Cell height = row height (56). Radius **4** in TV-box mode (14 otherwise) (`GuideCore.kt:121, 150-151`). Overlapping programmes: each one starts where the previous one ended (`GuideCore.kt:122-125, 146-149`).
- Fill: focused cell → `SkMateBlue` at **16 %**. Programme on now → ink **6 %**. Others → ink **3.5 %**, and past programmes are also multiplied by 0.5 (`GuideCore.kt:158-164`). Ink = white in dark (`#121212` in light). No ring, no glow in TV-box mode.
- Text: title **18/700** at (x + 14, 8), clipped to `w − 28` with ellipsis. Second line at y = 8 + title height + 3. It starts with icons (14 px, 8 apart): recording ● `REC` filled `#FF5B5B`, `BELL` for a reminder, `REWIND` if catch-up is available. Icons use the accent (`#F2F0ED`) at the past-fade alpha. Then the time «12:15 – 14:45» **14/400 tnum** in `Dim` (`GuideCore.kt:172-206`). Past programmes: title and time at 50 % alpha.
- Too narrow (visible text width ≤ 44): no text, only a dot of radius 2.5 in `Dim` at 70 % (× fade), centred, if the cell is wider than 8 (`GuideCore.kt:175-183, 408-410`). The screenshot shows these as «•».
- Nothing in view: a full-width box of ink 3 %, with «На это время телепередач нет.» 14/400 in `Dim` at x 17, vertically centred (`GuideCore.kt:208-213`). The screenshot shows this on rows 2 and 5.
- The cursor sits in a gap (the feed stops): the gap is drawn as the focused cell, `SkMateBlue` 16 % plus that same text (`GuideCore.kt:126-144`).
- The row is focused in **row stage**: the whole programme area (not the label column) gets the light bar `#E0E2E4`, radius 4 (`EpgScreen.kt:907`, `stageFocusLook(FX)` → `StageComponents.kt:101-103`). On the bar the cells are redrawn "on light": text `#16181D` (`BarInk`), dim text `#16181D` at 62 %, ink = black for fills (`GuideCore.kt:96-99, 405-406`). The screenshot shows this on row 1.
- **Cell stage** (cursor inside the row): no bar. Only the cursor's cell gets the `SkMateBlue` 16 % fill.
- Row data loads lazily when a row comes into view. If it is empty, it retries twice, 3 s apart (`EpgScreen.kt:855-865`).

##### D-pad and keys (`GuideRow` key handler, `EpgScreen.kt:824-926`; cursor stepping `EpgScreen.kt:142-165`)
Two stages per row (`inCellMode`, `EpgScreen.kt:280-282`).

| Key | Row stage (bar on the row) | Cell stage (cursor) |
|---|---|---|
| ▲ / ▼ | Move to the previous/next row (`onStep`). From the first row, ▲ is not handled, so focus moves up: with `liveMode` it reaches «Вид списка», otherwise there is nothing above. Below the last row, ▼ is swallowed. | Same. The cursor time is kept, so the new row's cursor is the programme on at that time. The row on arrival is in row stage, because `cellMode` is only true for `focusedRow`. |
| ◀ | Not handled: focus leaves to the left, which opens the hidden side menu (shell: ◀ from the content's left edge). | Cursor moves to the previous programme. Across a gap longer than 30 min it moves 30 min at a time. Clamped to the window. |
| ▶ | Enter cell stage: cursor = **now**. Also brings this channel into the preview (if Guide preview is on). | Cursor moves to the next programme, or by 30 min across gaps. |
| OK (short, on key-up) | Enter cell stage (same as ▶). | If the preview is on and not yet on this channel: put this channel into the preview (it plays after 700 ms). Otherwise **watch**: a past programme with archive plays from the archive (player chosen by the «Воспроизводить архив через» setting; ASK shows a chooser), otherwise the channel plays live (`EpgScreen.kt:390-394, 660`). |
| OK held (second KeyDown, `repeatCount > 0`) / Menu key | Opens the programme menu for the cursor's programme, or the channel menu if there is no programme (`EpgScreen.kt:882-889`). | Same |
| Back | Shell decides. `MainSection.EPG` → focus the side menu (`OwnTVShell.kt:1050-1052`). `liveMode` → back to the list view (`EpgScreen.kt:285`). Over the player → close the overlay. | Back to row stage (`EpgScreen.kt:286`) |

- Leaving the grid for anything other than its own menus resets to row stage (`EpgScreen.kt:374-375`).
- Entering the guide from the side menu (▶) lands on the row that was focused (`onEntryHook`, `EpgScreen.kt:430-436, 450-457`).
- After a menu closes, focus returns to the grid row after 60 ms (`EpgScreen.kt:362-373`).
- After watching a channel tuned from the guide, coming back restores that row (`restoreFocus`, `EpgScreen.kt:338-346`).

##### Preview behaviour (`EpgScreen.kt:239-251, 478-486`)
- The preview is shown when the player is not full screen, AND «Превью» (Live preview) is on, AND «Показывать превью» in the guide is on (default **true**: `engine/core/.../SettingsRepository.kt:1577`).
- The video does **not** follow focus. OK brings a row's channel in. After **700 ms** it starts playing (spinner while loading).
- With the guide preview off, moving through rows updates the preview channel only for the top band's text.

##### Menus opened from the guide (all are `GuideMenuHost`: centred, see the shared facts above)
1. **Programme menu** `GuideProgrammeMenu`, width **560** (`GuideStage.kt:417-511`). Header: programme title + «12:15 – 14:45 · 1 Sky Sports…». Items in order:
   - group НОВОЕ: «Напомнить» with value «за 5 мин» (`content_remind_before` + `player_duration_minutes`; «В начале» when the lead is 0; `reminderLeadText`, `features/live/LiveScreen.kt:1750-1752`), or «Напоминание установлено» ✓. Enabled only before the programme starts; selecting it closes the menu.
   - group СМОТРЕТЬ: «Смотреть канал» (`LIVE_TV`); «Смотреть с начала» (`REWIND`), disabled without archive, value «после HH:MM» for future programmes; «Воспроизводить архив через…» (`EXTERNAL`) with value «Всегда спрашивать» / «Плеер SkMate IPTV» / «Внешний плеер» — **this one cycles ASK→INTERNAL→EXTERNAL in place and the menu stays open**.
   - group ЗАПИСЬ: **inactive in TV-box mode** (`GuideStage.kt:472-491`).
   - group КАНАЛ: «Добавить в избранное» / «Удалить из избранного» (`FAVORITE`); «Автосопоставление EPG» (`SPARKLE`); «Выбрать канал EPG вручную» (`EPG`); «Смещение времени EPG» (`CLOCK`) with value = the channel's offset (`liveEpgShiftLabel`) or «Общий» (`content_epg_offset_global_short`).
   - Initial focus: Remind if enabled, else Watch channel.
2. **Channel menu** `GuideChannelMenu` (no programme at the cursor), width 560 (`GuideStage.kt:572-607`). Header «N · Name» / «На это время телепередач нет.». Items: СМОТРЕТЬ «Смотреть канал»; КАНАЛ favourite, auto-match, pick manually, time offset.
3. **Catch-up player chooser** `GuideCatchupChooser`, width 470 (`GuideStage.kt:666-676`). Header «Воспроизводить архив через» + description; items «Плеер SkMate IPTV» (`PLAY`), «Внешний плеер» (`EXTERNAL`).
4. **Auto-match review** `GuideReviewPopup` (`GuideStage.kt:678-838`). Glass card, width **1080**, x = 420/1920 of W, top **150**, radius 30, padding 30. Eyebrow `SPARKLE` + «Автосопоставление EPG · Сопоставлено N каналов» 18/700 accent. Title «Проверить N сопоставлений» 38/800. Description 18/400 `Muted`. List max height 5 × 78. Each row is 78 high, radius 18, padding 14, gap 16: plate 70 × 50 · name 20/700 (width 300) · «→» 22/400 `Dim` · feed name 18/400 `#D3DCD8` · score chip «87 %» 15/800 in `chipWarnText` on `chipWarnFill`, radius 8, padding 9/4 · Tool «Принять» `CHECK` · Tool «Пропустить» `CLOSE`. ▶ from «Пропустить» jumps to «Принять все». Footer (top 22, gap 12): switch «Использовать логотипы из гида» (56 high, radius 15) · «Пропустить все» · «Принять все» · «Готово» (StageButton, 56 high, text 19). Back = Done.
5. **Order menu** (`GuideOrderMenu`, 470) and **Day menu** (`GuideDayMenu`, 400): reachable only from the inactive control line, so **not reachable in SkMate**. In TV-box mode, sort/«show empty»/«show preview» are reached elsewhere (the channels screen's «Вид телегида» / options, see 2b).
6. EPG offset dialog `EpgOffsetDialog` and manual EPG picker `EpgMatchDialog` live in `features/live/` and are owned by the channel-list section (2b/2d).
7. **Reminder prompt** `ReminderPrompt` (when a reminder fires, «Спрашивать о переключении»): glass card 760 wide, centred, radius 30, padding 30. `BELL` + eyebrow 18/700 accent; title 38/800 (2 lines max); buttons «Смотреть канал» (focused first) and «Закрыть», 56 high, text 19, gap 12 (`GuideStage.kt:848-891`).
8. `ProgrammeDetailDialog` / `CatchupPlayerChooser` in `GuideCore.kt:217-403` are not opened by `EpgScreen`. They are older, shared dialogs (Live TV catch-up picker). Not needed for the guide.

##### Empty and loading states (`EpgScreen.kt:576-591, 928-943`)
- Loading: spinner of 44 (dp) centred in the grid area.
- No EPG sources and no channels: title «EPG не добавлен.» (`content_epg_empty`) 24/700 `Text`, body 19/400 `Muted` (max width 900, top 8), button «Добавить EPG» (`ADD`, 56 high, text 19) 24 below. The whole block sits 80 above the centre.
- Channel IDs don't match: title «Нет программы передач.» (`content_epg_no_guide`), body `content_epg_mismatched_ids`, button «Автосопоставление EPG» (`SPARKLE`).
- Otherwise: message title only (`content_epg_create_profile` / `_add_playlist` / `_no_channels_query`).

##### Screenshot `04-teleguide.png` vs code
- All structure matches: video top-left, eyebrow + 60/800 title, the day «ср, 25 нояб.» in blue left of the ruler, the blue «13:55» chip and the line, row 1 on the light bar with the number and name in blue, "no programme" rows, dots in short cells.
- The synopsis is empty in the screenshot. The code still reserves 2 lines (`minLines = 2`).
- Sizes are about 11 % larger than mpx (see the note at the top).

#### 2e.2 Sport (Спорт), `features/sports/SportsBoard.kt`

In TV-box mode `SportsHubScreen` shows **only the board** (`val simple = TvBoxNav` → `SportsBoard(...)`, `features/sports/SportsHubScreen.kt:181-195`). The hub's chips, leagues, «Сейчас в эфире» cards, «Скоро» row and the hold-OK follow/remind menu are **inactive**. `SportsMatchScreen`, `SportsLeagueScreen` and `SportsFilterPanel` are reachable only from the player's «Матч» button (`playback.pendingMatch`, `features/sports/SportsScreen.kt:46-55`); with the board, OK plays a channel directly. They are lower priority and not specified here.

Data: every sport programme on now on the user's channels (`state.onNow`, from the guide). The board waits 3 s before it says "nothing" (`SKELETON_MS = 3_000`, `SportsHubScreen.kt:183-185, 603`).

##### Layout (`SportsBoard.kt:112-184`)
| Part | Geometry |
|---|---|
| Board width | `gridWidth = W × 1560/1920` = **1560**. Side margin `side = (W − 1560)/2` = **180** on both sides. |
| Header block | Padding left/right 180, top **36**. Does not scroll. |
| Title | «Спорт» + « 76». **46/800**, letter-spacing −1 mpx, one line. «Спорт» in `Text`; the count in **accent** (`#F2F0ED` for INK), bottom-aligned. The count animates (count-up/down) over **500 ms** with FastOutSlowIn (`SportsBoard.kt:233-246`). |
| Search row | Padding top **10**, gap **12**, vertically centred: `StageSearchField` (weight 1, height 48) · «Все страны ▾» `StageTool` boxed, icon `LANGUAGE`, max width 360, **only if more than one country** · «По релевантности ▾» `StageTool` boxed, icon `SORT` (`SportsBoard.kt:121-140`). |
| Filter buttons | Only if there is more than 1 sport. Padding top **14**, gap **8**, **all equal width** (`weight 1`) across the 1560 width. Buttons in order: «Все N» (all items), then up to 7 sports sorted by item count (descending). If there are more than 7 sports, 6 are shown plus «Ещё N» (the sum of the rest) (`SportsBoard.kt:106-109, 142-160, 220-221`). |
| Grid | `LazyVerticalGrid`, **2 columns**, padding left/right 180, top **14**, bottom **110**. Column gap **20**, row gap **10**. Scrolls on its own (`SportsBoard.kt:162-168`). |

##### Components
- **Search field** (`ui/stage/StageControls.kt:59-197`, TV-box branch): height 48, **radius 4**. Idle fill `ControlFill` (ink 6 %). Focused or editing fill **`#E0E2E4`**, with text/icon `#121212` and placeholder `#121212` at 60 %. Padding 18 left/right, gap 12, icon `SEARCH` 20 (`Dim` when idle). Placeholder 18/400 `Dim`; typed text 18/500. The caret is `#121212` when lit, the accent otherwise. OK opens the keyboard (edit mode). Back ends editing and returns to the field. ▲/▼ while editing end editing and move focus up/down. The IME action «Search» ends editing. In the screenshot the field is lit because it holds focus on entry.
- **Boxed StageTool** (`ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:441-490`): height 48, idle fill `ControlFill` radius 4, padding 16, gap 9. Icon 20, text/value 18/700. The icon and «▾» (`CHEVRON_DOWN` 20) are `Muted`; the value is `Text`. Focused: the light bar `#E0E2E4` plus an inner box of `Text` 9 % (on the light palette, so `#121212` 9 %), and the content is `#121212`.
- **Filter button** `StageTab(centered = true)` (`StageComponents.kt:493-545`): 1.5 px outline, radius 4. Outline colour: **accent** when chosen (white-cream in dark, see «Все 76» in the screenshot), `Dim` at 45 % when not chosen, none when focused (the light bar shows instead). Padding 14 left/right, 12 top, 12 bottom. Label **20/700** (`Text` when chosen, `Muted` otherwise) and count **19/700** `Text` at 90 %, baseline-aligned, 8 apart, centred. OK toggles that sport on/off (multi-select). «Все» clears the selection. «Ещё» opens a menu.
- **Card** `BoardCard` (`SportsBoard.kt:267-296`): a `StageRow`, height **96**, padding 18 left/right, gap 18, focus = light bar.
  - Logo tile **78 × 48**, background `stageStillBack` `#0F1518` (dark) / ink 6 % (light), radius **10**. Fallback: icon `LIVE_TV` 26 in `Muted` (`StageComponents.kt:1321-1325`).
  - Line 1: «Спорт-label · title» **20/700** `Text`, one line with ellipsis. The separator is `content_epg_bits_separator` « · ».
  - Line 2: «Channel +N · 12:40 – 14:25» **18/400** `Muted` (`+N` = extra channels showing it).
  - Progress `StageProgress(flat)`: **4 high**, padding top 7, full width, round ends. Track = ink 16 %; fill = **ink** (text colour) in TV-box mode, not the accent (`ui/stage/StageControls.kt:255-281`). Fraction = (now − start)/(stop − start).
- **Menus** (centred `GuideMenuHost`):
  - Countries: width 640, header «Страна». The first item «Все страны» is ✓ when none is chosen; then each country (localised name, A–Z) ✓ when chosen, value = item count. **Multi-select: the menu stays open.**
  - Sort: width 560, header «Сортировка». Items «По релевантности / По времени начала / По названию А–Я / По каналу А–Я» (icon `SORT`), ✓ on the current one. Selecting closes the menu.
  - More sports: width 560, header «Ещё». The remaining sports ✓ when chosen, value = count. Multi-select, stays open (`SportsBoard.kt:186-213`).
- **Sorting** (`engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/sports/SportsBoardFilter.kt:72-106`):
  - Relevance = the viewer's country first (ru→RU, uz→UZ+RU, …, plus the device region), then known sport channels (Матч!/Боец/КХЛ/Eurosport/Setanta/…), then more channels first, then earlier start.
  - Time = start, then title. Title = normalised title. Channel = first channel name, then start.
  - With a query, items are ranked by query match first. The query matches the title + channel names; the search tolerates a wrong keyboard layout, any letter case and typos.
- **Empty** (`SportsBoard.kt:253-265`): a full-width grid item, **22/600 `Muted`**, centred, padding top 90. While loading: «Ищем спорт на ваших каналах…» (`sports_board_looking`). With filters on: `sports_board_nothing_filtered`. Otherwise: `sports_empty_nothing`.

##### Focus, D-pad, Back
- Entry from the side menu → the **search field** (`searchFocus = chipFocus`, which is the entry hook, `SportsHubScreen.kt:174-180, 188-193`).
- The header (field, tools, filter row) is one focus group; the grid is another. Standard spatial D-pad: ▼ from the field/tools → filter buttons → cards; ◀/▶ inside the 2-column grid; ◀ from the left column → side menu.
- OK on a card → plays `item.channels.first()` full screen, with the item's channels as the zap list (`SportsHubScreen.kt:194`, `features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:1597-1598`). When the player closes (`active` true again), focus returns to that card. It retries up to 15 frames, 60 ms apart (`SportsBoard.kt:92-102`).
- Hold OK on a card: nothing (no `onLongClick`).
- Back: no handler of its own → shell: «TV-box: Back from anything but the channels goes to the channels» (`OwnTVShell.kt:1053`) → Live TV.
- State: query and sort survive recomposition (`rememberSaveable`). Sport/country filters reset when the screen is left (`remember`) (`SportsBoard.kt:87-91`).

##### Screenshot `03-sport.png` vs code
Matches: title + count in accent, field with lit focus, «Все страны ▾», «По релевантности ▾», 8 equal buttons (Все + 6 sports + Ещё) with «Все 76» outlined in accent, 2-column cards with progress bars. «Единоборства» shows no count because of ellipsis in the equal-width button (code: one line, ellipsis on the label; the count is a separate Text that gets squeezed out).

---

#### 2e.3 Search (Поиск), `features/search/SearchScreen.kt`

Opened from the side menu item «Поиск» (`MainSection.SEARCH`, `features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:1520-1552`).

##### States and geometry (`SearchScreen.kt:300-546, 734-742`)
Horizontal positions are screen fractions: `listX = 64`, `listW = 846`, `panelX = 944`, `panelW = 912`, `centredX = (W − 846)/2 = 537`.

| State | What is shown |
|---|---|
| **Launcher** (query < 2 chars, no intent) | **Rolling caption** at x = fieldX, top **322**, width 846, height 64: **40/800**, letter-spacing −1 mpx, `Text`, centred. Six phrases in order: «Поиск по каналам», «Поиск» (`search_caption_programs`; this is the one in `05-poisk.png`), «Поиск по телегиду», «Поиск по фильмам», «Поиск по сериалам», «В любой раскладке». Each stays **2400 ms**. Change animation: the new phrase slides in from +50 % of its height and fades in; the old one slides out to −50 % and fades out at 2/3 of the time. The slide takes **420 ms** FastOutSlowIn (`SearchScreen.kt:740-782`). The **field** sits at x **537** (centred), top **404**, width 846. Below it, **recent searches** start at top 476 (404 + 72). |
| **Searching** (≥ 2 chars) | The field moves to x **64**, top **118**. Header «Поиск 55» at x 84, top **52**, width 826: both parts **46/800**, −1 mpx, 20 apart, the count in **accent**, counting up over **600 ms** FastOutSlowIn (`SearchScreen.kt:830-851`). Filter buttons at top **192**. Results list at top **252**, bottom padding 80, width 846, item gap 8. The right panel is at x 944, top **120**, width 912. |
| **Intent list** (Continue / Unwatched / Channels, from Home) | Like Searching, but no filter buttons, the list starts at top **192**, and the header shows the intent label instead of the count. |
| Busy and still empty | `SearchingNote` at top **270**, width 846, centred: «Ищем каналы, передачи, фильмы и сериалы…» **22/600 `Muted`**. Below it (18 down) a 320 × 4 bar: track `Dim`, a 30 %-wide `Muted` segment sweeping left→right, **1100 ms linear, infinite** (static at 35 % when not smooth) (`SearchScreen.kt:856-888`). |
| Nothing found | At top **300** (searching) / 230 (intent): title «Ничего не найдено по запросу «q»» **30/800** `Text`, 2 lines max. Body `search_nothing_found_body` **19/400**, line-height 29.45, `Muted`, top 10 (`SearchScreen.kt:890-900`). |

- The field moves between the launcher and results positions by animating both x and top over **360 ms** FastOutSlowIn (`FIELD_RISE_MS`, `SearchScreen.kt:311-334`).
- The field is the same `StageSearchField` as in Sport: height 48, radius 4, lit `#E0E2E4` when focused. Placeholder «Канал, передача или фильм».
- The query runs **250 ms** after the last keystroke (debounce) and needs **at least 2 characters** (`features/search/SearchViewModel.kt:73-76`).

##### Recent searches (`SearchScreen.kt:784-829`)
- Shown only in the launcher, only if there are any. At most **8** (`RECENT_SHOWN`, `SearchViewModel.kt:201`).
- Heading «НЕДАВНИЕ ПОИСКОВЫЕ ЗАПРОСЫ» (12.5/800, +0.13 em, `Dim`, start 20, padding top 8, bottom 6).
- Rows 56 high, gap 6, radius 16 (`StageRadii.Row`) → focus = light bar. Padding 12, gap 14. Plate 58 × 40 with the logo of what the term found, or a `HISTORY` icon 20 in `Muted`. Term **19/600**, `#CDD7D3` in dark / `ItemText` in light.
- Then «Очистить недавние запросы» `StageTool` (`TRASH`), top 10.
- OK on a row → the term becomes the query.

##### Filter buttons (searching only, `SearchScreen.kt:366-385`)
- A row at x = fieldX, top 192, width 846, gap 8.
- Four `StageTab(centered)` buttons in `SearchKind` order: «Прямой эфир N» (LIVE), «Телепередачи N», «Фильмы N», «Сериалы N». Each button's width weight = label length + count length + 4 characters, so wider labels get wider buttons.
- Multi-select: OK toggles a kind. None on = show everything.

##### Results list (`SearchScreen.kt:387-487, 558-595, 615-657`)
- Grouped when more than one kind is shown. Order: **Прямой эфир → Телепередачи → Фильмы → Сериалы**.
- Group heading «ПРЯМОЙ ЭФИР · 9»: 12.5/800, +0.13 em, `Dim`, start 20, top 18 (except the first).
- In grouped mode only the **first 4 channels** are shown, then a `StageTool` «Все N каналов» (`LIST`, top 10). OK on it switches to the LIVE filter only and focuses the first channel.
- Result row `SearchRow`: `StageRow`, **84** high, padding 18, gap 18, focus = light bar.
  - Leading: channel/programme → plate **76 × 54** radius 12; movie/series → poster **50 × 74** radius 8 on ink 6 % (fallback icon `MOVIES`/`SERIES` 22 `Dim`).
  - Title **21/700** (`Text`; `FxText` when focused, which is `#121212` on the bar).
  - 4 below: line **16/400** `Muted` (`FxLine` when focused).
  - Right side, 10 apart: provider tags (`StageTag`) + playlist mark.
- Lines:
  - channel = «category · number · now playing»
  - programme = «Channel · 12:00 – 13:00»
  - movie/series = «2005 · ★ 6.2 · Category» (★ in `#FFCC4D`)
- **Right panel**, from the focused result (or the first result):
  - channel or programme → Live TV's `LiveStagePane` (preview video + now/next + schedule; owned by section 2b). The preview plays only while a channel row (or its schedule) has focus, after **700 ms** (`SearchScreen.kt:209-224`).
  - movie/series → `PosterPanel` (`SearchScreen.kt:659-732`):
    - Frame: 16:9 frame, radius 28, black, shadow 55 % blur 80 dy 30.
    - Picture: the backdrop (cropped, aligned −0.4 vertically). Without a backdrop: the poster blurred + black 45 %, with the sharp poster at the right (end 40, top 36, height frame − 72, radius 14).
    - Overlays: bottom shade black 72 %. Tag «ФИЛЬМ»/«СЕРИАЛ» 13/800 +0.05 em, white on black 55 %, radius 6, padding 7/3, at 24/22. Title-art logo (height 110, 60 % width) or the title 44/800 white at 26/20.
    - Below the frame (top 34): the meta line (19), plot 19/400 with line-height 1.55, 3 lines, top 18; cast (5).

##### D-pad, OK, Back (`SearchScreen.kt:188-298, 335-345, 396-405, 444-451`)
- On open, focus goes to the **field**, or back to the row the user left when returning from a result (`vm.lastFocusKey`, retried 30 × 100 ms).
- Field: OK starts typing (system keyboard). ▼ → the first active filter button (searching), or the first recent row (launcher). ▲/▼ while typing end typing and move focus. Back while typing ends typing.
- ▲ out of the results list → the first active filter button (not the nearest one).
- ▶ on a channel row → the schedule list in the right pane, if there is a next programme.
- OK on a result:
  - channel/programme → **plays the channel full screen** (`playChannelById`).
  - movie → Movies with that movie revealed.
  - series → its series page.
  - Back from Movies/Series returns to Search once (`searchReturn`, `OwnTVShell.kt:1045-1046`).
- Hold OK: nothing (`onLongClick = {}`).
- Back (when a query or intent is active): clears the query, intent and filters and focuses the field. It is armed only **700 ms** after the player closes (`BACK_REARM_MS`). Otherwise Back goes to the shell → Live TV (`OwnTVShell.kt:1053`).

##### Screenshot `05-poisk.png` vs code
The launcher state with no recent searches: the caption «Поиск» (one of the six rolling phrases, not a static title), the centred field lit with focus, and nothing else. Caption top 322 / field top 404 in mpx correspond to about 358 / 449 px in the 111 % screenshot, which matches what is visible (caption ≈ 375–415, field 449–501).


### 2f. Settings

Part A: the sheet itself, row types, the section list, and the sections Быстрые настройки, Мой профиль, Источники каналов, Внешний вид, Вид экрана, Категории. The other sections (Настройки плеера … О приложении) are described in part B.

Path prefixes used below: `A/` = `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/`, `C/` = `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/`. Android tree = origin/main at commit `3ad3636`. Strings are given as `ru` text (resource key). Every key is in table 3f.

> **Scale warning (read first).** The screenshots were taken at the default UI zoom of **100 %** (`C/theme/UiZoom.kt:11` `DEFAULT = 100`). `mpx` is defined at **90 %** zoom (`A/ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:15-23`). So on the screenshots every mpx size is **×1.111**: group lines are 80 mpx in code and measure ≈89 px; group-page rows are 92 mpx in code and measure ≈102 px; the 30 mpx side inset measures ≈33 px. The **sheet width is a fraction of the screen** (37 %) and does not scale: it is 710 px on both. If the web stage uses 1 mpx = 1 px, the text and rows come out about 10 % smaller than on the screenshots while the sheet stays the same width. To match the screenshots pixel for pixel, use 1 mpx = 1.111 px (= zoom 100 %). Pick one and apply it everywhere. See risks.

#### 2f.1 Panel frame (the "sheet")

**Live code path.** `SettingsAsSidePanel = true` (`A/features/settings/SettingsStage.kt:378`). Every settings page is drawn by `StageSettingsPage` → `SettingsSidePage` → `SettingsSheet` (`SettingsStage.kt:267-286, 382-453, 460-552`). The wide two-column "Stage" layout (rows x 50–1110 plus a help panel at x 1160) in `SettingsStage.kt:288-369` is **dead code on TV**. Do not build it. The More list (`A/features/more/MoreScreen.kt:179-228`) is also hidden while `sheetOnly`: it is moved off-screen and cannot take focus (`MoreScreen.kt:175-182`). The legacy `SettingsGroupCard` 3-column card grid (`A/features/more/MorePages.kt:244-288`) is not used either.

**Where it lives.** More and Settings are one layer over the channel screen: `OwnTVShell.kt:1385-1459` (`A/features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt`). The screen behind (Home, "Ваши каналы") stays drawn and **is not dimmed**: "No grey wash" (`SettingsStage.kt:499-504`). A tap or click outside the sheet acts as Back (same lines).

**Geometry** (`SettingsStage.kt:505-546`, `561-578`):

| Property | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Alignment | right edge, full height (`Alignment.CenterEnd`, `fillMaxHeight`) | :505, :516 |
| Width | `screenW × 0.37` (710.4 px at 1920), at least `SideMinWidth = 600 mpx`; `wide = true` pages (backup, sync, log) use `× 0.64` | :515, :567 |
| Corners | top-left and bottom-left `20 mpx`; right corners square | :517 |
| Fill | dark: `#232629` (`SideFill`), light theme: `#FFFFFF` | :561, :577 |
| Header band colour | `#333639` / `#F2F3F5` is defined (`sheetHeaderFill`) but **never used**: no band behind the title | :563-564, :578 |
| Title row | padding start 30, end 24, top 36, bottom 14 mpx; text `stageText(32, 800)`, letterSpacing −1/46 em, colour `StageColors.Text`, 1 line, ellipsis; optional `headerTools` on the right (About puts its version there, 32/800 Muted, `MoreScreen.kt:343`) | :529-536 |
| Content | column with `SideFocusInset = 0` horizontal padding: rows and their focus bar run edge to edge across the sheet | :540-545, :570 |
| Rows column | `verticalScroll`, padding top 12, bottom 40, spacing 0 | :406-415 |
| Scrolling | `edgeScrollSpec`: scroll only as far as needed to keep **one row of room** ahead of the focused row (`A/features/live/LiveStage.kt:536-551`) | :405 |

Screenshot check (10-nastroyki.png): sheet left edge x = 1210 (1920 − 710). Title "Настройки" baseline ≈ y 64. First line focus bar from y 115 to 204. Matches the code × 1.111.

**Colours inside the sheet** (`SettingsStage.kt:506-511`):
- Palette: the app's own (`StagePalette.Dark` normally, `StagePalette.Light` in the light theme). The code comment says "Always the dark grey sheet, whatever the app's theme", but the code switches to Light/white. **Code wins**: light theme → white sheet.
- Accent override: `StageAccent(SkMateBlue #0086FC, onAccent = White, focus = White)` (`A/ui/brand/SkMateWordmark.kt:16`). So switches ON, choice ticks and stepper values are SkMate blue whatever the user's accent.
- `LocalInSettingsSheet = true`: every FX-style element gets the grey focus bar (below).

**Focus look inside the sheet** (`A/ui/stage/StageComponents.kt:188-243, 254, 263, 266`):
- Focused row = a flat bar `#E0E2E4` (`SheetFocusFill`, dark theme) or `#E7E8EA` (`LightFocusFill`, light theme). Shape `RoundedCornerShape(4 mpx)` (`FocusBarShape`). No ring, no shadow, no scale (FX lift = 1.0, `StageComponents.kt:270-287`).
- While focused, the content is redrawn with **`StagePalette.Light`**: title `#121212`, muted line becomes `fxLine` = `#343433` (`SettingsStage.kt:972, 1007`), icon muted `#474645`. The accent becomes SkMate blue (accent luminance > 0.6 → `SkMateBlue`, `StageComponents.kt:232-236, 1365`). Values on a focused row are always brand blue (`focusedBlue`, `SettingsStage.kt:1632-1641`).
- The bar appears and disappears at once: no animation on the bar. Only the switch knob animates (2f.2).
- Text that does not fit scrolls while focused: `basicMarquee`, 900 ms initial and repeat delay, endless; overflow `Clip` while focused, `Ellipsis` otherwise (`A/ui/components/FocusMarquee.kt:11-25`). Screenshot 20-bystrye-1 shows the focused line "…дить выбранный канал в прямом эфире" in mid-scroll.
- Key hints (`StageKeyHints`) are **never drawn** on TV: `if (TvBoxNav) return` (`A/ui/stage/StageControls.kt:202`; `TvBoxNav = true`, `OwnTVShell.kt:2609`).
- The context panel (`SettingHelp`, `SETTING_HELP` texts) is **not drawn** in the sheet. `SettingsSidePage` never renders `panel.help` (`SettingsStage.kt:382-453`); only the panel *editors* (choice lists, steppers) are shown. The web can skip all help texts.

**Initial focus.** When the sheet has no focus, it takes focus on its first focusable after `SHEET_FIRST_FOCUS_MS = 250 ms`, or `SHEET_REFOCUS_MS = 700 ms` after the first time (`SettingsStage.kt:484-494, 1647-1648`). If focus is lost inside the sheet it comes back to the first line (focusGroup, :537-545).

**Animations** (exact):

| What | Type | Duration / easing | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Opening More/Settings from the side menu | whole layer: `translationX = (1 − p) × screenWidth` and `alpha = p` | `SheetSlideMs = 300 ms` × motion speed (Fast 0.6, Normal 1.0, Slow 1.5), `FastOutSlowInEasing` = cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1); 0 ms when animations are off or the device is throttled | `OwnTVShell.kt:1388-1406, 2616`; `A/ui/theme/MotionPrefs.kt:14, 113` |
| Closing (Back from the list) | same tween back to 0 (slides out to the right and fades) | same | same |
| Moving list → group page → sub-page and back | **none**: the page is swapped at once | `SideInMs = 0` ("Settings change pages at once: no slide, no fade") | `SettingsStage.kt:572-574, 522-526` |
| Choice list / stepper opening over the rows | `AnimatedVisibility` slide-from-right 1/3 + fade, but with `SideInMs = 0` → **instant** | `SettingsStage.kt:417-441` |
| Back to the group list | appears in place, same scroll offset, nothing slides (`slideIn = !returning`) | `MorePages.kt:297-303` |
| Switch knob | knob x and track colour interpolate | 200 ms × speed, FastOutSlowIn; 0 ms on "lite" devices | `StageComponents.kt:1295-1317` |

**Back behaviour, by depth:**

| Depth | Back does | Source |
|---|---|---|
| 0. Section list ("Настройки") | leaves More: section → Live TV (`TvBoxNav`), the sheet slides out | `MoreScreen.kt:157-162, 106-110`; `OwnTVShell.kt:1451` |
| 0b. A page opened from the list's lower block (Резервные копии, Синхронизация ТВ, Пульт Telegram, Как пользоваться, О приложении) | back to the list, focus on that line | `MoreScreen.kt:160, 128-135`; `MorePages.kt:235-236, 319-321` |
| 1. Group page (e.g. "Внешний вид") | back to the list (More), focus on the group's line, list at its old scroll | `SettingsScreen.kt:448` (`A/features/shell/components/SettingsScreen.kt`); `OwnTVShell.kt:1420`; `MorePages.kt:229-233, 297-303` |
| 1, search has text | clears the query, focus to the search field | `SettingsScreen.kt:215-218` |
| 1, reached through a Quick pin | back to Quick on that pin | `SettingsScreen.kt:395-400` |
| 2. Sub-page (Плейлисты, Источники EPG, Стекло и фон, Шрифты…, Дата, время и погода, Кнопки пульта, Ширина…, Просмотр и списки, Меню долгого нажатия, Настройка категорий…) | back to the group page, focus on the row that opened it | `StageFullPage` BackHandler `SettingsStage.kt:1197`; `SettingsScreen.kt:403, 412-444, 958-993` |
| Choice list / stepper open in the sheet | closes it, focus back to the row that opened it | `SettingsStage.kt:636-639, 361, 439` |
| Popup (StagePopup, StageConfirm, colour picker…) | closes it, focus back to the opener row at the same scroll | `SettingsScreen.kt:273-288`; `StagePopup.kt:79` (`A/ui/stage/StagePopup.kt`) |

**D-pad in the sheet:** ▲ ▼ move between rows. OK activates. ◀ ▶ change the value **only** on stepper or segmented rows (`onStep`, `SettingsStage.kt:933-945`); on other rows they do nothing (there is nothing focusable to the left: the More list has `canFocus = false`). Hold OK (long press) on a group-page row opens the pin menu (2f.2 "Hold-OK menu"). Inside a choice list, ◀ closes it like Back (`SettingsStage.kt:685-687`).

#### 2f.2 Row types

##### a) Section line in the list (`GroupLine`) — `MorePages.kt:326-335`
- `StageSurface(radius 0, FX)`, full width, **height 80 mpx**.
- Row padding horizontal 30, gap 22, vertically centred.
- Icon 32 mpx, colour `StageColors.Text` at **85 %** alpha (100 % when focused).
- Title `stageText(25, 700)`, `StageColors.Text`, 1 line, ellipsis. No value, no chevron.
- Divider before the lower block: padding horizontal 24, vertical 10; height 1 mpx; `Text` at 12 % (`MorePages.kt:318`).

##### b) Setting row in the sheet (`StageSettingRow`, side mode) — `SettingsStage.kt:884-990`
```
|<-30->[icon 38][18][ title 26/600 ........ (•pin 7) ][18][ value ]<-30->|   min height 92
                     [ line 20/500, top 3 ]                               
```
- Height `heightIn(min = 92 mpx)`: grows if needed (:932).
- Padding horizontal **30**, gap 18 (:950-951).
- Icon 38 mpx, `StageColors.Muted` (:954).
- Title `stageText(26, 600)`, colour `Text` (`Dim` when disabled), 1 line, marquee when focused (:957-964).
- Pinned-to-Quick mark: a 7 mpx accent dot after the title (:965). Not drawn on the Quick page itself (`SettingsScreen.kt:1399-1400`).
- **Second line** = the current value for Choice / Opens / Segmented / Saved / Action rows (`sideValueLine`, :992-1001). Otherwise it is the row's description. `stageText(20, 500)`, `Muted` (focused: `fxLine` → `#343433` on the light bar), padding top 3, 1 line, marquee when focused (:968-975).
- **Right side** shows only Switch, Stepper and Custom values (`sideKeepsValue`, :1003-1005). A Choice shows **no ▾** and an Opens shows **no ›** in the sheet: the value is the second line instead. Screenshot 23 confirms: "Тема / Тёмная", no chevron.
- Disabled row (`enabled = false`): title Dim, not clickable (:903, :960).
- `marked` / `keepPanel` / the row whose choice list is open: idle background `Text` at 7 %, shape 4 mpx (:927).
- Long OK: `onLongClick`. A release within 800 ms after a long press does not also click (:912-921).

##### c) Value types (`SettingValue`, `SettingsStage.kt:92-110`; drawn by `SettingValueView` :1009-1039)
| Type | Sheet appearance | Keys |
|---|---|---|
| `Switch(on)` | 56×32 switch on the right (below) | OK toggles |
| `Choice(text)` | text as the second line | OK opens a choice list (in the sheet) |
| `Opens(text?)` | text (if any) as the second line | OK opens a sub-page or popup |
| `Stepper(text)` | "− 100% +" box on the right (below) | ◀ ▶ step, OK opens a big stepper or popup |
| `Segmented(options, i)` | **only the chosen option's label as the second line** (the segmented control itself is not drawn in the sheet) | ◀ ▶ previous/next (clamped or cycling per row), OK = next |
| `Saved(text, any)` | text as the second line | OK |
| `Action(text)` | text as the second line | OK runs it |
| `Custom{…}` | drawn on the right as given (colour dot + hex, tags…) | per row |

Outside the sheet (not used on TV) Choice is accent text + ▾ 20 mpx and Opens is accent text + › 20 mpx (:1016-1025).

**Switch** (`StageSwitch`, `StageComponents.kt:1293-1317`): track 56×32 mpx, radius 16. Track colour = lerp(`Ink` 14 %, accent, t), where accent = SkMate blue in the sheet. Knob 24 mpx circle at x = 4 + 24·t, y = 4. Knob colour lerp(`knobOff`, White, t): `knobOff` is `#D0D3D6` in dark and `#FFFFFF` in light. 200 ms animation. On a focused row the palette is Light, so the off track becomes `#121212` at 14 % with a white knob (screenshot 20-bystrye-1, first row). Display only: the row owns focus and OK flips it.

**Stepper box** (`StageStepper`, `StageComponents.kt:1258-1288`): background `ControlFill` (= `Ink` at 6 %), radius 13, padding 4, gap 4. "−" and "+" are 34×34 boxes, text `stageText(20, 700)` Muted; they take mouse taps only, never focus. Value `stageText(18, 700)` Text, centred, min width 64.

**Segmented, wide layout only** (not visible in the sheet, kept for completeness): `ControlFill` capsule radius 15, padding 4; options 40 high, padding horizontal 14, text 17/700; the chosen pill (`segmentOn` = white 13 % dark / white light, radius 11) slides 160 ms (`SettingsStage.kt:1041-1083`).

##### d) Group heading inside a page (`StageSettingsHeading`) — `SettingsStage.kt:864-882`
`stageText(24, 800)`, `StageColors.Text`, 1 line. Padding start 22, top 6 (first heading of a page) or 26, bottom 10. Not focusable. No count is drawn (the parameter is ignored). Examples: "Источники", "Телегид и архив", "Тема и цвета".

##### e) Note text (empty states) — `StageSettingsNote` `SettingsStage.kt:1109-1116`
Padding horizontal 22, vertical 16. Title 21/700 Text, body 17/500 Muted, top 6.

##### f) Text field row (`StageFieldRow`) — `SettingsStage.kt:1352-1523`
- In the sheet: min height 92, padding horizontal 30, icon 38, label 26/600; the value line 20/500, SemiBold. Outside the sheet (popups): height 90, padding 22, icon 32, label 21/700, value 19.
- Empty → placeholder in `Dim`.
- While focused and not editing, "Изменить" (`common_edit`) 18/700 in **SkMate blue** on the right.
- OK starts editing: the field gets focus and the keyboard opens. Back (key up) or IME Done ends editing and returns focus to the row. Typing a character on a hardware keyboard starts editing at once.
- Password: dots; ▶ toggles show/hide. Without a panel the row shows "▶ Показать/Скрыть" 18/700 Muted.
- While editing the row keeps the focused look (`highlighted`).

##### g) Choice list opened in the sheet (`PanelChoices` inside `SettingPanelEditor`) — `SettingsStage.kt:417-441, 634-734, 1643-1680`
Every `PickerDialog` call on a settings page opens here, **not as a popup** (`A/features/settings/VideoPlayerSettingsScreen.kt:2166-2168`).
- Overlay over the rows area (under the sheet title), background = sheet fill. Padding horizontal 24, top 20, bottom 40. Scrollable.
- Heading: the setting's name `stageText(22, 700)` Muted, max 2 lines, padding start/end 30, top 4. Then a 12 mpx gap (:641-657).
- Optional search field (searchable lists): `StageSearchField`, bottom 10.
- Options: LazyColumn, spacing 2. Each is a `StageSurface(radius 14, FX)` → grey bar on focus, **min height 84**. Row padding horizontal 30, vertical 8, gap 12. Label `stageText(24, 600)`, `Text` if chosen or focused, else `Muted`, marquee when focused. Optional description `stageText(18, 500)` Muted, max 2 lines. Optional preview under it (top 8). Optional "РЕКОМЕНДУЕТСЯ" tag (`StageTag`). Then the tick.
- Tick (`StagePopupRadio`, `StagePopup.kt:270-288`): a 24 mpx slot; only the chosen row shows a `CHECK` icon 24 mpx. Colour: SkMate blue on a light palette (which includes the focused bar), white on a focused dark row, else accent (= SkMate blue in the sheet).
- Focus: lands on the current option 60 ms after opening. The list starts scrolled to (selected − 2). Focus cannot leave the list (`trapAllFocusExit`).
- Keys: ▲ ▼ move, OK picks (the callers then close it), ◀ or Back close without change. Focus returns to the opening row (`view.post`).

##### h) Big stepper opened in the sheet (`PanelStepper`) — `SettingsStage.kt:736-787`
- One `StageSurface(radius 18, FX)` 84 high, full width.
- Inside, padding horizontal 22: left chevron (`CHEVRON` rotated 180°) 26 Muted · value `stageText(36, 800)` in accent (SkMate blue), centred · right chevron 26 Muted.
- Optional preview above (bottom 18): Popup size shows `PopupSizeSample` (:808-834).
- ◀ ▶ step at once. In the sheet the change applies live and there are **no Reset/Done buttons** (:774-779). OK = done. Back = close. For "confirm" steppers (guide days) Back cancels and OK keeps (`A/ui/components/DayStepperDialog.kt:35-47`).

##### i) Popups used from part A (Stage popup on TV) — `StagePopup.kt:63-124`
- Full-screen scrim `StageColors.Scrim` (dark `rgba(4,5,6,0.55)`, light `rgba(18,18,18,0.30)`). The popup is centred; while the TV keyboard is up it moves to the top.
- Card: `solidCard(30)` on TV (`StagePopup.kt:100`; `StageComponents.kt:1326-1335`): fill `#232629` (dark) or white (light), shadow black 50 % (light 16 %), blur 40, y offset 14, radius 30. Width default 880 mpx (pickers 760 or 880, text input 820), never wider than the screen minus 2×40.
- Padding horizontal 12, vertical 30. Heading block padding horizontal 18:
  - Eyebrow `stageText(15, 800, +0.12em)` Dim. Settings pages provide "НАСТРОЙКИ · ВНЕШНИЙ ВИД" style text (`settings_breadcrumb_eyebrow` "%1$s · %2$s", upper-cased; `SettingsStage.kt:262-266`).
  - Title `stageText(38, 800, −0.5 mpx)` Text, max 2 lines, top 6.
  - Body `stageText(18, 400)` line-height 1.45, Muted, top 10. Then 22 mpx.
- Buttons row: top 24, start/end 18, gap 14, right-aligned.
- Back or a tap outside closes. Focus is trapped inside.
- **Popup option row** (`StagePopupOption`, `StagePopup.kt:202-268, 382-386`): `StageSurface(radius 18, FX)` → grey bar on focus. Min height 84 (96 with subtitle). Padding horizontal 24, vertical 10, gap 18. Title 25/600 (danger colour, or accent when `chosen` and not focused). Subtitle 19/500 Muted, max 2. Value "`value` ›" 18/700 in accent (SkMate blue when focused).
- **Buttons** (`StageButton`, `StageComponents.kt:341-389`): on TV every button is the same slab. Idle `ControlFill` (ink 6 %) with 4 mpx corners. Focused = the grey bar with dark text. Text `Text` colour. In popups height 56 and text 19/700, inside sheet editors 52 / 18. Padding horizontal 30, gap 12. "tinted" has no effect on TV.
- **Confirm** (`StageConfirm`, `StagePopup.kt:126-153`): width 760, buttons Cancel + confirm; `focusCancel` for destructive ones.
- **Notice** (`StageNotice` :155-170): one OK button.
- **Hold-OK menu** (`SettingsRowMenu`, `SettingsScreen.kt:2827-2877`; `StageMenu` `StageComponents.kt:690-725`): scrim, centred `StageMenu` 520 wide, `solidCard(28)`, padding 16/16/22/16. Header 23/800 (padding 12, bottom 12). Items `StageMenuItem`: on TV 76 high, text 23/600, icon 28, hairline under each (`Text` 8 %, inset 14) (`StageComponents.kt:747-805, 1367-1369`). Items: "Закрепить в Быстрые" / "Открепить от Быстрых" (`settings_row_menu_pin` / `_unpin`, icon SPARKLE); on Quick also "Выше" / "Ниже" (`settings_row_menu_move_up` / `_down`, CHEVRON_UP / CHEVRON_DOWN). Items that cannot apply are left out.
- **Row actions popup** (`RowActionsPopup`, `SettingsStage.kt:1326-1347`): `StagePopup` width 600, no eyebrow, title = item name. One `StagePopupOption` per action, leading icon 28 (danger colour for Delete). OK runs the action and closes.
- **Centered form window** (`StageFormPage` windowed / `CenterWindow`, `SettingsStage.kt:1220-1324`): scrim `#000` at 67 % (dark) or 40 % (light); card radius 26, fill = sheet fill, padding 40 / 34, width 1000 (form) or 820, max height 92 %. Breadcrumb 30/700 Dim, "›" separator, title 30/800. Used by the add / edit playlist and guide forms (form contents: see 2a wizard part).

Matches screenshots 10/11. In the screenshots the REC icon for "Мультиэкран" looks like a circular arrow and EXPAND for "Качество картинки" like two diagonal arrows. Use the shared icon set (part 1).

Focus on entry: line 1, or the line last opened (`MorePages.kt:313, 320`). The list keeps its scroll offset when coming back (`MorePages.kt:229-230, 301-302`).

Disagreement: the comment says "groups as plain lines (Search first)" (`MorePages.kt:291-294`), but **no search line is drawn**. A group page also has no search field: the field is drawn only when there is no parent crumb (`SettingsStage.kt:400-401`), i.e. on the search page, which on TV is reachable only from code paths not on screen. Follow the code: no search.

**Group page title** = the same short name (`pageTitleRes` → `shortRes`, `SettingsScreen.kt:3240-3242`). The count ("10 настроек") is passed but **not drawn** in the sheet (`SettingsSidePage` ignores `count`, `SettingsStage.kt:382-399`).

**How a group page is built** (`SettingsScreen.kt:1308-1411`):
- Quick: the pinned rows, in pin order (:899-916).
- Profile: `ProfileSettingsRows()` (:1388).
- Sources, Appearance, Layout, App: split into parts (`partDefs`, :3163-3211); each part gets a `StageSettingsHeading` and its rows (:1375-1380).
- Content: plain rows (:1391-1406).
- Player, Picture, Live, Watching: `VideoPlayerGroupRows` (part B).

Rows removed from the TV build: `vod_layout`, `tab_METADATA`, `tab_OPEN_SUBTITLES`, `tab_HOME`, `app_icon`, `brand_accent` (`RemovedRootKeys`, :3150-3156). Do not build them.

On entry to a group, focus goes to the page's first row (:949-955, :995-1003). Every row on group pages can be held (OK long press) to pin to or unpin from Quick (:1397-1405, :1415-1451).

#### 2f.4 Быстрые настройки (Quick) — screenshots 20-bystrye-1/2

Rows = `quickPinnedKeys`. Default `["quick_live_preview","quick_preview_sound","quick_channel_numbers","quick_hdr","quick_autoplay","quick_check_update"]` (`C/settings/SettingsRepository.kt:3486-3490, 2244-2247`). The user can add any row by holding OK on it, and reorder pins (Выше/Ниже). Empty Quick → note "Пока ничего не закреплено" / "Удерживайте OK на любой настройке и выберите «Закрепить в Быстрые»." (`SettingsScreen.kt:1390`). On Quick the pin dot is not drawn.

Default rows. All are `Switch` (chip On/Off → Switch, `SettingsScreen.kt:2800-2806`); the second line = `SETTING_LINE` (`A/features/settings/SettingsHelpTable.kt:14-19`).

| # | Icon | Title | Line | Default | OK does | Code |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | LIVE_TV | Предпросмотр эфира (`settings_quick_live_preview`) | Воспроизводить выбранный канал в прямом эфире (`settings_line_quick_live_preview`) | ON unless the device is low-spec (`livePreviewDefault = !lowSpecDevice`, Repo:2447-2451) | toggle. Turning ON while the Live preview panel is hidden shows a notice popup "`settings_live_preview_panel_hidden_title`" (OK only) instead (`SettingsScreen.kt:354-364, 2316-2323`) | SettingsScreen.kt:458-465 |
| 2 | AUDIO | Звук предпросмотра (`settings_quick_preview_sound`) | Звук в живом превью (`settings_line_preview_sound`) | OFF (Repo:2481) | toggle | :466-472 |
| 3 | LIVE_TV | Номера каналов (`settings_quick_channel_numbers`) | Наберите номер на пульте, чтобы переключить | ON (Repo:1685) | toggle direct tune | :473-479 |
| 4 | VIDEO | Предпочитать SDR (`settings_quick_prefer_sdr`) | SDR, если в потоке есть оба (`settings_line_prefer_sdr`) | OFF (Repo:2492) | toggle | :480-486 |
| 5 | AUTOPLAY_NEXT | Автовоспроизведение (`settings_quick_autoplay`) | Следующая серия запускается сама | ON (Repo:1855) | toggle | :487-493 |
| 6 | DOWNLOADS | Проверить обновление (`settings_quick_check_update`) | При запуске приложения (`settings_line_when_app_opens`) | ON (Repo:1159) | toggle update check at start | :494-500 |

Screenshot state: 1 off, 2 off, 3 on, 4 off, 5 on, 6 on. Row 1 being off on the emulator is consistent with the low-spec default.

A pinned row from another group shows the same title, line and value as in its home group. Pinned player rows (`vp_*`) show "Закреплено из: %1$s" (`settings_pinned_from`) as their line (:885-897, :2807-2809). OK on a pinned toggle flips it in place; other pins jump to their home group on that row (:876-897, :384-394).

#### 2f.5 Мой профиль (Profile) — screenshots 21-profil-1/2

`A/features/settings/ProfileSettingsRows.kt:46-153`.

1. **One row per profile.** Icon PERSON. Title = profile name.
   - Line = joined by " · " (`content_epg_bits_separator`): "Используется" (if active) · "Детский режим" (if kids) · "Блокировка PIN-кодом" or "Без блокировки" (:79-85).
   - Right side, a `Custom` value with gap 8: tag "ИСПОЛЬЗУЕТСЯ" (accent tint), tag "ДЕТСКИЙ" (`profiles_kids_tag`), tag "PIN" (`profiles_pin`), and a › (CHEVRON 20 Muted) when not in use (:94-101).
   - Tags (`StageTag`, `StageComponents.kt:596-621`): text `stageText(13, 800, +0.05em)`, padding 7 / 3, radius 6.
     - Plain tag: fill `Ink` at 8 %, text `TagText` (`#C9CDD1`).
     - Tinted tag on TV: no fill, a **1.5 mpx outline** in the tint, text in the tint. "ИСПОЛЬЗУЕТСЯ" = SkMate-blue outline and blue text (screenshot 21-profil-1).
   - OK → profile editor popup. Hold OK (only when there is more than one profile) → confirm "`profiles_delete_title`" / "`profiles_delete_message`" with buttons Отмена (focused first) and Удалить (`ManageSourcesScreen.kt:601-619`).
2. **"Добавить профиль"** (`settings_profile_add`), icon SPARKLE, line "Зритель со своим избранным, историей и настройками" (`settings_profile_add_line`), no value. OK → editor in create mode (:108-116).

After the editor or confirm closes, focus returns to the same row (or its neighbour after a delete) (:56-68).

**Profile editor popup** (`A/features/profiles/ProfileComponents.kt:110-226`), drawn by `ProfileScrim` (geometry not read in detail; follows the popup rules above):
- Title "Новый профиль" / "Изменить профиль" (`profiles_new` / `profiles_edit`).
- Name field (`StageFieldRow`, PENCIL, `profiles_name`, placeholder `profiles_name_hint`). A duplicate name shows `profiles_name_taken` 17/600 Danger.
- Label "АВАТАР" (`profiles_avatar`, `StagePopupLabel` 14/800 +0.12em Dim).
- Horizontal row of avatars: 88 mpx circles, FX focus, chosen one ringed 3 mpx accent, image 70 mpx, gap 14.
- Option "`profiles_avatar_own_picture`" (IMAGE icon or the picture 44 mpx).
- Divider.
- Switch row "`profiles_kids`" / "`profiles_kids_description`".
- (Existing PIN) switch row "`profiles_remove_pin`" / "`profiles_no_pin`".
- PIN field (password, digits only, 4–6): "`profiles_change_pin`" or "`profiles_optional_pin`", placeholder `profiles_pin_digits`.
- Buttons Отмена and Создать / Сохранить (`StagePopupButton`), disabled while invalid.

Code vs screenshot: in the screenshot rows 3–8 show the values as second lines ("Выкл.", "Спрашивать о переключении", "за 5 мин", "7 дней", "Автоматически", "Выкл."), not the `SETTING_LINE` descriptions. That matches `sideValueLine` (values replace descriptions in the sheet).

**Catch-up form** (in the sheet editor, `SettingsScreen.kt:2595-2660`):
1. Choice pills (`StageMenuChoice`, `StageComponents.kt:1195-1239`): Автоматически / Устройство / Вручную (`settings_catchup_timezone_automatic`, `_device`, `settings_manual`).
   - Each pill 46 high, radius 13, gap 8, text 17/700. Idle `ControlFill`, Muted text. Chosen: accent 18 % fill, 1.5 mpx accent 60 % ring, accent text. Focused: grey bar.
   - Focus starts on the chosen pill.
2. If Manual: an offset row 72 high, radius 18, FX. Chevrons 24, value 30/800 accent (e.g. "UTC+05:00"). ◀▶ ±15 min, −12 h … +14 h (Repo:1064-1067).
3. Label "ПЛЕЕР ДЛЯ АРХИВА" (`settings_catchup_player`, 14/800 +0.12em Dim, padding start 8, top 18, bottom 4).
4. Pills Спрашивать / Встроенный / Внешний (`settings_catchup_player_ask/_internal/_external`).
5. Button "Готово" (`settings_done`) right-aligned, 52 high.

Changes apply immediately. Back or Готово closes.

##### Sub-page "Плейлисты" (`A/features/settings/ManageSourcesScreen.kt:288-536`)
Same sheet, title "Плейлисты". Rows in order:
1. Empty state: note "`settings_sources_empty`" (:298).
2. (More than one playlist) "Показывать плейлист" (`settings_show_playlist`), icon LIST. Value Choice = the default playlist's name or "Все плейлисты" (`content_all_playlists`). OK → choice list [Все плейлисты, each playlist] (:300-319).
3. One row per playlist (`SourceRow` :436-536). Icon PLAYLIST, title = name.
   - Line = counts · "Автообновление: …" · "Действует до: …", or the type + URL if empty (:470-476, :509).
   - Value = status as Action text: "Удаление…", "Синхронизация NN%", "Отключён", "По умолчанию" (:477-484).
   - OK → `RowActionsPopup` (title = playlist name) with actions in this order:
     1. Отключить / Включить (EYE_OFF / PLAYLIST)
     2. Изменить (PENCIL)
     3. Информация (INFO) → test popup
     4. Обновить / Отменить (REFRESH)
     5. Очистить названия (SPARKLE)
     6. Удалить (TRASH, danger)
     
     (:486-505, :511). In the wide layout these actions sat in the panel behind ▶; in the sheet only OK works.
4. (Build with an author playlist URL) "`settings_author_playlist`" / "`settings_author_playlist_desc`", Switch. ON imports it; OFF asks to delete (:351-375).
5. "Добавить плейлист" (`settings_sources_add`), icon ADD. OK → the add-source flow, a centred window. Its form is the same as the wizard's "Добавить источник" (see 2a) (:376-384, :160-231).

Popups: delete confirm "`settings_sources_delete_title`" (Cancel focused); test popup "`settings_sources_test_title`" with buttons Повторить / OK (or Пропустить while measuring) (:631-684).

##### Sub-page "Источники EPG" (`A/features/settings/EpgSourcesScreen.kt:101-164`, `EpgSourceRow.kt:74-192`)
1. Empty → note `settings_epg_sources_empty`.
2. One row per guide. Icon EPG, title = name, line = status (e.g. "Синхронизировано …", "Ещё не синхронизировано"). A failed guide shows its address as the line and the error as a Custom value on the right (17/600 Muted, max 420 mpx). OK → actions popup: Обновить / Отменить, Изменить, (Выше / Ниже when several), Удалить (danger).
3. Heading "`settings_epg_catalog`" + note "`settings_epg_catalog_description`".

5. "Добавить EPG" (`content_epg_add`), icon ADD → add-guide form.
