## 4. Hard on Tizen 5 / webOS 4 and nearest equivalents

Engines: **Tizen 5.0 (2019 Samsung) = Chromium 63**; **webOS 4.x (2018-2019 LG) = Chromium 53**. Write CSS/JS for Chromium 53 and treat anything newer as an enhancement. Versions below are the first Chrome release with unprefixed support (Chrome Platform Status / MDN).

### 4.1 CSS

| Spec relies on | Chrome since | Tizen 5 (63) | webOS 4 (53) | Nearest equivalent |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Flexbox `gap` (row/list spacing everywhere, `Arrangement.spacedBy`) | 84 | no | no | margins: `.row > * + * { margin-left: Xpx }`, `.col > * + * { margin-top: Xpx }`. Do not use `gap` at all. |
| CSS Grid (`display:grid`, `grid-gap`) | 57 | yes | **no** | flexbox with fixed widths / `flex-wrap` + margins (guide cells: absolute positioning, as Android draws them on a canvas anyway). |
| CSS custom properties (`--mpx`, palette tokens) | 49 | yes | yes | OK. Avoid `var()` inside `calc()` in hot animated properties (slow recalc); precompute px in JS if needed. |
| 8-digit hex `#RRGGBBAA` (Android ARGB tokens like `#F2232629`, `#B3000000`) | 62 | yes | **no** | always write `rgba(r,g,b,a)`. Convert ARGB → RGBA (Android puts alpha FIRST: `#F2232629` = `rgba(35,38,41,0.95)`). |
| Space-separated `rgb(0 0 0 / 50%)` | 65 | no | no | comma `rgba()`. |
| `backdrop-filter: blur()` (glass panels, "frost") | 76 | no | no | Android itself does not live-blur: it pre-blurs the background picture once and draws the matching slice (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/Glass.kt:29-98`). Web: pre-blur the picture (canvas/StackBlur once, or server-side) into one image and use it as `background` of the panel with `background-attachment`-like positioning (`background-position: -left -top` of a full-screen-sized image), or simply the solid fill `#1E2125`/`#232629`. In TV-box mode menus/popups are solid anyway (`SC:697`, `SP:100`). |
| `filter: blur()` on large layers | 53 (unprefixed) | slow | slow | avoid on anything bigger than a thumbnail; pre-blur images. |
| `filter: saturate() brightness()` (watched episode still, `SC:1100-1106`) | 53 | yes | yes (unprefixed since 53; keep `-webkit-filter` too) | fine on small stills; or a black 28% overlay. |
| Large `box-shadow` glows (30-60 px blur) | — | slow | slow | Android drops them on weak TVs (`DevicePerf.lite`). Since the shipped TV-box focus is a flat bar with no shadow, use shadows only on popups/cards, never on per-row focus. |
| `aspect-ratio` (16:9 preview, posters) | 88 | no | no | fixed mpx width + height (all Android sizes are explicit), or `padding-top: 56.25%` box. |
| `inset: 0` | 87 | no | no | `top:0; right:0; bottom:0; left:0`. |
| `clamp()`, `min()`, `max()` | 79 | no | no | compute in JS (e.g. popup width `min(880mpx, 100vw − 80mpx)`), or media queries. |
| `:focus-visible` | 86 | no | no | do not rely on DOM focus styling: keep a JS "focused" class (D-pad focus manager) and style `.is-focused`. |
| `:focus-within` | 60 | yes | **no** | JS class on the parent (`.has-focus`). |
| `:is()`, `:where()`, `:not(a, b)` lists | 88 | no | no | write selectors out; `:not()` with one simple selector only. |
| `position: sticky` (sticky headers, settings group headers) | 56 | yes | **no** | absolutely positioned header outside the scroller, or JS. |
| `display: contents` | 65 | no | no | real wrappers. |
| `object-fit: contain/cover` (logos, posters) | 32 | yes | yes | OK. |
| `font-variation-settings` / variable fonts (Plus Jakarta Sans ships as one variable TTF) | 62 | yes | **no** | ship **static** WOFF/WOFF2 files per weight 400, 500, 600, 700, 800 (5 files, Cyrillic + Latin subsets). WOFF2 is fine on both (Chrome 36). Weight 900 is requested by the wordmark but the family stops at 800 (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/brand/SkMateWordmark.kt:31`): use 800. |
| `font-feature-settings: "tnum"` (channel numbers) | 48 | yes | yes | OK (Plus Jakarta has `tnum`). |
| `text-overflow: ellipsis`, `-webkit-line-clamp` (2-line titles) | old | yes | yes | OK (`display:-webkit-box; -webkit-box-orient:vertical`). |
| Marquee on focused rows (`basicMarquee`, 900 ms delay) | — | — | — | CSS `@keyframes` translateX on an inner span, started after 900 ms only when `scrollWidth > clientWidth`; use `transform`, not `left`. |
| `scroll-behavior: smooth`, `scrollIntoView({block:'center', behavior:'smooth'})`, `scrollTo({…})` options | 61 | yes | **no** (options object ignored / treated as `true`) | manage scroll yourself: set `scrollTop` (instant), or translateY a list container with a CSS transition. Android lists keep the focused row in view with instant moves on weak TVs anyway. |
| `overscroll-behavior` | 63 | yes | no | not needed on TV (no touch). |
| `conic-gradient` | 69 | no | no | SVG/canvas (countdown ring is an SVG arc anyway). |
| `mask-image` | prefixed only | `-webkit-mask` | `-webkit-mask` | prefix it. |
| `will-change: transform` | 36 | yes | yes | use sparingly (memory on 1 GB TVs); only on the moving panel (side menu, settings sheet, notices). |
| `transition`/`@keyframes` with `cubic-bezier` | old | yes | yes | Android curves: FastOutSlowIn `cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1)`, FastOutLinearIn `(0.4,0,1,1)`, LinearOutSlowIn `(0,0,0.2,1)`. Animate only `transform` and `opacity`. |
| `img loading="lazy"` (channel logos) | 77 | no | no | `IntersectionObserver` (Chrome 51: available on both) or windowed rendering of visible rows only. |
| Viewport units / `calc()` | old | yes | yes | OK. |

### 4.2 JavaScript / Web APIs

| API | Chrome since | Tizen 5 | webOS 4 | Nearest equivalent |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ES2017+ syntax: `async/await` (55), object spread (60), optional chaining / `??` (80), class fields (72) | — | partial | partial | transpile to **ES5** (TS `target: es5` or Babel preset-env with `chrome 53`), bundle as a classic script (no `<script type=module>`, Chrome 61). |
| `Object.entries/values` (54), `String.padStart` (57), `Array.prototype.flat` (69), `Promise.prototype.finally` (63), `Object.fromEntries` (73) | — | partly | no | core-js polyfills for chrome 53. |
| `ResizeObserver` | 64 | **no** | no | layout is fixed 1920×1080: measure once on load and on `resize`; or the `resize-observer-polyfill`. |
| `IntersectionObserver` | 51 | yes | yes | OK (use for logo lazy-load). |
| `Intl.PluralRules` (Russian "1 день / 3 дня / 5 дней", plural strings) | 63 | yes | **no** | own CLDR rule for ru: `n%10==1 && n%100!=11 → one; n%10 in 2..4 && n%100 not in 12..14 → few; else many` (+ `other` for fractions); English one/other. |
| `Intl.DateTimeFormat` with `hour/minute` (clock, guide times) | 24 | yes | yes | OK; `formatToParts` is Chrome 57 → avoid on webOS. `hourCycle` option is Chrome 73 → use `hour12: false`. |
| `fetch` (42), `AbortController` (66) | — | fetch yes / Abort **no** | fetch yes / Abort no | polyfill AbortController or ignore stale responses with a request id. |
| `requestIdleCallback` | 47 | yes | yes | OK. |
| Web Animations `element.animate()` | 36 (basic) | yes | yes | prefer CSS transitions; `finished` promise is Chrome 84. |
| `Element.closest` (41), `classList` | — | yes | yes | OK. |
| `KeyboardEvent.key` (51) vs `keyCode` | 51 | yes | yes, but TV remotes often send only `keyCode` | always switch on `keyCode`: Back = **10009 (Tizen) / 461 (webOS)**, Enter 13, arrows 37-40, ChannelUp/Down = **427/428 (Tizen), 33/34 (webOS)**, colour keys Tizen 403-406 / webOS 403-406, media keys need `tizen.tvinputdevice.registerKey()` on Tizen. |

### 4.3 Video and layering (the biggest real difference)

- **Hardware video plane.** Tizen AVPlay (and the platform players behind `<video>` on both TVs) composite the picture **under** the HTML layer. Consequences for the Android design:
  - The preview card on the channel list has rounded corners (`01-kanaly.png`, preview at x≈980). Rounded clipping (`border-radius` + `overflow:hidden`) does **not** clip a hardware video plane. Nearest: draw the four corner masks as page-coloured (`#232629`) quarter-circle overlays on top, or accept square corners on the live picture and round only the frame/border. AVPlay position/size is set with `webapis.avplay.setDisplayRect(x,y,w,h)` in real screen pixels (convert mpx ×10/9 if you adopt that scale).
  - **"Full screen grows out of the preview"** (`SHELL:2619-2620`, 320/260 ms): animating a hardware plane frame-by-frame is not possible smoothly; Android itself turns it off on weak devices and docks instantly (`DockMs = 0`, `SHELL:2631`). Web: jump to full screen in one step (optionally cross-fade the UI around it).
  - Any element drawn over video must leave the area transparent; the "picture fades in from black" (`SHELL:2641-2644`) can be done with a black HTML overlay whose opacity animates 1 → 0 over 320 ms after a 250 ms delay.
  - CSS `transform`/`filter`/`opacity` on the `<video>` element itself is ignored or very slow on both; never scale/blur the video element.
- **On-screen keyboard (IME).** Both platforms' keyboards cover the lower half and do not resize the page. Android moves the popup to the top above the keyboard (`SP:90-99`). Web: on input focus, translate the popup/field to the top area; restore on blur. (The repo's web app already has a fix for this: commit `364bad6`.)

### 4.4 Performance budget (weak 2018-2019 TVs)

- Android's own rule for weak boxes is the right model (`app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/DevicePerf.kt`, `MotionPrefs.kt`): no big shadows, no background glows/lights, no hairlines, no skeleton pulse, panels appear instantly, menus overlay instead of pushing content. Implement a `lite` class on `<html>` and switch it on by default for Tizen ≤ 5 and webOS ≤ 4 (or when frames are measured slow).
- Lists with hundreds of channels: render only the visible window (~12 rows of 92 mpx) plus a small buffer; reuse DOM nodes. Changing focus must touch at most two rows (remove/add the focus class).
- Large radial/linear gradients repainted per frame are expensive (Android measured ≈30 ms per screen-sized gradient pass and replaced them by pre-rendered images, `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/theme/GradientTextures.kt:28-38`). Web: use a single pre-rendered PNG/JPEG for the light-theme Stage background, never animated gradients.
- Logos: decode at display size (Android decodes at 160 px, `app/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/ui/components/ChannelLogoTile.kt:125-126`); avoid hundreds of full-size PNGs in the DOM. The per-logo "ink contrast plate" measurement (`ChannelLogoTile.kt:144-183`) needs `getImageData` on a canvas, which fails for cross-origin logos without CORS (tainted canvas). Nearest: skip the plate unless the logo host sends CORS headers; or proxy logos.
- Icons: inline SVG paths (Phosphor 256-grid / Unicons 24-grid) are cheap; prefer one SVG `<symbol>` sprite and `<use>` (supported on both).

### 4.N Per-screen notes

#### Agreement + wizard (2a)

- **Scale 1 mpx vs screenshots.** Android at the default zoom 100 % draws 1 mpx = 1.111 px; the screenshots show that. A web stage at 1 mpx = 1 px looks 10 % smaller (= Android at 90 %). Decide one global factor. The live zoom and font-size steppers of the Display-size step also need the whole stage to rescale at runtime: on Chromium 53/63 use `transform: scale()` on a fixed 1920×1080 root (or a root `font-size` with rem units); CSS `zoom` works in Chromium but changes layout metrics and is slower on webOS 4.
- **Flexbox `gap` does not exist** before Chrome 84 (Tizen 5 = Chromium 63, webOS 4 = Chromium 53). All "gap 6 / 14 / 16" rows must use margins (e.g. `> * + *` margin-left/top).
- **Variable font** (`plus_jakarta_sans_variable.ttf`): `font-variation-settings` / variable TTF needs Chrome 62+; webOS 4 (53) does not support it. Ship static WOFF (or TTF) files for weights 400, 500, 600, 700, 800.
- **Lottie TV on Welcome** (`welcome_tv.json`, looping): lottie-web (SVG renderer) is heavy on webOS 4 / Tizen 5. Nearest: lottie-web light build with the canvas renderer at low frame rate, or pre-render to an animated WebP/PNG sprite sheet and loop it with CSS `steps()`.
- **Animations** (slide ±1/8 width + fade 300/150 ms, sliding focus highlight 200 ms, step label expand 320 ms, segmented pill 160 ms, switch 200 ms): use CSS transitions on `transform`/`opacity` only (GPU). `expandHorizontally`/`animateContentSize` of the step label: animate `max-width` + `opacity`, or skip size animation on webOS 4. Compose default tween easing = `cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)`.
- **Text input / on-screen keyboard.** Android opens the system IME on OK and closes it on Back/Done. Tizen: focusing an `<input>` opens the Samsung IME; its Done/Cancel arrive as keyCodes 65376 / 65385; Back while the IME is open must not close the step. webOS: the IME opens on focus; Back closes it first. Keep the "row highlighted, real input focused only while editing" pattern; `inputmode="numeric"` is not honoured on Chromium 53/63, use `type="tel"`/`type="number"` for the PIN, `type="password"` for passwords, `type="url"` for addresses.
- **"Remote / from the phone" (QR + LAN web server + PIN)** for adding a playlist, restoring a backup, sending a profile picture: a web app on Tizen/webOS cannot open a listening HTTP server. Nearest options: a cloud relay (the TV polls a short code), or hide those cards on web and say so. Same for **"Sync device"** (LAN discovery/NSD): not available in a browser runtime; hide or replace with a relay.
- **Local files** (M3U files on the TV, backup `.own` files, profile pictures from storage): Tizen has `tizen.filesystem` (needs privilege), webOS has no general file access for web apps. Nearest: hide the local-file rows/cards on web, or USB access only where the platform API allows.
- **Closing the app** (Decline on the terms, Back held ~1 s): Tizen `tizen.application.getCurrentApplication().exit()`, webOS `window.close()` (or `webOS.platformBack()` for Back to launcher).
- **First-run language** = device language if supported and not English, else Russian: read `navigator.language` (Tizen also `tizen.systeminfo` LOCALE, webOS `webOS.systemInfo`/`navigator.language`).
- **Spinner**: Android's is 36 dp (= 64.8 mpx), 12 spokes stepping 12×/s; easy as an SVG with a `steps(12)` CSS rotation, cheaper than a per-frame JS animation.

#### Home / channel list (2b)

- **Live preview video inside a rounded 28 px card** (`LiveStage.kt:354-393`). On Tizen/webOS the hardware video plane (AVPlay / `<video>` with HW decoder) is often a hole punched behind the page: `border-radius` + `overflow:hidden` does not clip it, and a second simultaneous decoder (preview + full screen) may be unavailable. Nearest: one shared player element repositioned between preview rect and full screen (Android does the same "shrink back into the preview rect", `LiveStage.kt:362-366`), square corners on the video with a 28 px rounded frame drawn over its corners in the page colour; if no HW preview, show the 288 px logo on black (the state the screenshot shows).
- **Box shadows with big blur** (preview card 80 px blur, menus 40 px, `LiveStage.kt:368`, `StageComponents.kt:1333`). `box-shadow` works on Chromium 53/63 but large blurs over video are slow on TV GPUs; use a pre-rendered shadow PNG/9-slice or drop it on weak models (Android itself drops effects on `DevicePerf.lite`).
- **Marquee of long names on focus** (`FocusMarquee.kt`): no `basicMarquee`; implement with a CSS `@keyframes translateX` on the focused row only (supported in 53/63). Avoid `<marquee>`.
- **`font-feature-settings: "tnum"`** for clock and times: supported from Chromium 48, OK; but the bundled font must contain tabular figures (check the Plus Jakarta Sans build used).
- **Per-character rolling clock** (`StageStatus.kt:243-266`): fine with CSS transforms; keep it to changed characters only.
- **`gap` in flexbox** is NOT supported in Chromium < 84: every `spacedBy(n)` (toolbar 8, row 18, trailing group 8, list 8, next list 2) must be done with margins.
- **CSS `aspect-ratio`** (Chromium 88+) is unavailable: give the preview an explicit height (912 x 9/16 = 513) or the padding-top 56.25% trick.
- **`position: sticky`, `inset`, `:focus-visible`, CSS variables in `@media`**: variables are OK (49+), `inset` is not (87+) — use top/right/bottom/left.
- **Lazy, paged list with ~10k+ channels** (Android uses Paging + LazyColumn): needs a hand-written virtual list (render ~15 rows, translateY), and index-based ▲/▼ stepping as Android does (`LiveScreen.kt:312-335`) instead of spatial navigation, otherwise a held key outruns rendering.
- **Hold-OK detection** relies on key auto-repeat (`repeatCount`, `LiveScreen.kt:961-967`). Tizen/webOS remotes do not always auto-repeat Enter; use a keydown->keyup timer (~600 ms, as `ChNavPaging.kt:96`) with keyup as the fallback.
- **CH+/CH− and ⏪/⏩ keys**: must be registered on Tizen (`tizen.tvinputdevice.registerKey('ChannelUp'|'ChannelDown'|'MediaRewind'|'MediaFastForward')`); webOS delivers 33/34 and 412/417. Menu key (hold-OK alternative) has no stable keycode on webOS.
- **TV on-screen keyboard over the search field** (`StageControls.kt:102-119` scrolls the field into view): on webOS/Tizen the IME resizes or overlays the page differently; the web app already has a fix for this (commit 364bad6).
- **Logo ink measurement** (`ChannelLogoTile.kt`): reading pixels needs canvas `getImageData`, which throws on cross-origin logos without CORS. Nearest: skip measuring and show the logo on transparent (accept that some dark logos are hard to read) or measure only when CORS allows.
- **Weather icon is canvas-drawn and time-of-day tinted** (`WeatherConditionIcon.kt`): an SVG set with the listed colours is the practical equivalent.

#### Side menu (2c)

- **Screenshot scale vs mpx (affects every screen, not only the menu).** The reference screenshots are at UI Zoom 100% on a 2.0-density emulator, so 1 mpx = 1.111 screenshot px, while `fx()` geometry (fractions of 1920) is 1:1. Measured on `02-menu.png`: 84 mpx rail = 93 px, 58+6 mpx item pitch = 71 px, 46 mpx avatar = 51 px. If the web app maps 1 mpx = 1 px, mpx-sized parts will look 10% smaller than the screenshots while fx-sized parts match. Pick one rule project-wide (suggest: a CSS variable `--mpx: 1.1111px` to match the screenshots, since the Android default zoom is 100%: `engine/core/src/main/java/tv/own/owntv/core/theme/UiZoom.kt:11`).
- **Rail and page sliding together (300 ms) on Chromium 53/63.** Use `transform: translateX()` on two layers (rail and a page wrapper) with the same `transition: transform 300ms cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1)`; never animate `left`/`width`/`padding` (re-layout of the channel list every frame). Add `will-change: transform` only while animating; on webOS 4 too many composited layers cost memory. Provide the instant path (animations off / weak TV) as Android does (`MotionPrefs.smoothMenu`).
- **Fade-out while sliding** (rail alpha = 1 + progress) is fine as `opacity` transition with the same timing.
- **Hairline 1 mpx** at 1.111 px scale renders blurry/uneven on some TV panels; use `1px` exactly (or `box-shadow: inset -1px 0 0 rgba(...)`).
- **Icons**: Unicons/Phosphor are filled `path` data; inline SVG with `viewBox` 24 or 256 and `fill: currentColor` works on Chromium 53. Avoid CSS `mask-image` icons (prefixed only `-webkit-mask` on 53).
- **Countdown ring** (dwell 3-2-1): draw with SVG `stroke-dasharray`/`stroke-dashoffset` updated from `requestAnimationFrame` using wall-clock time, as Android does (not a CSS animation), so the number and ring stay in step.
- **Locale-grouped counts** ("1 689"): `Intl.NumberFormat('ru')` exists on Chromium 53/63 but older webOS images may ship without full ICU; fall back to inserting U+00A0 every 3 digits for ru.
- **Long press OK** for the category menu: TV remotes on Tizen/webOS send key repeat for a held Enter; detect via keydown repeat (`e.repeat` or a second keydown before keyup) after ~500 ms, and swallow the following keyup so a click is not fired too. Android also uses the remote's Menu key for the channel menu.
- **Back key**: Tizen `keyCode 10009`, webOS `461`; "press Back again within 2 s to exit" needs `tizen.application.getCurrentApplication().exit()` / `webOS.platformBack()` (or `window.close()`), not a dialog.
- **Uppercasing «ГРУППЫ»** with `toLocaleUpperCase(lang)` is supported; CSS `text-transform: uppercase` also works for Russian, but Turkish i/İ needs `lang` on the element (Android uses the platform locale for this reason).

#### Player (§2d): what may be hard on Tizen 5 (Chromium 63) / webOS 4 (Chromium 53)

- **Picture docking into the corner (§2d.4).** Android shrinks the video into a rect at 680/36, 1204×677, over 220 ms. It does this by resizing the surface itself. On Tizen, AVPlay draws on a hardware plane *behind* the web page. `webapis.avplay.setDisplayRect(x,y,w,h)` can move it, but every call is a re-layout of that plane: animating it frame by frame stutters or flickers on many sets. webOS `<video>` with hardware decoding has the same plane-behind-the-page model. **Nearest equivalent:** call `setDisplayRect` / set the video element's box once, straight to the end rect, with no per-frame animation (Android itself goes instant on weak devices: `DockAnimMs` = 0 when not smooth). Keep the panel's fade/slide (CSS `transform`/`opacity`) animated. Do not change the design silently; report it if the corner jump is visible.
- **The clear "window" in the panel fill (`BlendMode.Clear`).** On the web the video plane is already behind the page. So the panel can simply be four dark rects (or one rect with a transparent hole made from `box-shadow: 0 0 0 9999px #F2232629` on a transparent box at the dock rect, radius 14). The page and `body` must have a transparent background where the video shows (Tizen AVPlay needs a transparent page area). Avoid `mix-blend-mode` and `mask` tricks: unreliable on Chromium 53.
- **Growing out of the preview (320 ms) / shrinking back (260 ms).** These are the same surface-resize problem. Nearest equivalent: animate a black cover box (CSS transform) from the preview rect to full screen, set the video rect once at the end, then fade the cover out (Android also shows black while it moves: `OwnTVShell.kt:1820-1823`).
- **Overlays over hardware video.** Text and gradients over AVPlay are fine. But CSS `backdrop-filter` (frosted glass) does **not** exist in Chromium 53/63, and it cannot blur a hardware plane anyway. The TV-box player uses flat alpha fills (`#F2232629`, `#B3000000`, `#C7000000`), so no blur is needed. Keep them flat.
- **Gradient washes** (420/560 dp bottom shade, scrims) need `linear-gradient` with multiple stops. That is supported. Use `rgba()`, not 8-digit `#RRGGBBAA`: Chromium 53 does not parse 8-digit hex (support came in Chromium 62, so Tizen 5 is fine but webOS 4 is not).
- **CSS features to avoid for webOS 4:**
  - `gap` on flexbox (flex `gap` arrived in Chromium 84): use margins.
  - `aspect-ratio` (Chromium 88): compute height = width × 9/16 in JS or with a padding-top trick.
  - `inset` shorthand (Chromium 87).
  - CSS custom properties are OK (Chromium 49+).
  - `position: sticky` is OK on Chromium 56+, so it is **missing on webOS 4**.
  - `object-fit` is OK.
- **Marquee of long names on the focused row** (`focusMarquee`). Use a CSS `@keyframes` translateX on an inner span. It is fine on both, but keep it to one row (the focused one) for weak CPUs.
- **Countdown ring (3-2-1).** Android draws an SVG-like arc that follows the wall clock. On the web, use an SVG circle with `stroke-dasharray` / `stroke-dashoffset` updated from `requestAnimationFrame` (or a CSS transition of 3 s linear). Both work on Chromium 53.
- **Spinner** (12 spokes, a 1-s turn, stepped). Use CSS `steps(12)` rotation of one SVG. Cheap on both.
- **Key codes.** Android key names do not exist on the web:
  - Tizen gives `keyCode` 37–40 for the arrows, 13 OK, 10009 Back, 427/428 CH+/CH−, 415 Play, 19 Pause, 10252 Play/Pause, 412 RW, 417 FF, 457 Info, 403–406 colour keys, 48–57 digits.
  - Media and colour keys need `tizen.tvinputdevice.registerKey(...)` first.
  - webOS: Back = 461, CH± = 33/34 (PageUp/PageDown), RW 412, FF 417, Play 415, Pause 19, colour 403–406.
  - Build one key map and translate into the Android key names used in the §2d.8 table.
  - **Long OK (600 ms) and long Back:** measure from the first keydown (Android does the same: `OwnTVShell.kt:1063-1066`), because TVs fire repeated keydowns. webOS may not send repeats for every key; long Back on webOS is usually taken by the system (opens the launcher). The nearest equivalent there is to skip "long Back closes the app".
- **Key-repeat pacing** (`MainActivity.kt:85-107`, drop a held repeat until the previous frame has drawn). On the web: ignore a keydown with `repeat` while a `requestAnimationFrame` from the previous step is still pending. This is needed on webOS 4 / Tizen 5 CPUs, or long lists will lag behind the remote.
- **"OK acts on key-up" on tiles.** This is straightforward. But Tizen can deliver keyup to a different element if focus moved on keydown. Track the "down happened here" flag per tile, as Android does (`TvBoxLiveHud.kt:55-74`).
- **Media keys going to the system.** On Android, unhandled Play/Pause reaches the MediaSession. On the web nothing does that: the app must handle 415/19/10252 itself (toggle play when the HUD is shown; show the info when hidden, matching §2d.8).
- **Live preview while focusing rows (dwell 1.5 s + 3-2-1).** Each preview re-opens a stream. AVPlay `open/prepare` can take 1–3 s, and many providers allow only one connection. Keep Android's rule: the stream opens only after the countdown fires, and an isolated zap opens at once / a burst after 500 ms (`ZapTuneDelay.kt`). Call `avplay.stop()` + `close()` before the next `open()`, or Tizen returns `PLAYER_ERROR_INVALID_STATE`.
- **Archive scrub (10 s steps that grow while held).** On AVPlay, live HLS seeking is limited: catch-up/timeshift is a *new URL* per offset (as on Android, where the VM debounces and reloads the archive). Debounce on the web too: update the dot and counter at once, open the archive URL only after the key run rests (Android shows the counter for 1.5 s).
- **HLS / formats.** Tizen AVPlay plays HLS/MPEG-TS natively. webOS `<video>` plays HLS natively on most models, but not every codec mix (e.g. MPEG-2 TS with AC-3, some H.265). Android falls back to mpv, which the web does not have. hls.js on Chromium 53 needs Media Source Extensions, which is present but weak (H.264/AAC only, high CPU). The player error screen (§2d.11 "This video cannot be shown") should be the honest fallback. Do not hide the failure.
- **Audio / subtitle track dialogs.** AVPlay exposes `getTotalTrackInfo()` / `setSelectTrack()`. webOS `<video>` exposes `audioTracks` / `textTracks` only on some models (webOS 4 often lacks `audioTracks`). If missing, hide the "Звук" tile exactly as Android does when there is ≤1 track. A/V-sync and subtitle-timing steppers have no AVPlay/HTML5 equivalent for audio delay: hide those rows (Android hides them when `audioDelayAvailable()` is false).
- **Aspect modes.** AVPlay has `setDisplayMethod` (letterbox / full screen / auto aspect ratio). HTML5 has `object-fit` contain/cover/fill. "Original 1:1", "Force 16:9" and "Force 4:3" need a computed rect. Doable, but check every mode on real sets.
- **Sleep timer "turn off the screen".** No web API on Tizen/webOS for apps (Tizen: `tizen.power` needs a partner privilege). Show the row as unavailable with the same toast text as Android (`player_sleep_timer_screen_off_unavailable`).
- **Mini player / Multiview / Audio-only / Record tiles.** A second hardware video plane is not available on most Tizen 5 / webOS 4 sets (one AVPlay instance), and local recording to disk is not available. Hide the tiles (Android hides each tile whose feature is off) and say so in the report. Do not fake them.
- **Time formats.** Android uses the system 12/24-h formatter and `EEEdMMM` dates. `Intl.DateTimeFormat` exists on Chromium 53, but its `ru` data may be incomplete on some firmware. Format times by hand (`HH:mm`) and use the Russian day/month names from a table.

#### Guide, Sport, Search: risks on Tizen 5 (Chromium 63) / webOS 4 (Chromium 53)

- **Guide grid size.** One week × hundreds of channels is far too many DOM nodes. Android draws **one canvas per row** and keeps one focus target per row with a time cursor (`features/epg/GuideCore.kt:62-67`, `features/epg/EpgScreen.kt:171-174`). On the web do the same:
  - Virtualise the rows: render only the ~10 visible rows plus 2–3 extra. Recycle row elements.
  - Draw each row's cells into one `<canvas>`, or use absolutely positioned `<div>`s for only the cells inside the visible window (± one slot).
  - Make the whole row a single focus target. Never use one focusable node per programme.
  - Re-draw on horizontal scroll with `transform: translateX` on a per-row layer, not by changing `left` on every cell.
  - Chromium 53 has no `IntersectionObserver` (it arrived in 58) and no `ResizeObserver`. Compute visible rows from the scroll position.
- **Shared horizontal scroll.** The ruler, the now-line and every row read one `scrollPx` (`EpgScreen.kt:257-264`). Keep it in one JS variable and apply `transform: translateX(-scrollPx)` to each row's content and to the ruler. Avoid `scrollLeft` on many elements at once.
- **Canvas text.** `ctx.measureText`, ellipsis and `font-feature-settings: "tnum"` are not available on canvas in Chromium 53/63 (`ctx.fontVariantNumeric` does not exist). Options: use a font whose default digits are tabular (check whether the main font, Plus Jakarta Sans per `ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:248-249`, has them; not verified here), or draw the time text in DOM spans, or accept proportional digits in cells. Write the ellipsis yourself: measure, then trim and add «…». For DOM text, `font-feature-settings: "tnum"` works in Chromium 48+.
- **Glow shadows.** The now-line glow (box-shadow blur 14) and the menu/video shadows (blur 40–80) are expensive on old TV GPUs. Use `box-shadow` on a few static elements only. Never animate it. On the now-line a 3 px div with `box-shadow: 0 0 14px #0086FC` is fine.
- **Long press OK** (programme menu). Android detects a held OK from the second KeyDown with `repeatCount > 0` (`EpgScreen.kt:882-888`). On Tizen/webOS key repeat is unreliable: some remotes send no repeats, some send keydown/keyup pairs. Use a timer instead: start ~500 ms on `keydown` of Enter (13); if `keyup` comes first it is a short press. Ignore repeated `keydown`s. The shell already times long Back from the first KeyDown for the same reason (`features/shell/OwnTVShell.kt:1076-1080`). Also map the remote's Menu/Info key (Tizen `KEY_MENU` needs `tizen.tvinputdevice.registerKey`; webOS has no Menu key, so it falls back to long OK).
- **Search field + on-screen keyboard.** Android opens the system IME only on OK (`ui/stage/StageControls.kt:59-197`). On Tizen, focusing an `<input>` opens the Samsung IME at once and captures the arrow keys. On webOS the virtual keyboard opens on focus and the "Enter/Done" key fires `keydown 13`. Nearest equivalent:
  - Keep the input `readonly` (or blurred) while it is only D-pad-focused. On OK, remove `readonly` and call `.focus()`.
  - Leave typing on Back (Tizen 10009 / webOS 461), on ▲/▼ (with Samsung IME these may not reach the page), or on IME "Done".
  - On Tizen listen for the `blur` the IME causes when it closes.
  - The keyboard covers the bottom ~45 % of the screen (Android fallback estimate `FALLBACK_KEYBOARD_FRACTION = 0.45`, `ui/components/TvImeMetrics.kt:33`). The search field sits at the top, so this is fine.
- **Layout-tolerant query matching** («В любой раскладке», `engine/core/.../sports/SportsBoardFilter.kt:72-99`; search uses the same `QueryVariants`/`SearchRank`). This is pure logic: port it from `engine/core` to JS rather than approximating it.
- **Animated count-up** (Sport 500 ms, Search 600 ms) and the rolling caption (2400 ms hold, 420 ms slide): use `requestAnimationFrame` with a cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1) easing function. CSS `transition` on `transform` + `opacity` is fine for the caption. These are cheap.
- **Field rise animation** (Search, x and top over 360 ms): animate `transform: translate()`, not `left`/`top`, so Chromium 53 can composite it.
- **Live preview video** in the guide and in search. On many Tizen/webOS TVs only **one** hardware decoder is available to a web app. The guide preview and the player must share one `<video>`/AVPlay instance. Android also uses Live TV's single preview engine ("no second decoder", `features/epg/GuideStage.kt:105-107`). On webOS 4, `<video>` inside a rounded-corner clip (`border-radius` + `overflow:hidden`) is often drawn on a separate video plane and **ignores** the corner clip. The nearest equivalent is a square video with a page-coloured rounded frame drawn above it.
- **`gap` in flexbox** does not exist in Chromium < 84. Every "gap N" in this section must become margins (or `display:grid` with `grid-gap`, available from Chromium 57, so **not on webOS 4 / Chromium 53**). Sport's 2-column card grid and the equal-width filter buttons: use `display:flex` with `flex:1 1 0` and margins on both browsers.
- **`position: sticky`** (Chromium 56+) is not available on webOS 4. The guide's fixed ruler/day header must be a separate, non-scrolling element above the scrolling rows, which is also how Android builds it.
- **Day label format** `EEEdMMM` («ср, 25 нояб.»). `Intl.DateTimeFormat` with `{weekday:'short', day:'numeric', month:'short'}` in Chromium 53 gives «ср, 25 нояб.» for `ru`, but the comma and the dot vary by ICU version. Check on the TV, or format it by hand with month/weekday tables.

#### Settings part A (2f)

- **Screenshot scale vs mpx (settings and every other screen).** The screenshots are at UI zoom 100 %; mpx is defined at 90 %. So all mpx sizes appear ×1.111 on the screenshots (settings rows 92 → ≈102 px, group lines 80 → ≈89 px). The sheet width is 37 % of the screen and does not scale. Decide once whether the web stage maps 1 mpx = 1 px (code-exact, ≈10 % smaller than the screenshots) or 1 mpx = 1.111 px (screenshot-exact). Sources: `C/theme/UiZoom.kt:11`, `A/ui/theme/StageTokens.kt:15-23`.
- **Marquee on focused rows** (`basicMarquee`, 900 ms delay, endless). Chromium 53/63 has no `<marquee>` worth using. Build it with a CSS `@keyframes` `transform: translateX()` on an inner span, started only when `scrollWidth > clientWidth`. Use `-webkit-` prefixes on webOS 4. Avoid animating `left`.
- **Sheet slide-in** (300 ms `translateX` + opacity, cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)): use `transform: translate3d()` so the old GPUs composite it. Page changes inside the sheet are instant on Android, so no extra animation work is needed there.
- **Edge-to-edge focus bar plus `edgeScrollSpec`** ("keep one row of room"): `scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'})` options are unreliable on Chromium 53. Compute `scrollTop` by hand (offset − room / offset + size + room − container).
- **HSV colour picker** (accent and clock colours): two stacked CSS gradients work in Chromium 53 (`linear-gradient` to the right white → hue, then to the bottom transparent → black). Avoid `conic-gradient` (not available) for the hue bar; use a 7-stop `linear-gradient`.
- **`FlowRow` / gap.** Flexbox `gap` is not supported before Chromium 84. Use margins for every "spacedBy" (rows, tags, pills, swatches).
- **Text input on TV keyboards** (field rows, location, profile name, PIN). Tizen and webOS virtual keyboards resize or cover the viewport differently. Android moves the popup to the top while the keyboard is up (`StagePopup.kt:90-99`). The web needs the same "field above keyboard" handling already done in commit 364bad6.
- **Live-preview / zoom-dependent settings** (UI zoom 50–150 %, popup size, font size): the web must apply them as a CSS scale or root font-size multiplier. `zoom` is non-standard but works in Chromium. `transform: scale` breaks hit-testing and fixed-position layers less, but needs layout compensation.

#### Risks from settings part B (Player … About)

Status words: **not possible** = no web API on Tizen/webOS web apps that we know of; **partial** = can be approximated; **unverified** = depends on firmware or on a server and was not tested. None of this was tested on a TV.

- **Внешний плеер** (`vp_external`, `features/settings/VideoPlayerSettingsScreen.kt:870-878`): Android hands the stream to another app with an intent. Tizen/webOS web apps cannot open VLC/MX Player. Not possible. Nearest: hide the row on web, or keep it and show the three switches disabled with a note. Do not change the look without telling the Android team.
- **Автоматическая частота кадров / Пауза при переключении экрана / Подбирать разрешение** (`VideoPlayerSettingsScreen.kt:953-980`): Android display-mode switching (`player/FrameRateController.kt`). No web API for refresh-rate switching on Tizen 5 or webOS 4. Not possible. Nearest: hide the «Частота кадров» part (heading included) on web.
- **Буфер и задержка эфира, для плейлиста** (`VideoPlayerSettingsScreen.kt:1090-1120`; seconds in `engine/core/.../settings/LiveLatency.kt:26-31`): on Android this sets libVLC/ExoPlayer buffers. Partial: with hls.js / mpegts.js the nearest knobs are `liveSyncDuration`/`liveMaxLatencyDuration` (hls.js) and `liveBufferLatencyChasing`/`stashInitialSize` (mpegts.js). Native `<video>` or AVPlay on Tizen and the webOS media pipeline give little or no control. Keep the 4 options and the warning popup; map Low = 2 s, Stable = 15 s, Custom = 1–60 s where the engine allows it.
- **Прекращать попытки через** (`VideoPlayerSettingsScreen.kt:1151-1180`): pure timer logic. Possible in JS.
- **Буфер фильмов и архива** (`VideoPlayerSettingsScreen.kt:984-995`): partial: hls.js `maxBufferLength`; native players have no setting.
- **Мультиэкран / Максимум окон** (`VideoPlayerSettingsScreen.kt:1218-1239`): needs 2–4 videos decoding at the same time. Tizen AVPlay is a single instance. Several HTML5 `<video>` elements usually share one hardware decoder on Tizen 5 and webOS 4, so a second live tile may fail or fall back to software decode. Unverified. Nearest: keep the setting, let a tile that cannot start say why (as Android does).
- **Мини-плеер** (`features/settings/MiniPlayerSettingsDialog.kt:49-91`): the in-app mini player is the same video resized and moved. Possible with CSS (`position: fixed`, width %). Avoid `transform` scaling on the video element on webOS 4 (Chromium 53): some firmwares draw video on a hardware plane that ignores CSS transforms. Use real width/left/top. Unverified.
- **Часы поверх видео** (`VideoPlayerSettingsScreen.kt:1183-1191`, `2556-2614`): possible. On a hardware video plane the clock must be a normal DOM element above the video with a transparent page background.
- **Проверить обновления, Проверять при запуске, Обновлять автоматически, Проверять обновления (как часто), Когда устанавливать** (`features/shell/components/SettingsScreen.kt:798-839`, `features/update/UpdateDialog.kt`): Android downloads and installs an APK from GitHub Releases. A Tizen/webOS web app cannot install itself. Not possible. Nearest: on web keep only a version check that says «у вас последняя версия» / «есть новая версия, обновите через магазин или переустановите», or hide all five rows. Product decision.
- **Прокси** (`features/settings/NetworkSettingsScreen.kt:30-109`): Android routes all app traffic and playback through an HTTP proxy. A web app cannot set a proxy for `<video>` or `fetch`. Not possible. Nearest: hide the row, or rewrite stream URLs through a user-given HTTP relay (not the same feature).
- **DNS / DoH** (`features/settings/DnsSettingsScreen.kt:27-166`): media and XHR requests use the TV's system resolver. Not possible for playback. A DoH lookup via `fetch` could resolve names for API calls only, and HTTPS streams would then fail on certificate host checks. Nearest: hide the row.
- **Подробный журнал воспроизведения** (`VideoPlayerSettingsScreen.kt:1411-1418`): possible (keep a log ring buffer in memory or localStorage).
- **Резервные копии** (`features/settings/BackupScreen.kt`): "save to a folder on this TV" and "restore from a file on this TV" need a user-visible file system. Tizen web apps have `tizen.filesystem` (privilege `http://tizen.org/privilege/filesystem.write`, USB storage only on some models). webOS 4 web apps have no user file access. Partial on Tizen, not possible on webOS. "Send to / restore from another device" needs an HTTP server on the TV (see next item).
- **Синхронизация ТВ / Local sync** (`features/settings/LocalSyncScreen.kt`; server `engine/core/.../companion/CompanionHttpServer.kt:71-165`, discovery `engine/core/.../sync/local/LocalSyncDiscovery.kt` via Android `NsdManager`): the TV listens on a TCP port and announces itself by mDNS. A browser page cannot open a listening socket or do mDNS. Not possible in a plain web app. Nearest: webOS packaged apps may include a JS service (Node.js-based) that can listen on a port; Tizen web apps can bundle a service app on some versions only. Both unverified. Otherwise relay through the SkMate server (pairing code), like the Telegram remote does.
- **Пульт через Telegram** (`features/settings/TelegramRemoteScreen.kt`; pairing server `https://nutrokstore.xyz/skmate/tv`, `features/remote/TelegramRemote.kt:178`): HTTPS polling from the TV. Possible if that server sends CORS headers for the web app's origin (a packaged `file://` origin on Tizen/webOS). Unverified. «Свой бот» polls `https://api.telegram.org/bot…` (`features/remote/OwnTelegramBot.kt:356`) directly from the TV; Telegram's CORS behaviour for long-polling from a TV web app is unverified.
- **QR codes** (Telegram page, About link popups, Local sync, Backup remote): Android renders them natively (`core/companion/CompanionLink.renderQr`). Web needs a small QR library inlined into the app (no CDN at runtime on a TV). Pick one that runs on Chromium 53 (ES5 build) and draws to `<canvas>`.
- **Animated tick on the Telegram page** (`TelegramRemoteScreen.kt:238-260`: spring scale + path drawn over 450 ms): use an SVG path with `stroke-dasharray`/`stroke-dashoffset` CSS transition and a cubic-bezier overshoot for the disc; works on Chromium 53. Spring physics → approximate with `cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1)` over ~400 ms.
- **Marquee of long titles on focus** (e.g. «Подробный журнал воспроизведения» scrolls in screenshot 30-2; `ui/components/FocusMarquee.kt`): CSS `@keyframes` translateX on the focused row's title. Works on Chromium 53; keep it on the text only.
- **Units:** the Local sync hosting card is sized in **dp**, not mpx (`LocalSyncScreen.kt:459-501`, `774-775`): 1 dp = 1.8 px on the 1920 stage (250 dp = 450 px, QR 188 dp = 338 px). Easy to get wrong.
- **Sheet title vs list label differ** for Backup («Резервные копии» → «Резервная копия и восстановление»), TV sync («Синхронизация ТВ» → «Локальная синхронизация») and Telegram («Пульт Telegram» → «Пульт через Telegram»). Not a platform risk; a likely copy mistake for web developers.
